### [1] [Management of Behavioral Addictions via Artificial Intelligence based Digital Technologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23341)
*Süleyman Eken,Serdar Solak,Mustafa Hikmet Bilgehan Uçar,Bahadır Geniş,Ömer Kardaş,Hande Albayrak,İsmail Emre Ak,Nesibe Sağlam*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本书综述通过数字技术与人工智能（AI）手段预防、检测、治疗行为成瘾（问题性游戏、赌博、社交媒体使用、网络购物）的研究现状，旨在为研究者、临床医生与从业者提供统一、跨学科的视角，并指出可扩展、个性化、有效干预的路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为成瘾已成为全球性关注问题，带来显著心理、社会与经济后果；传统评估与治疗虽有临床基础，但在可扩展性、可及性与长期依从性（engagement）方面存在局限，亟需数据驱动的数字化解决方案。

Method: 采用综述（survey）方法，将临床基础与前沿计算方法相结合，系统梳理AI与数字技术在行为成瘾预防、检测、治疗及康复各环节的应用。

Result: 形成对AI驱动数字技术在行为成瘾管理中应用机会与局限的统一跨学科认识，并凝练出面向更可扩展、个性化、有效干预策略的发展路径。

Conclusion: 数字技术与AI为行为成瘾管理提供了高可扩展性与个性化的潜力，但其局限亦需正视；未来应推动临床与计算方法的深度融合，以实现更可及的干预体系。

Abstract: Behavioral addictions, such as problematic gaming, gambling, social media use, and online shopping, have emerged as a growing global concern with significant psychological, social, and economic consequences. Traditional assessment and treatment approaches, while clinically grounded, often struggle with scalability, accessibility, and long-term engagement. In recent years, advances in digital technologies and artificial intelligence (AI) have enabled the development of novel, data-driven solutions for the prevention, detection, treatment, and recovery of behavioral addictions. This book presents a comprehensive survey of the management of behavioral addictions through digital technologies, bridging clinical foundations with state-of-the-art computational approaches. This book aims to provide researchers, clinicians, and practitioners with a unified, interdisciplinary perspective on the opportunities and limitations of AI-based digital technologies in behavioral addiction management,and to highlight pathways toward more scalable, personalized, and effective intervention strategies.

</details>


### [2] [RummiAlgebra: A Polynomial Representation Framework for Game-Based Algebra Learning with Jokers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23443)
*Luis Adrián Lizama-Pérez*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出 RummiAlgebra——一个将 Rummikub 式牌块重排任务映射到多项式加法、公因子提取、有限等比求和与上下文相关代换的设计框架及浏览器原型，并给出可靠性与完备性证明、示例与对照实验校验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 入门代数中"符号表达式—可操作对象"的联结是长期难点：抽象符号缺乏可动手操作的具体载体。作者希望借助已有物理规则的牌块游戏，构造形式化、可验证的教学对应关系，使学生在可操作的排列任务中体验多项式运算与代换。

Method: 将编号彩色牌编码为单项式 c x^v，实体万能牌表示为符号 J_i（其代数角色仅在特定候选排列中固定）；定义顺子（run）与刻子（group）的容许多项式形式及"牌张数守恒"不变量；给出可靠性与完备性证明，说明仅凭多项式等式不足，须物理牌张清点与代数模板共同刻画合法终局；给出分解、竞争性因式分解、单/双万能牌代换、优化扩展四例；用独立穷举参考枚举器校验原型的六道小挑战与规则—表达式检查器的最优分。

Result: 形式化证明成立：物理牌张核算＋代数 run/group 模板可刻画课堂规则下的合法终局，而单纯多项式相等不充分。原型六个挑战及检查器的最优分与独立穷举参考枚举器一致。作者明确指出这些校验仅证明示例层面的一致性，不证明学习效果，也不主张底层代数运算的新颖性或更快的求解算法。

Conclusion: 贡献在于一个形式化规定的教学对应关系与交互式、面向证明的活动设计；后续给出课堂实施序列、评估构念与受控实验方案，以检验教学有效性。

Abstract: Connecting symbolic expressions to manipulable objects is a recurring challenge in introductory algebra. This paper presents RummiAlgebra, a design-oriented framework and browser-based prototype that maps a Rummikub-style tile-rearrangement task to polynomial addition, common-factor extraction, finite geometric sums, and context-dependent substitution. A numbered colored tile is encoded as a monomial c x^v, while a physical joker is represented by a distinct symbol J_i whose algebraic role is fixed only for a particular candidate arrangement. We define admissible polynomial forms for runs and groups and establish an inventory-conservation invariant. A soundness-and-completeness proof shows that physical-tile accounting together with the algebraic run/group templates characterizes valid final arrangements under the stated classroom rules; polynomial equality alone is insufficient. Four detailed examples illustrate decomposition, competing factorizations, one- and two-joker substitutions, and optimization as an optional extension. A targeted review distinguishes the approach from established Rummikub solvers, Rummikub-inspired arithmetic-sequence activities, algebra-tile manipulatives, and polynomial games. The prototype implements six small challenges and a separate rule-and-expression checker; their optimal scores were checked against an independent exhaustive reference enumerator. These checks establish agreement for the examples but do not establish learning effectiveness or novelty of the underlying algebraic operations. We close with a classroom sequence, assessment constructs, and a controlled evaluation protocol. The claimed contribution is a formally specified pedagogical correspondence and an interactive proof-oriented activity, not a new asymptotically faster Rummikub solver.

</details>


### [3] [When Do LLMs Model the Right Target? Problem Formulation in High-Stakes Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23638)
*Jennifer Zhang,Amanda Coston*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 论文考察LLM在高风险决策场景中构建预测问题时能否正确处理"决策依赖性"（历史决策影响观测结果所导致的效度问题），发现无提示引导时LLM无法可靠应对，显式方法学指导可使目标对齐实现率达99–100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI已广泛用于辅助数据科学任务，但其能否正确"形式化"分析问题（而非仅写代码）尚不清楚；在高风险决策建模中，目标定义与方法假设的正确性直接决定模型效度，其中"决策依赖性"是常被忽视的关键效度威胁。

Method: 在儿童福利、医疗、就业三个场景中，使用OpenAI与Anthropic共11个模型、7种提示条件，评估LLM生成代码是否采用了在给定假设下估计目标结果所适宜的程序，以及其解释是否识别出决策依赖性。

Result: 无提示引导时LLM不能可靠处理决策依赖性；当提示明确指定目标、所需假设与估计程序时，目标对齐实现率达99–100%；对前沿模型而言，仅用简单提示描述"决策如何影响结果"即足以引出目标对齐实现；部分回答在文字上承认决策依赖性但代码中并未处理。

Conclusion: 分析者不应在缺乏明确方法学指导与细致审阅的情况下依赖LLM形式化预测问题；作者提出以效度为中心的LLM辅助预测建模指南（提示设计、目标与估计程序的验证、关键假设识别）；效度确立是高风险场景部署模型的必要早期步骤，但部署还需权衡伦理、制度与实践层面因素。

Abstract: Generative AI is increasingly used to assist with data science tasks, yet fundamental questions remain about its ability to correctly formulate analytical problems. We investigate how well large language models (LLMs) can formulate prediction problems when developing models for high-stakes decision making. We focus on one particular challenge to validity: decision dependency, which arises when historical decisions affect observed outcomes. We evaluate this aspect of problem formulation in LLM-generated code and explanations across child-welfare, healthcare, and employment settings, using 11 models from OpenAI and Anthropic and seven prompt conditions. We assess whether the LLM-generated code implements a procedure appropriate for estimating the intended outcome under the study's assumptions and whether the explanation acknowledges decision dependency. We find that LLMs do not reliably address decision dependency without guidance in the prompt. When prompts specify the target, the assumption needed to estimate it, and the estimation procedure, target-aligned implementation reaches 99-100%. Among frontier models, a simple prompt description of how decisions affect outcomes appears sufficient to elicit target-aligned implementation. Some responses also acknowledge decision dependency without addressing it in their code. Our findings suggest that analysts should not rely on LLMs to correctly formulate prediction problems without explicit methodological guidance and careful review. We propose validity-centered guidelines for LLM-assisted predictive modeling, covering prompt formulation, validation of the target and estimation procedure, and identification of key assumptions. Establishing validity is a necessary early step in evaluating whether to deploy a predictive model in a high-stakes setting, but deployment requires weighing broader ethical, institutional, and practical considerations.

</details>


### [4] [Same Model, Different People: Numerical Execution Contracts and the Reproducibility of Fairness Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23657)
*Erzheng Zhang,Agnes Chu*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究将模型、记录、阈值与审计代码固定，仅改变数值执行契约（浮点求和/乘积顺序与降精度配置），在CPU/GPU多平台上对表格模型与大语言模型做公平性审计，发现近阈值决策会随数值契约变化，个别诊断性裁定（如vLLM FP8）发生翻转，并提出“审计稳定性协议”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公平性审计通常把已训练模型视为固定的决策函数，但评估该函数的数值计算（乘积与求和的执行方式）本身也是该函数的一部分。作者希望区分“算术本身的因果效应”与“部署配置的效应”，检验审计结论对数值执行契约的敏感性。

Method: 采用双层设计：Tier 1 仅改变乘积与求和的数值计算契约，序列化权重逐字节一致，用于算术的因果检验；Tier 2 运行降精度部署配置（BF16 计算、FP8 与 8-bit 权重），其效应归属于配置。审计三个表格模型族与两个开放权重语言模型（4B、24B），覆盖四个公开任务（含医院再入院），在 NVIDIA、AMD、Apple 的 CPU 与 GPU 上运行；以预设的非加权检验裁定结果，并记录各裁定的区间与采样不确定性。

Result: Tier 1 在阈值附近改变了部分决策，且哪些决策被改变取决于 GPU 及软件栈，但未改变任何预设非加权检验的裁定。Tier 2 改变更多决策及部分诊断性裁定，但无四分之三规则（four-fifths）裁定翻转。用 vLLM FP8 服务 24B 模型时选中比例为 54% 而非 45%，并在一项按性别的就业状态审计中通过了其严格 FP32 参考未通过的检验：人口统计 parity 与 equalized-odds 差距由 0.0567、0.0515 降至 0.0417、0.0394（诊断容差 0.05）；在参考的选中计数下 FP8 反而未通过人口统计 parity，且其 equalized-odds 变化的配对区间无一排除零。此外，在冻结阈值下，被配置改变的所有通过/失败裁定，其采样区间均包含判据。

Conclusion: 审计结论依赖于数值执行配置，FP8 并未被证明更公平；裁定翻转多落在采样不确定性之内。作者提出审计稳定性协议：审计者在评分前声明契约、分组、权重与检验，并记录每个裁定在各配置下的取值范围，将其与采样不确定性分开报告。

Abstract: Fairness audits treat a trained model as a fixed decision function, but the arithmetic that evaluates it is part of that function. This study holds the model, records, threshold and audit code fixed and varies the numerical execution contract in two tiers. Tier 1 changes only how products and sums are computed, with the serialized weights byte-identical, and is the causal test of arithmetic. Tier 2 runs reduced-precision deployment configurations (BF16 compute, FP8 and 8-bit weights), which can also round weights and change engines and kernels, so its effects belong to the configuration. It audits three tabular model families and two open-weight language models (4B and 24B parameters) on four public tasks, including hospital readmission, on CPUs and GPUs from NVIDIA, AMD and Apple. Tier 1 changed decisions near the threshold, and which ones depended on the GPU and its software stack, but no verdict under the prespecified, unweighted tests. Tier 2 configurations changed more decisions and some diagnostic verdicts, but no four-fifths verdict. Serving the 24B model with vLLM FP8 selected 54% of people rather than 45% and passed an employment-status audit by sex that its strict FP32 reference failed: its demographic-parity and equalized-odds gaps fell from 0.0567 and 0.0515 to 0.0417 and 0.0394, against a diagnostic tolerance of 0.05. At the reference's selection count FP8 failed demographic parity, and no reading gave its equalized-odds change a paired interval excluding zero. The declared audit depends on the configuration; FP8 is not shown to be fairer. At the frozen thresholds, every pass-fail verdict a configuration changed had a sampling interval containing its criterion. It proposes an audit stability protocol in which the auditor declares contracts, groups, weights and tests before scoring and records each verdict's range over them apart from its sampling uncertainty.

</details>


### [5] [Decent Work Standards and Non-Conventional Work: Measurement Gaps and Uneven Capability Formation in Kenya's Online Platform Labour Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23757)
*Farhan Latif*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该文以肯尼亚经验丰富的线上自由职业者为对象，用收敛式混合方法考察劳动者层面的"体面工作"评估如何与生计策略及结果交织，指出横截面式体面工作测量无法揭示条件的持久性、生产机制与生计转化能力，主张以"能力—生计"视角补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在常规劳动力市场较弱的背景下，数字平台上的线上自由职业既是脆弱生计又是被追捧的谋生路径；既有体面工作（decent work）研究多停留在劳动者层面的静态指标测量，难以说明这些条件能否转化为真正的生计安全与能动性，作者因此追问测量条件背后的持久性、供给主体与可转化性。

Method: 收敛式混合方法（convergent mixed-methods）：52名劳动者的问卷指标 + 20名参与者的访谈，两类数据并行收集并相互印证；分析中区分条件的三重维度——是否持久（durability）、由制度提供还是靠劳动者无偿劳动自行生产（production）、能否转化为生计安全与能动性（convertibility）。

Result: 指标层面：工作—生活平衡、包容性、安全性与技能发展获得较积极评价，而收入稳定性、社会保障（保护）与话语权（voice）表现薄弱。访谈层面：测得条件在持久性、制度供给vs.无偿劳动自产、以及向生计安全与能动性的转化上存在显著差异，即同样的"良好"评分对不同劳动者的实际意义不同。

Conclusion: 体面工作提供必要的规范性基线，但仅凭横截面的劳动者层面操作化无法揭示所测条件的持久性、生产机制与生计后果；引入"能力取向的生计视角"可解释谁能把平台参与转化为安全、能动性与未来机会，从而对体面工作评估形成互补。

Abstract: Online freelancing through digital platforms is a precarious but sought-after livelihood pathway in contexts with weaker conventional labour markets. This article examines how worker-level decent-work assessments intersect with livelihood strategies and outcomes among experienced online freelancers in Kenya. A convergent mixed-methods study combines survey indicators from 52 workers with interviews with 20 participants. Findings show relatively favourable assessments of work-life balance, inclusion, safety and skill development, alongside weak income consistency, protection and voice. Interviews reveal that measured conditions differ in durability, whether they are institutionally provided or produced through workers' unpaid effort, and their convertibility into livelihood security and agency. The article argues that decent work provides a necessary normative baseline, but cross-sectional worker-level operationalisations alone cannot reveal the durability, production or livelihood consequences of measured conditions. A capability-informed livelihood perspective complements decent-work assessment by explaining who can convert platform participation into security, agency and future opportunity.

</details>


### [6] [AI Surge Accelerates the Transition to Liquid Cooling: Rethinking Energy and Water Use in Data Center Cooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23903)
*Yingbo Zhang,Eric Masanet,Shengwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究重新评估了AI数据中心向液冷转型对全球能源与水需求的整体影响，量化了液冷的节能与节水潜力及设计目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI工作负载功率密度快速上升，推动散热方式从风冷向液冷转变，但这一转型对全球能源与水资源需求的影响尚缺乏系统评估。

Method: 构建全球尺度的建模分析，对比液冷与风冷的冷却能耗；计算最佳实践PUE与WUE随温度条件的变化，并识别蒸发式与干式系统的自然冷却机架进液温度阈值。

Result: 液冷相对风冷可降低冷却能耗25–56%，对GW级AI数据中心节能显著；最佳实践PUE在现有系统为1.07–1.08、新兴设计为1.03–1.04，WUE为0.38–2.63 L/kWh（依温度条件）；全球自由冷却进液温度阈值为蒸发式33 °C、干式49 °C，表明多地可实现100%自然冷却；最低PUE不一定对应最低总能耗，因冷却液温度升高会增加服务器功耗。

Conclusion: 液冷具有巨大的可持续性潜力，研究为数据中心设计与政策提供了可操作的目标，并提示需以总能耗而非仅PUE为优化指标。

Abstract: The power density of AI workloads is rapidly driving shifts to liquid cooling. This study rethinks and assesses the global impact on energy and water demand of this cooling transformation. Results suggest that liquid cooling can reduce cooling energy use by 25-56% relative to air cooling, offering substantial energy savings for GW-scale AI data centers. Best-practice PUE values could be as low as 1.07-1.08 for today's systems and 1.03-1.04 for emerging designs, and WUE values range from 0.38 to 2.63 L/kWh, depending on temperature conditions. We identify global free-cooling rack inlet temperature thresholds of 33 $^\circ$C for evaporative systems and 49 $^\circ$C for dry systems, demonstrating the practicality of 100% free cooling in many locations. The model finds that minimum PUEs do not necessarily coincide with minimum total energy use, because warmer coolant raises server power. Results quantify the large sustainability potential of liquid cooling and provide actionable targets for data center design and policies.

</details>


### [7] [Human-LLM Cognitive Extension: A Framework for Studying Coupled Cognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24149)
*Ernesto Biempica,Emiliano Bruner,Juan F Poyatos*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"人类-大模型认知延展"(HLCE)方法论框架，主张按四个不同的认知系统（独立人类、人工智能体、人机联合构型、分工后的人类）分别匹配实验设计、测量与因果推断，并将耦合架构本身视为实验对象，以厘清人机交互中看似矛盾的研究结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于大语言模型对认知影响的研究结论分歧明显：既有"辅助下表现提升"，也有"迁移有限"与"无辅助学习受损"。作者认为这种不一致部分源于研究者在未明确分析单元的情况下评测了不同的认知系统，因而需要一个能区分系统层级的方法论框架。

Method: 基于延展认知与分布式认知理论，构建HLCE框架：明确界定四个分析单元——独立行动的人类、人工智能体、人机联合构型、以及分工/卸载之后的人类；将耦合架构（如信息传递方式）纳入实验变量；并在概念上区分四个层级的主张：表现增强、系统层面的互补性、交互后的人类变化、以及更强的构成性认知整合主张。以一个合成的诊断性实验为例，比较两种信息沟通方式不同的连接构型。

Result: 示例显示：即便最终任务结果保持一致，不同的耦合方式仍会改变联合问题解决的组织过程（过程层面的差异先于结果层面的差异显现），说明仅以结果指标评估人机协作会掩盖系统结构上的差异。

Conclusion: HLCE为跨不同人机认知交互形式设计受控实验、解释既有证据提供了方法论基础；它不预设人机构型即为延展心智，而是将"是否构成认知延展"作为需要经验检验的问题，从而提升结论的可比性与因果推断的严谨性。

Abstract: Recent research on the use of Large Language Models (LLMs) reports divergent cognitive outcomes, including improved assisted performance, limited transfer, and compromised unaided learning. Here, we argue that part of this seeming inconsistency arises because studies often assess different cognitive systems without making the unit of analysis explicit. Building on work in extended and distributed cognition, we propose the Human-LLM Cognitive Extension (HLCE) as a methodological framework for matching experimental designs, measures, and causal inferences to four distinct systems: the human acting independently, the artificial agent, the joined human-LLM configuration, and the human after division. The framework also treats the coupling architecture as part of the experimental object. HLCE separates performance augmentation, system-level complementarity, post-interaction human change, and stronger claims of constitutive cognitive integration. This separation can improve the interpretation of existing evidence and guide the design of human-LLM interactions without assuming in advance that these configurations constitute extended minds. We illustrate the application of HLCE with a synthetic diagnostic experiment comparing two connecting configurations that differ in how supporting information is communicated. The example highlights how distinct couplings can shape the organization of joint problem-solving, even when the final outcome remains unchanged. Overall, HLCE provides a methodological basis for designing controlled experiments and interpreting evidence across distinct forms of human-LLM cognitive interaction.

</details>


### [8] [Hi Gemini, Who Should I Vote For? The Benefits (and Problems) of Using LLMs As Voting Advice Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24684)
*William John Atkinson,Paul Saproza*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文检验 Gemini 3 Flash 能否充当投票建议应用（VAA），基于6022名德国受访者的政策偏好给出政党推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究关注 LLM 提供政治信息，但尚无研究评估现成 LLM 作为投票建议应用（VAA）的表现，作者填补这一空白。

Method: 以6022名德国调查受访者为样本，仅依据其政策偏好与议题优先级，让 Gemini 3 Flash 推荐应投票的政党，并与标准显著性加权空间邻近模型比较契合度及推荐分布。

Result: 对多数受访者而言，遵循 LLM 建议可提升选民—政党政策契合度，且对政治兴趣低、正规教育程度低的选民补救效应显著更强；但推荐存在偏差：AfD 被推荐频率约为标准显著性加权邻近模型的1.5倍，Die Linke 与 BSW 几乎从未被推荐。

Conclusion: LLM 可近似 VAA 行为，但其建议仍存在难以追溯的偏差，用户需保持警惕。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can provide voters information about political issues, party positions, and even advise on which party to vote for. However, no previous study explores how well off-the-shelf LLMs function as voting advice applications (VAAs). In this article, we address this gap by examining how Gemini 3 Flash advises a sample of 6,022 German survey respondents on which party to vote for, based on their policy preferences and priorities alone. In brief, we find that for most respondents, following the LLM's advice would benefit voter-party policy alignment (i.e. congruence), with a significantly stronger remedial effect among voters with low levels of political interest and formal education. However, our analysis also reveals concerning biases in the LLM's recommendations. For instance, the LLM suggested voting for the AfD at approximately 1.5 times the frequency of a standard salience-weighted proximity model. In addition, the LLM hardly ever advised voting for either Die Linke or the BSW. We conclude that while LLMs can approximate the behaviour of VAAs, users should be aware that the LLM's recommendations are still biased in ways that are extremely difficult to trace.

</details>


### [9] [Mapping the AI Supply Chain: Dependencies, Vulnerabilities, and Market Concentration in the AI Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24927)
*Sarah H. Cen,Lindsey A. Gailmard,Joohan Kim,Rishi Bommasani,Daniel E. Ho,Percy Liang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该论文系统绘制AI供应链中的组织关系网络，发布多层大规模数据集（含日期、描述、类型、标签等标注），并通过中心性、网络集中度、社区与瓶颈分析揭示AI生态的政治经济结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统由众多组织共同贡献模型、数据、算力、资本与人才，但AI栈各层内部结构虽较清晰，跨层的行为主体与组件关系却记录稀疏。作者关注集中度、权力、瓶颈、级联失效、责任碎片化与风险等问题，因而需要系统刻画组织形式上的AI供应链。

Method: 提出一套AI供应链映射流程（pipeline），据此构建多层级、大规模的行为主体与关系数据集，对每条记录标注日期、描述、类型、标签等元信息；数据公开于Hugging Face并提供交互式可视化平台。基于该数据开展示例性分析：识别最核心主体、分析网络集中度、检测社区与瓶颈。

Result: 产出覆盖模型、数据、芯片、基础设施、资本乃至人才流动的多层组织关系数据集，可系统度量AI供应链属性；示例分析给出核心主体、集中度、社区结构与瓶颈节点等发现，为AI开发与集中度的政治经济学研究提供实证基础。作者亦指出数据受公开文件限制（私人交易未披露）与检索设计影响，存在必然的不完整性。

Conclusion: 该工作朝系统映射与度量AI供应链及其属性迈出一步，其方法、数据与发现可作为研究AI生态的公共资源，支持对产业关系的批判性考察。

Abstract: AI systems are built across many organizations that contribute models, data, compute, capital, talent, and other assets. While the layers of the AI stack are relatively well understood, the relationships between actors and components across layers remains sparsely documented. In this work, we focus on the organizations that make up the AI supply chain: the complex network of actors that contribute to the development and deployment of AI. We motivate the examination of the organizational AI supply chain---highlighting concerns about concentration, power, chokepoints, cascading failures, fragmented accountability, and risk---then describe our pipeline for mapping the AI supply chain. Our pipeline yields our main contribution: a multilayered, large-scale dataset of actors and relationships in the AI ecosystem, annotated with dates, descriptions, types, tags, and more. This work takes a step toward systematically mapping and measuring the AI supply chain and its properties, cataloging how models, data, chips, infrastructure, capital, and even talent flow between organizations. This data enable critical examinations of industry relationships and yield insights into the political economy of AI development and concentration. We provide several illustrative analyses, including studying the most central actors, analyzing network concentration, and identifying communities and chokepoints. While our dataset is fairly comprehensive, it is also necessarily incomplete: public documents omit private deals, and coverage is shaped by search design. Together, the approach, data, and findings aim to serve as a resource for studying the AI ecosystem. The dataset is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/sarahcen/ai-supply-chain and can be explored interactively at https://aisupplychains.org/.

</details>
