### [10] [PGPO: Prototype-Guided Progressive Obfuscation for Privacy-Preserving LLM-Enhanced Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23236)
*Shengxiang Lin,Jiajie Su,Pengyang Zhou,Xiang Chen,Xiaolin Zheng,Feng Tian,Chaochao Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出以"关系保真"为核心目标的原型引导渐进式混淆（PGPO），通过GRPO优化对高影响力原型词施加拓扑感知混淆并沿语义图传播，在保护物品文本隐私的同时近乎无损地保持推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM增强推荐系统将物品侧信息编码为语义嵌入，但这些嵌入可能经反演攻击泄露敏感文本属性。现有输入侧防御依赖可信采集或局部token扰动，往往破坏推荐所需的物品间语义拓扑。

Method: 以关系保真为关键目标（隐私需语义偏移、效用需保持相对物品关系）。据此提出PGPO：为高影响力原型词学习拓扑感知的混淆锚点，并沿静态语义图传播；用GRPO优化，奖励由个体差异性、结构一致性与语义约束三部分组成。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上实现强反演防御，同时推荐性能接近理论上界。

Conclusion: 关系保真可作为兼顾隐私与效用的统一优化目标，原型引导+图传播+GRPO的组合能在不破坏物品间语义拓扑的前提下有效抵御嵌入反演，代码已开源。

Abstract: LLM-enhanced recommender systems encode item side information into semantic embeddings, but these embeddings may leak sensitive textual attributes through inversion attacks. Existing input-side defenses rely on trusted collection or local token perturbation, often disrupting the inter-item semantic topology needed for recommendation. To address it, we introduce relational fidelity as the key objective, requiring semantic shift for privacy while preserving relative item relations for utility. Based on this principle, we propose prototype-guided progressive obfuscation (PGPO), which learns topology-aware obfuscation anchors for high-influence prototype words and propagates them through a static semantic graph. PGPO is optimized with GRPO using individual dissimilarity, structural consistency, and semantic constraint rewards. Experiments on two benchmarks show strong inversion defense with near-upper-bound recommendation performance. The code is available at https://github.com/Shengxiang-Lin/PGPO.

</details>


### [11] [From Docket Minutes to Case Trajectories: Building Analysis-Ready Criminal Court Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23537)
*Erfan Hosseini Sereshgi,Kory Rosen,Jaya Dhariwal,Nicholas Mattei,Aron Culotta*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍一个面向新奥尔良刑事法院庭审数据的采集、协调与可视化仪表盘系统，服务专家与非专业公众，支持人口统计、时间趋势与案件事件分析，并通过社区共创与形成性研究验证可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刑事法院记录常碎片化、含噪声且彼此孤立，使法律专家与社区利益相关者难以获取和分析法院数据，从而难以识别趋势并开展公共监督。

Method: 构建集数据摄取、数据协调（harmonization）与仪表盘可视化于一体的系统：采集并协调异构数据、生成案件轨迹（case trajectories），并基于社区反馈设计可视化技术，用于人口统计、时间趋势与特定案件事件分析。

Result: 通过对新奥尔良近期短期趋势与政策影响的探索性分析展示系统能力，说明其可辅助调查异常、政策变化与程序性违规；小型形成性研究反馈显示，参与共创与使用系统的社区利益相关者认为其有助于改善本地法院数据的获取与解读。

Conclusion: 该数据协调与可视化系统可提升刑事法院数据的可及性与解释性，支持专家与公众的趋势分析与公共监督；但证据来自小规模形成性研究，结论具有初步性。

Abstract: Criminal court records are often fragmented, noisy, and siloed, making it challenging for both legal experts and community stakeholders to access and analyze court data to identify trends and provide public oversight. In this paper, we introduce a system for data ingestion, harmonization, and dashboard visualization designed for New Orleans criminal court proceedings data, aimed at both expert and lay audiences. Our system collects and harmonizes disparate data, generates case trajectories, and provides intuitive visualization techniques based on community feedback to analyze demographics, temporal trends, and specific case events. We demonstrate the system through an exploratory analysis of recent short-term trends and policy impacts within New Orleans, illustrating how it can support the investigation of anomalies, policy changes, and procedural irregularities. We also present feedback from a small formative study of community stakeholders who have helped co-design and use the system, suggesting that it may improve access to and interpretation of local court data.

</details>


### [12] [CONCORD-Rec: Catalog-Conditioned Modality Utility for Semantic-ID Diffusion Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23710)
*Haopeng Jin,Bingkang Shi,Tao Yu,Yifei Ma,Hao Liu,Hongzhu Yi,Jinwen Luo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 CONCORD-Rec，一种由反事实目录分区效用监督的可靠性条件离散扩散推荐模型，用可用性掩码的 token 级门控在行为降噪器周围分配有界模态残差，提升缺失/退化/冲突模态下的推荐鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态生成式推荐中的离散语义标识（SID）需要区分“仅改变 token 预测”的历史模态与“真正降低目录级歧义”的模态。当音视频、视觉或文本流缺失、退化或语义冲突时，统一融合与无约束 token 门控会把误导性内容在迭代去噪中逐步传播。

Method: 在固定 tokenizer 与部分掩码 SID 状态下，对精确可行目录支撑上的 out-of-fold 教师联盟进行评估，并在每个未解析位置按目标码分区聚合物品后验；以反事实损失差值（而非直接蒸馏原始 token 概率）监督可用性掩码的 token 级门控，在行为降噪器周围分配有界模态残差，并保留精确行为回退槽位；同时给出门控扰动下固定支撑的排序稳定性理论界，并进行位置级与码条件分配分析。

Result: 在 20 行协议-数据集兼容设定上 NDCG 提升 4%；匹配 30% 冲突条件下 NDCG 提升 14.63%；完整 vs 均匀 T=16 扫描中质量提升 2.00–3.58%，p95 延迟最多降低 86.89%。

Conclusion: 以目录分区效用差作为监督信号的门控机制能有效抑制不可靠模态的负迁移，在保持行为回退安全性的同时提升多模态生成式推荐的准确性、鲁棒性与推理效率。

Abstract: Multimodal generative recommendation with discrete semantic identifiers (SIDs) requires distinguishing historical modalities that merely alter token predictions from those that genuinely reduce catalog-level ambiguity. When audiovisual, visual, or textual streams are missing, degraded, or semantically conflicting, uniform fusion and unconstrained token gates can propagate misleading content across iterative denoising steps. We propose CONCORD-Rec, a reliability-conditioned discrete diffusion recommender supervised by counterfactual catalog-partition utility. Given a fixed tokenizer and a partially masked SID state, CONCORD-Rec evaluates out-of-fold teacher coalitions over the exact feasible catalog support and aggregates item posteriors across the target code partition at each unresolved position. Rather than distilling raw token probabilities directly, the resulting counterfactual loss differences supervise an availability-masked tokenwise gate that allocates bounded modality residuals around a behavioral denoiser while retaining an exact behavioral fallback slot. We establish theoretical fixed-support ranking stability bounds under gate perturbations and evaluate position-level and code-conditional allocation across audiovisual and image-text recommendation benchmarks under controlled missingness, signal degradation, and semantic conflict. Across 20 source-compatible protocol-dataset rows, CONCORD-Rec improves NDCG by 4%; under matched 30% conflict it improves NDCG by 14.63%. The full-vs-uniform-T=16 sweep improves quality by 2.00-3.58% while reducing p95 latency by up to 86.89%.

</details>


### [13] [MARS Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24006)
*Tong Liu,Chaoqun Hou,Longxiang Xiong,Junhong Lou,Jiaqi Zheng,Qixin Li,Zixin Zhao,Yong Duan,Xiannian Hu,Fengze Jiang,Yu Zhu,Cheng Guo,Xinyuan Qi,Zi Zhuang,Ping Liu,Lv Shao,Zongwei Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出 MARS 多阶段智能体推荐系统，用近线小模型（Qwen3-0.6B）为每个用户生成覆盖渠道激活、配额、模型选择、分数融合与重排设置的七维流水线配置，通过三阶段策略训练与沙箱重放实现全链路个性化编排，并在淘宝五大场景的大规模A/B中取得显著收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统采用 retrieval→pre-ranking→ranking→re-ranking 的多级级联，但各阶段的渠道激活、配额、模型选择、分数融合等流水线级决策仍依赖人工调参，且在同一场景内对所有用户同质化（user-agnostic），限制了端到端效果与效率优化。

Method: 将个性化从单阶段预测扩展到整条流水线编排：近线智能体基于微调 Qwen3-0.6B，在受限动作空间内为每用户生成七维配置（流水线模式、召回配额、模型选择、融合权重、重排器设置）；配置经校验、缓存后异步消费，级联服务不阻塞等待LLM推理，并以 schema 校验、回退与 kill switch 保障安全。策略分三阶段训练：置信度感知 SFT 蒸馏教师模型的合法格式生成；FAPO 通过 ActionMask 与后验加权学习动作偏好；GRPO 借助 Sandbox replay 与冻结奖励模型精炼策略，避免探索动作进入线上流量。

Result: 淘宝五场景、日活超千万用户的两周 A/B 实验显示全部核心指标正向：最大场景 V2P +5.22%、GMV +7.34%，两个点击面 CTR 均提升；约 20% 请求采用轻量 Minimal 模式，部署后平均服务时延较线上降低 12 ms。

Conclusion: MARS 验证了以 LLM 智能体进行流水线级、用户粒度编排的可行性与收益，在保证服务安全与低时延的前提下同时提升效果指标并降低时延，为多级级联推荐的系统级个性化提供了可落地范式。

Abstract: Modern industrial recommender systems use multi-stage cascades--retrieval, pre-ranking, ranking, and re-ranking--to narrow millions of candidates to a personalized list. While each stage deploys sophisticated models, pipeline-level decisions on channel activation, quotas, model selection, and score fusion remain engineer-tuned and user-agnostic within each scenario.
  This report presents MARS (Multi-stage Agentic Recommendation System), extending personalization from individual-stage predictions to full pipeline orchestration. A near-line agent built on fine-tuned Qwen3-0.6B generates per-user configurations--a seven-dimensional action covering pipeline mode, retrieval quotas, model selection, fusion weights, and reranker settings--from a constrained action space. Configurations are validated, cached, and consumed asynchronously; the cascade never waits for LLM inference. Schema checks, fallbacks, and a kill switch ensure serving safety.
  The policy is trained in three stages: Confidence-aware SFT distills schema-valid generation from a teacher model; Format-Anchored Preference Optimization (FAPO) learns action preferences via ActionMask with posterior weighting; Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) refines the policy through Sandbox replay with a frozen reward model, without exposing exploratory actions to live traffic.
  Two-week A/B tests across five Taobao scenarios with over ten million daily users show positive gains on all key metrics. The largest reach +5.22% V2P and +7.34% GMV, with positive CTR lifts on both click surfaces. About 20% of requests use a lightweight Minimal mode, and deployment shows 12 ms lower mean serving latency versus production.

</details>


### [14] [X-split Argus: Learning Reusable History Tokens for Efficient CTR Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24283)
*Alexander Ploshkin,Nikita Stepanov,Alexey Pismenny,Viacheslav Ovchinnikov,Nikolay Savushkin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: X-split ARGUS 是一个历史压缩器与在线排序器端到端训练的广告排序框架：压缩器将长行为序列离线编码为少量可复用 token，在线排序器通过交叉注意力对候选广告打分；离线 NE 相对降低 0.81%，线上推理成本仅增 15%，A/B 测试中广告主转化价值指标提升 4.65%、点击提升 2.96%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CTR 预测需同时刻画用户长期稳定偏好与近期意图，但对每次排序请求都处理超长行为历史在生产算力与延迟约束下代价过高，因此需要一种可离线预计算、在线复用且算力可调的历史建模方案。

Method: 提出 X-split ARGUS：压缩器将历史交互编码为紧凑 token 集合，离线计算并对所有候选复用；在线排序器通过交叉注意力结合这些 token 与近期事件表征进行打分。训练端到端，联合使用点击反馈的自回归损失与候选级点击 logit 蒸馏——教师模型以未压缩历史对每个候选打分。压缩 token 数与近期历史长度可按服务算力/延迟预算调节。

Result: 离线评估中归一化熵（NE）相对基线降低 0.81%；在大规模实时竞价系统部署后，在线推理成本相对生产基线仅增加 15%；线上 A/B 测试相对基线在反映广告主转化货币价值的业务指标上提升 4.65%，点击提升 2.96%。

Conclusion: 该工作说明"离线压缩 + 在线交叉注意力 + 教师蒸馏"的端到端范式可在可控成本增量下有效利用长行为历史，取得离线与线上一致的显著收益，并具备按算力预算弹性伸缩的工程友好性。

Abstract: Accurate click-through rate (CTR) prediction depends on user representations that capture both persistent preferences and recent intent, yet processing extensive behavioral histories for every ranking request is costly under production compute and latency constraints. We present X-split ARGUS, a framework for ad ranking whose history compressor and online ranker are trained end to end. The compressor encodes past interactions into a compact set of tokens that are computed offline and reused across candidates, while the online ranker scores ads through cross-attention to these tokens and representations of recent events. Training combines an autoregressive loss for click feedback with distillation of candidate-specific click logits from a larger teacher that scores each candidate using the uncompressed history. The number of compressed history tokens and the length of recent history can be adjusted to fit serving compute and latency budgets. Offline evaluation shows a 0.81% relative reduction in normalized entropy (NE) compared with the production baseline. Deployment in a large-scale real-time bidding system increases online inference cost by 15% relative to the production baseline. An online A/B test against this baseline yields relative gains of 4.65% in a business metric reflecting the monetary value of conversions generated for advertisers and 2.96% in clicks.

</details>


### [15] [Can Programs Replace LLM Judges? Executable Program Judges for Scalable and Efficient Relevance Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24849)
*Eugene Agichtein,Charles L. A. Clarke,Kaustubh Dhole,William Stern*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 ExecJudges：用前沿 LLM 将评估准则转写为可执行程序（仅依赖词法匹配、解析器等标准 NLP 库与池统计量）作为逐主题相关性判定器，替代逐文档的 LLM 标注调用；在七个 TREC 集合上与 NIST 人工标注、LLM 判定器及逐主题分类器对比，取得相当的一致性且成本极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 作为评判者（LLM judge）已成大规模相关性标注的常用手段，但存在三方面问题：逐文档调用前沿模型成本高昂；判定结果难以解释；可复现性差。

Method: 提出 ExecJudges：由前沿 LLM 将评估指南转化为可执行程序，程序只依赖词法匹配、解析器等标准 NLP 库以及结果池统计信息；变体 ExecJudges-L 额外利用少量人工标注示例。在 7 个 TREC 集合上，将生成标签与 NIST 训练有素的评估员、前沿 LLM 判定器、近期提出的逐主题分类器进行对比，并评估对官方 TREC DL run 的排序能力。

Result: 在 TREC DL 2019–2021 上，与 NIST 评估员的一致性达到 Claude Sonnet 5.5 LLM 判定器水平；每主题给予少量人工标签时，ExecJudges-L 在全部 7 个集合上优于同等标签条件下的逐主题分类器，且在 Krippendorff's alpha 上超过相同标签条件的 LLM 判定器或差距约在 0.05 以内；生成标签对官方 TREC DL run 的排序 Kendall's tau 为 0.87–0.94；程序编写为一次性成本每主题 $0.17–$0.49，后续评分无额外开销。

Conclusion: ExecJudges 为深度结果池标注提供了一种可复现、可审查、高效且经济的高质量替代方案，可用于系统评估或排序改进，取代 LLM 判定器。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) judges have become a common way to create large-scale relevance judgments for retrieval evaluation. However, each judgment requires a potentially expensive call to a frontier model, and the judgments are hard to interpret and may not be reproducible. In this paper, we ask whether an LLM can write a readable program for each topic that replaces its per-document judgments. To address these issues, we propose ExecJudges, a method for generating fast, inspectable and scalable judges for automatic relevance labeling. Specifically, ExecJudges uses a frontier LLM to turn assessment guidelines into executable programs, which rely only on lexical matching, standard NLP libraries like parsers, and pool statistics. An ExecJudges-L variant also uses examples of human labels if available. We evaluate ExecJudges on seven TREC collections by comparing the produced labels with trained NIST assessors, frontier LLM judges, and recently proposed per-topic classifiers. Our results show that ExecJudges agrees with NIST assessors at the level of the Claude Sonnet 5.5 LLM judges on TREC DL 2019-2021. With a small number of human labels per topic, the ExecJudges-L variant agrees with NIST more than per-topic classifiers with the same labels on all seven collections, and either exceeds the agreement of LLM judges provided the same labels or is within about 0.05 of it in Krippendorff's alpha. The labels rank the official TREC DL runs with Kendall's tau of 0.87 to 0.94, and writing the programs incurs a one-time cost of $0.17 to $0.49 per topic, with no additional costs for grading. Our results suggest that ExecJudges offers a reproducible, inspectable, efficient and cost-effective alternative to LLM judges for labeling deep result pools for system evaluation or ranking improvements.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [16] [Incremental Roseland: Batch-wise Landmark Diffusion via Kernelized Sequential Karhunen--Loève Update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23414)
*Jeanie Schreiber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: iRoseland 将 Roseland 的地标亲和矩阵视为按行增长的矩阵，用核化 SKL 增量更新其 SVD，配合对度校正的一阶展开与样本量不变的嵌入，实现单遍、有界内存的增量地标扩散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始 Roseland 通过数据-地标核矩阵的 SVD 得到类扩散映射谱嵌入，代价远低于全扩散映射，但要求所有数据一次性到齐；因此需要一个支持流式数据、有界内存、并能跟踪分布漂移的增量版本。

Method: 把地标亲和视为行增长矩阵，采用核化 Sequential Karhunen–Loève 方案增量更新其 SVD；度校正通过对参考均值核行的一阶展开近似处理，使地标侧特征基在每步仅需一次地标×地标特征分解即可恢复；引入遗忘因子以跟踪漂移分布，并采用样本量不变的嵌入保证跨时间输出可比。

Result: 在平稳数据上与 Roseland 一致到舍入误差；在长流数据上，在同等内存预算下精度比 Roseland 高一个数量级；在漂移数据上，采用与漂移匹配的窗口时遗忘机制带来显著增益。

Conclusion: iRoseland 在保持 Roseland 低代价优势的同时，实现了单遍、有界内存、状态大小与已处理点数无关的增量谱嵌入，并具备漂移跟踪能力，适用于长流与漂移场景。

Abstract: Landmark diffusion (Roseland) computes a diffusion-map-style spectral embedding from the singular value decomposition of a degree-normalized data-to-landmark kernel matrix, at a cost far below that of full diffusion maps. It assumes, however, that all data are available at once. We introduce incremental Roseland (iRoseland), which casts the landmark affinity as a row-growing matrix whose SVD can be updated with a kernelized Sequential Karhunen--Loève (SKL) scheme. Degre correction is handled by a first order expansion about a reference mean kernel row and so that the landmark-side eigenbasis may be recovered from a single landmark-by-landmark eigendecomposition at each step. The presented method requires only a single pass over the data and runs in bounded-memory with state independent of the number of points processed. A forgetting factor lets the method track drifting distributions, and a sample-size-invariant embedding keeps outputs comparable across time. Experiments show agreement with Roseland to round-off on stationary data, and accuracy that exceeds equal-memory Roseland by an order of magnitude on long streams. On drifting data, we observe large gains from forgetting with a window matched to the drift.

</details>


### [17] [Neural Network-Based Modeling of Hysteretic Systems: From Prandtl and Preisach Neural Networks to Neuro-Madelung and Neuroplastic Continuous Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23240)
*Mehrdad Shafiei Dizaji,Farzad Shafiei Dizaji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 综述Prandtl、Preisach及其推广型神经网络滞回模型（PNN、PreisachNN、GPNN、NMM、EPNN、DPCNN）在记忆表示机制、网络结构与激活函数、训练算法、退化与速率依赖性处理及结构/材料系统应用方面的进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 滞回是兼具记忆依赖、路径依赖，并常伴随退化、非一致性、非对称性与速率依赖的复杂非线性现象，需要系统梳理各类神经网络滞回模型的发展脉络与能力边界。

Method: 按发展顺序评述六类模型：PNN通过停止算子将滞回记忆直接嵌入神经激活；PreisachNN增设非线性隐层以表示非Masing滞回；GPNN引入退化停止神经元刻画刚度与强度退化及非一致滞回；NMM将Madelung滞回规则与多层前馈网络结合；EPNN融合退化滞回算子、非线性神经映射与输入速率信息；并对DPCNN作相应讨论。

Result: PNN实现了结构非线性动力分析的准确模拟；PreisachNN扩展至非Masing滞回；GPNN可表征退化与非一致滞回；NMM覆盖多种速率无关与速率相关行为；EPNN统一处理Masing/非Masing、对称/非对称、一致/非一致及速率无关/速率相关滞回。

Conclusion: 以滞回算子嵌入神经元为内核、辅以非线性隐层与速率信息输入的路线，逐步构建出可统一描述多类滞回行为的神经网络框架，为非线性结构与材料系统建模提供了通用工具。

Abstract: Hysteresis is a complex nonlinear phenomenon characterized by memory dependence, path dependence, and, in many physical systems, degradation, non-congruency, asymmetry, and rate dependence. This review examines the development and capabilities of Prandtl neural networks (PNNs), Preisach neural networks (PreisachNNs), generalized Prandtl neural networks (GPNNs), the neuro-Madelung model (NMM), extended Preisach neural networks (EPNNs), and dynamic plastic continuous neural networks (DPCNNs). Particular attention is given to their mechanisms for representing memory, network architectures, activation functions, training algorithms, treatment of deterioration and rate dependence, and applications to nonlinear structural and material systems. The PNN introduced hysteretic memory directly into neural activation through stop operators and demonstrated accurate nonlinear dynamic analysis of structures. Preisach-NNs subsequently extended this concept through an additional nonlinear hidden layer to represent non-Masing hysteresis. GPNNs introduced deteriorating-stop neurons to capture stiffness and strength degradation and non-congruent hysteresis. A different generalization was provided by the NMM, which combined Madelung's hysteresis rules with multilayer feedforward neural networks to represent diverse rate-independent and rate-dependent behaviors. These developments culminated in the EPNN, which combines deteriorating hysteretic operators, nonlinear neural mappings, and input-rate information to accommodate Masing and nonMasing, symmetric and asymmetric, congruent and noncongruent, and rate-independent and rate-dependent hysteresis.

</details>


### [18] [GridForge: An Open Physics-Informed Digital Twin and Benchmark Suite for UK 11 kV Underground Distribution Cables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23241)
*Lilliane Linnet Musoke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 英国配电电缆监测领域数据丰富但缺乏框架。作者提出 GridForge——开源物理信息数字孪生与基准套件，含IEC标准热模型、64条电缆（320电缆年）合成数据集、满足IEC 60287代数约束的PINN代理模型（RMSE 0.148 °C）及五任务基准，公开数据、代码与模型以支持DNO独立验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 英国配电网络运营商（DNO）每资产每日采集大量遥测数据，但缺乏公开基准数据集、共享参考模型与统一评估协议，导致面向运维决策支持（尤其维护规划与优先级排序）的数据密集型模型无法进行横向比较与验证。

Method: GridForge由四个模块构成：(i) 基于IEC 60287与IEC 60853的稳态与时变热模型并经校验；(ii) 标定的合成数据集，覆盖64条电缆（320电缆年）、四种英国11 kV典型热原型与四种状态模式；(iii) 训练中嵌入IEC 60287代数平衡约束的PINN代理模型；(iv) 含三基线（纯物理、梯度提升树、PINN）的五任务基准套件；参数均取自公开标准，代码采用MIT许可。

Result: PINN在独立留出集上相对解析参考基准的RMSE为0.148 °C，训练约两分钟（见10.1节平台配置）；目前仅虚拟传感器任务（T4）能区分各参考基线，其余任务在该测试划分上持平或未定义。

Conclusion: GridForge填补了该领域公开基准空白，使DNO工程与研究人员可独立复现与验证；局限在于基准区分度不足，后续需为各任务扩展任务特定逻辑的基线模型。

Abstract: The UK distribution-cable monitoring sector is data-rich but framework-poor: each Distribution Network Operator (DNO) collects megabytes of telemetry per asset per day, but there is no public benchmark dataset, no shared reference model, and no commonly agreed evaluation protocol for benchmarking sectoral, data-intensive models for operational decision support; notably for maintenance planning and prioritisation. We address this gap with GridForge, an open-source physics-informed digital twin and benchmark suite for fault diagnosis and prediction, integrating four building blocks: (i) a verified steady state and transient thermal model based on IEC 60287 and IEC 60853; (ii) a calibrated synthetic dataset of 64 cables (covering 320 cable-years) representing four canonical UK 11 kV thermal archetypes and four condition modes; (iii) a Physics-Informed Neural Network (PINN) surrogate that satisfies the algebraic balance defined by IEC 60287 during training and reaches an RMSE of 0.148 °C on an independent held out set against the reference analytical oracle; and (iv) a five-task benchmark suite that includes three reference baseline models (pure physics, gradient-boosted trees, and the PINN). All numerical parameters are sourced from public standards, the code is licensed under the MIT licence, and the trained PINN converges in only two minutes (as run on the computing platform in Section 10.1), enabling independent verification by DNO engineering and research practitioners. At present only the virtual-sensor task (T4) discriminates between the reference baselines; the other tasks tie or are undefined on this test split, so extending the baselines with task-specific logic is a priority future enhancement. For open validation, the dataset, test sets and models are made available at https://github.com/mlily2024/Gridforge

</details>


### [19] [Biologically-Inspired Spike Encoding and Optimization for Spiking Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23242)
*Ayaan Qayyum,Bruce Yi Bu,Zicong Li,Jingfeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将生物启发的脉冲编码（Rate/Latency/DVS式Delta调制/DoG滤波）与Rouser自适应阈值替代梯度、K-FAC二阶优化结合，在MNIST/Fashion-MNIST上验证其可提升SNN稀疏性与参数效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN理论上能效高，但在视觉任务上该优势依赖稀疏脉冲表示，而朴素率编码无法提供；因此需要生物启发的编码与训练改进来缩小SNN与非脉冲基线的精度差距而不增大模型规模。

Method: 组合三类要素：(1) 四种脉冲编码——率编码、延迟编码、DVS式Delta调制、DoG滤波；(2) Rouser可学习逐神经元自适应阈值的替代梯度训练；(3) K-FAC二阶优化，并与Adam基线对比。

Result: 生物启发编码器在容量受限情形下带来最大精度增益，是有效的参数效率手段；DoG与Delta调制产生的脉冲串显著比率编码稀疏；K-FAC相对Adam带来小幅但稳定的精度提升；Rouser可学习阈值通过稳态放电率调节将Fashion-MNIST精度从87.37%提升至88.13%。

Conclusion: 生物启发的预处理方法可在不增加模型规模的前提下帮助缩小SNN与非脉冲基线的精度差距，并提升稀疏性与参数效率。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) offer theoretical energy-efficiency advantages over conventional deep networks, but realizing these advantages on vision tasks requires sparse spike representations that naive rate-coding methods fail to provide. We combine biologically motivated spike-encoding schemes with two training improvements: Rouser adaptive-threshold surrogate gradients and Kronecker-Factored Approximate Curvature (K-FAC) second-order optimization. The spike encoding schemes experimented with are rate coding, latency coding, DVS-style delta modulation, and Difference-of-Gaussians (DoG) filtering. Experiments on MNIST and Fashion-MNIST demonstrate that biologically inspired encoders yield the largest accuracy gains in capacity-constrained regimes and serve as an effective parameter-efficiency lever. DoG and delta modulation encoders produce significantly sparser spike trains than rate coding. K-FAC yields modest but consistent accuracy improvements over Adam-trained baselines, and Rouser's learnable per-neuron thresholds improve Fashion-MNIST accuracy from 87.37% to 88.13% by enabling homeostatic firing-rate regulation. Biologically grounded pre-processing methods can help close the accuracy gap between SNNs and non-spiking baselines without increasing model size.

</details>


### [20] [Goemans's Unsplittable-Flow Conjecture: The Anatomy of AI-Assisted Counterexample Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23244)
*Jingwei Ge,Na Xie,Li Li,Jiayang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将Rybin借助ChatGPT发现的关于Goemans无分流流猜想的七顶点反例纳入一个正向构造框架，从前向角度解释该反例为何成立、其所属的更大反例族，以及其难以盲搜发现的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Goemans曾猜想任意分数单源流都可转化为无分流流且不增加总成本、任一弧负载增量不超过最大需求。Rybin通过与大模型交互得到七顶点反例，推翻了这一长期猜想，但该交互无法说明特定拓扑与需求、分数流、成本选择为何共同奏效，不清楚反例是否属于更大族、哪些结构特征决定反例可能出现，也未能解释AI辅助搜索成功而人类此前努力未果的原因。

Method: 提出正向反例构造流水线：以“失败不可能发生”的树状结构为起点，加入进入终点的捷径弧；由此得到的负载约束定义了一组有限的可行路径选择，其凸包即“冲突多面体”，可精确给出需求、分数流与成本需满足的条件。以简单树状结构为起点，流水线选出Rybin的拓扑并刻画其支持的所有反例；实验令ChatGPT遵循该流水线、改变初始树状结构与捷径位置以检验可迁移性。

Result: 在Rybin拓扑上完成了全部反例的完整刻画；实验中ChatGPT遵循流水线后在明显不同于Rybin的拓扑上构造出反例，并从另外93种有根树状结构出发找到有效构造，说明该流水线可迁移。

Conclusion: 理论解释了原反例为何难以通过盲目枚举发现，并表明Rybin与大模型的交互处于盲目枚举与完全理论引导构造之间；该工作将个例反例提升为可系统生成反例族的构造性理论工具。

Abstract: Goemans conjectured that any fractional single-source flow could be converted into an unsplittable flow without increasing total cost and while increasing any arc load by at most the largest demand. Recently, Dmitry Rybin announced a seven-vertex counterexample found through an interaction with ChatGPT, which disproved this long-standing conjecture. However, the interaction did not reveal why this particular topology and its demand, fractional-flow, and cost choices work together, whether the example belongs to a broader family, or which structural features determine where counterexamples can occur. It also left unclear why the AI-assisted search succeeded where earlier human efforts had not. In this paper, we develop a forward counterexample construction pipeline. It starts from an arborescence on which failure is impossible and adds terminal-entering shortcut arcs. The resulting load constraints define a finite set of admissible route choices; their convex hull, the conflict polytope, yields exact conditions for demands, fractional flows, and costs. Starting from a simple arborescence, the pipeline selects Rybin's topology and characterizes all counterexamples it supports. Experiments further demonstrate the pipeline's transferability: when instructed to follow it while varying the initial arborescence and shortcut placement, ChatGPT produced counterexamples on topologies genuinely different from Rybin's and found valid constructions starting from 93 other rooted arborescence shapes. The theory also explains why the original example was difficult to discover through blind enumeration and suggests that Rybin's interaction lies between blind enumeration and a complete theory-guided construction.

</details>


### [21] [Response-Relevant Information Is Not Sufficient for Neural Training-State Equivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23245)
*Truong Xuan Khanh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出以"训练状态等价"的操作性定义（相对某干预族与时间跨度，训练扰动后响应相似即等价），在一层Transformer学模加法的设定下验证该目标可测（权重衰减脉冲响应的rho_state=0.934），并检验15个权重谱、表示几何与优化几何描述子是否携带超出现有指标的响应相关信息；结论是这些信息虽可被解码器提取，却无法在配对几何中组织起来以支持相似性判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作默认"响应相关信息"足以判断两个神经网络是否处于同一训练状态，但缺乏可操作、可测量的训练状态等价定义；同时，描述子携带的信息是否真能转化为检查点匹配等实用判断尚未被严格检验，需区分"信息存在"与"信息可用于配对相似性判断"。

Method: 操作性定义训练状态等价：给定干预族与时间跨度，若两检查点在训练被扰动并继续后响应相似则等价。实验对象为一层Transformer学习modular addition的全新训练运行，施加权重衰减脉冲作为探针，计算状态间响应相关性rho_state。在开发集上构造15个描述子（权重谱、表示几何、优化器几何），控制权重范数与损失，跨seed块预测留出响应偏差，并与保持描述子对常规可观测变量拟合依赖的条件安慰剂对照比较，另设校准阳性对照排除简单不敏感性。

Result: 500步时rho_state=0.934，扰动响应相对于检查点间差异高度可重复，目标可测。15个描述子在开发集上携带超出权重范数与损失的响应相关信息。但其在匹配检查点任务上16.1%的表观优势无法与条件安慰剂区分；校准阳性对照表明简单不敏感性不太可能解释该结果。

Conclusion: 响应相关信息的存在不等于可用于配对相似性判断：解码器可提取的信息未必组织在相似性判断所需的成对几何中。因此以响应相关信息作为"同一训练状态"的证据并不充分，需重新审视基于描述子的检查点匹配与等价性推断。

Abstract: Response-relevant information is not sufficient evidence that two neural networks are in the same training state. We define training-state equivalence operationally: two checkpoints are equivalent, relative to an intervention family and horizon, if they respond alike when training is perturbed and continues. On fresh runs of a one-layer transformer learning modular addition, response to a weight-decay pulse is highly reproducible relative to between-checkpoint differences (rho_state = 0.934 at 500 steps), establishing the target as measurable. Fifteen descriptors of weight spectra, representation geometry and optimiser geometry carry within-run response-relevant information beyond weight norm and loss on a development dataset, predicting held-out response deviation across seed blocks. But their apparent 16.1% advantage at matching checkpoints by future response does not separate from a conditional placebo preserving each descriptor's fitted dependence on the conventional observables, and a calibrated positive control makes simple insensitivity an unlikely explanation. Information a decoder can extract need not be organised in the pairwise geometry that similarity judgments require.

</details>


### [22] [Chebyshev Polynomial Zonotope Abstractions for Certified Reachability of Learning-Enabled Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23246)
*Peng Xie,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文证明：用等阶Chebyshev抽象替代Taylor展开作为多项式zonotope的基，可消除由复平面奇点造成的可达集宽度上限，得到无上限且宽度随阶数几何增长的认证，并给出闭式严格余项（含首个softmax transformer多项式化处理）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非线性动力学可达集计算普遍采用逐点截断Taylor展开加严格余项：当自变量从点变为集合时，单一多项式须在整个集合上保持精度，其有效范围受收敛圆盘限制。该限制在最需要的地方最紧——现代控制器的有理项与sigmoid门在实轴附近（复数平面内）存在奇点，门越锐利奇点越靠近实轴，从而给可覆盖集合宽度设下硬性上限，尤其在控制器决策的切换面上。

Method: 提出Taylor与Chebyshev的二分性论证：证明不存在任何Taylor阶数能认证超过该上限的集合宽度；而等阶Chebyshev抽象（锚定于整个集合、嵌入同一多项式-zonotope流水线）无此上限，宽度随阶数几何速率增长。给出闭式严格余项，包括首个多项式化softmax transformer；并在闭环、训练分类器（至真实MNIST数字）上对比两种基的可认证范围，配合大样本Monte-Carlo检验与适用性地图。

Result: 实测"墙"（宽度上限）与理论公式吻合度为89%–94%；闭环中Taylor证书发散处Chebyshev证书仍成立，规范由"未知"翻转为"已证"；在训练分类器上Chebyshev前沿能认证Taylor基放弃的输入区域；所有认证结论经大规模Monte-Carlo验证零违例；并给出预先判定基选择无差别的适用性地图。

Conclusion: Taylor展开的收敛圆盘奇点对可达集宽度构成本质、不可通过提高阶数绕过的上限；Chebyshev集合锚定抽象在同一流水线中既无上限又具几何收敛性，且可给出严格余项，因而在闭环控制与分类器验证中显著扩展可认证范围。

Abstract: Nearly all current methods for computing reachable sets of nonlinear dynamics propagate them with truncated Taylor expansions plus rigorous remainders. The expansion is pointwise: once its argument is a whole set rather than a point, one polynomial must stay accurate across that set, and it reaches only as far as its disc of convergence. The limit binds hardest on the rational terms and sigmoid gates of modern controllers: smooth as these look on the real line, in the complex plane they blow up at points just off the real axis, closer as a gate sharpens, and those points cap the set width a Taylor expansion can cover, most tightly on the switching surface where the controller decides. We prove a dichotomy: no Taylor degree certifies sets wider than that cap, while a Chebyshev abstraction of equal degree, anchored to the whole set in the same polynomial-zonotope pipeline, has no cap and gains width at a geometric rate in degree. Measured walls match the formulas to between 89 and 94 percent. We give closed-form rigorous remainders, among them the first polynomial softmax transformer; existing sound treatments are linear relaxations. On closed loops, certificates survive where the Taylor ones diverge, and specifications flip from unknown to proven; on trained classifiers up to real MNIST digits, the Chebyshev frontier certifies input territory the Taylor basis surrenders. Every certified claim passed large Monte-Carlo harnesses with zero violations, and an applicability map states in advance where the basis provably cannot matter.

</details>


### [23] [AllotNet: an Interpretable Mixture-of-Experts Choice Model for Moral Judgement in Humanitarian Aid Allocation, Validated against a Planted Synthetic Population](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23474)
*Mark Nowak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出可解释的专家混合条件logit系统AllotNet，用于援助资源分配中的人类伦理判断建模，并先构建合成"真值"数据完成全部16项预设检验后才计划用于真实数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2025年官方发展援助下降23.1%，"资源不足时谁先受助"的分配决策正在进入软件；一个已部署的机器学习定向系统排除了53%的合格家庭。作者主张分配规则若落入代码，应承载多文化的判断，机器执行、人来裁断，因此需要一个可解释、可跨文化、可在预算约束下运行的分配判断模型。

Method: AllotNet：面向Allot（预注册六语言工具）所诱导分配判断的可解释专家混合条件logit——七个伦理标准上的八个线性"口味"专家，由参与者的gate按语境加权，带阻尼残差、家庭intake编码器、结果头和预算约束规划器（构建复核队列、从不拒绝任何人）。由于真人判断数据无答案，先写"答案"：由生产代码路径生成560万会话、1.04亿响应（9800万可用选择）的植入合成总体，真值对损失函数隐藏。模型23,550参数，量化为NVFP4（可解释核心保fp32）训练，W4A16下福利秩一致性ρ=0.9937（一项parity阈值事后放宽），以125KB bundle发布并提供C11/Java/Rust/Python运行时。

Result: 16项预设检验全部通过：标准排序ρ=1.000，隐藏分群被恢复（ARI 0.671），结果头达噪声上限的99%，规划器达oracle的99.4%。同时报告局限：两项检验弱于其标题所称，一次实时生成失败损失260,712个seed。尚未在真实数据上训练。

Conclusion: AllotNet以小型、可解释、可量化部署的形式验证了"多文化判断+机器执行+人类裁断"的分配框架在合成真值下可行；真实规模研究仍待进行，注册的比较基准是分层贝叶斯混合logit。

Abstract: Official development assistance fell by 23.1 per cent in 2025, and the decision of who is helped first when there is not enough for everyone is moving into software. A leading published machine-learning targeting system excluded 53 per cent of eligible households as deployed. If the rule of distribution ends up in code, it should carry the judgement of many cultures, and the machine should execute while a person judges. AllotNet is an interpretable mixture-of-experts conditional logit for the allocation judgements elicited by Allot, a pre-registered six-language instrument: eight linear taste experts over seven ethical criteria, weighted by a gate over participant context, with a damped residual, an encoder for household intake, an outcome head and a budget-constrained planner that builds a review queue and never rejects anyone. A model of human judgement has no answer key on real data, so we wrote one first: a planted synthetic population of 5.6 million sessions and 104 million responses (98 million usable choices), generated by the production code path from a ground truth hidden from the loss. All sixteen pre-specified checks passed: criterion ranking $ρ= 1.000$, hidden segments recovered (ARI 0.671), outcome head at 99 per cent of the noise ceiling, planner at 99.4 per cent of oracle. The caveats are reported with them: two checks are weaker than their titles, and a live-generation failure cost 260,712 seeds. The model has 23,550 parameters, is trained quantisation-aware for NVFP4 with its interpretable core in fp32 (welfare-rank parity $ρ= 0.9937$ at W4A16 on the shipped bundle; one parity threshold was relaxed post hoc), and ships as a 125 KB bundle with C11, Java, Rust and Python runtimes. Nothing has been trained on real data yet; at the study's real scale a hierarchical Bayesian mixed logit is the registered comparator.

</details>


### [24] [Budgeted Proper Loss Design for Uncertain Decision Thresholds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23247)
*Ankit Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在阈值分布属于有限给定族、且损失权重需满足正下界与固定总质量的约束下，推导出使"代理遗憾→阈值平均决策遗憾"的最坏情形乘子达到最小且尖锐的权重，解为阈值密度的上包络的缩放裁剪，其归一化通过一维求根得到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一平均阈值分布不能保证最坏情形遗憾，需要针对阈值分布族设计proper loss，使得从代理遗憾到阈值平均决策遗憾的乘子最小化。

Method: 在可积、正点态下界、总质量固定的损失权重族中，最小化尖锐且分布无关的乘子；其解为按比例缩放阈值密度上包络并在下界处截断，归一化由一维根搜索确定；另给出尖锐性、最优性与密度误设下稳定性的证明。

Result: 给出尖锐性、最优性及密度误设定下稳定性的证明，并揭示原子阈值律的局限；实现精确直方图算法，在20个随机种子上进行两个合成实验验证；优化乘子优于均匀与平均密度权重，但拟合模型表现并非一致提升。

Conclusion: 该结果是在显式预算约束下的损失设计保证，而非预测优越性的普遍结论。

Abstract: A binary probability estimate may be used by several decision makers with different error costs. Proper scoring rules can emphasize selected probability thresholds, but a single average threshold distribution need not give a good worst-case regret guarantee. We study the choice of a proper loss when the threshold distribution belongs to a finite specified family. Among integrable loss weights with a positive pointwise floor and a fixed total mass, we derive the weight that minimizes the sharp, distribution-free multiplier from surrogate regret to threshold-averaged decision regret. The solution clips a rescaled upper envelope of the threshold densities at the prescribed floor; its normalization is found by a one-dimensional root search. We give proofs of sharpness, optimality, and stability under density misspecification, as well as a limitation for atomic threshold laws. An exact histogram implementation and two synthetic experiments across 20 seeds verify the construction. The optimized multiplier improves over uniform and average-density weights, while fitted-model performance does not improve uniformly. The result is a loss-design guarantee under an explicit budget, rather than a general claim of predictive superiority.

</details>


### [25] [Exploring Parkinson's Diagnosis Using Machine Learning Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23248)
*Farhaan Qazi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将SVM、决策树、多层感知机、KNN、逻辑回归、随机森林（含/不含粒子群优化）应用于利兹教学医院NHS信托58人帕金森病数据集，比较其诊断性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帕金森病为不可治愈的神经退行性疾病，亟需准确、及时的诊断手段，机器学习被视为有前景的工具，因此研究探索ML算法在PD诊断中的整合与应用潜力。

Method: 在58名受试者的PD数据集上，采用支持向量机、决策树、多层感知机、K近邻、逻辑回归和随机森林（分别在有、无粒子群优化条件下）进行建模与分类性能评估。

Result: 准确率方面，决策树与逻辑回归最佳，达72–76%；多层感知机效果最差，为72–76%。各模型对阳性PD病例的敏感度突出，最高达97.56%，其中SVM最优；但将健康对照与PD病例区分开的能力整体不显著。

Conclusion: 所选模型在该数据集上对PD患者分类结果尚可，但特异性不足；扩大数据规模、深入研究和改进ML方法有望提升诊断效果，并增进对帕金森病的理解。

Abstract: In recent years, machine learning (ML) techniques have emerged as promising tools for accurate and timely diagnosis of Parkinson's disease (PD) which is an incurable neurodegenerative disorder. This study explores the integration of ML algorithms and PD diagnosis, highlighting their potential applications in various aspects of the disease. ML methods such as support vector machines, decision trees, multilayer perceptron, k-nearest neighbours, logistic regression and random forest (with and without particle swarm optimisation) were applied on a PD dataset obtained from Leeds Teaching Hospitals NHS Trust containing data from 58 people. In terms of accuracy, the Decision Tree and Logistic Regression performed the best with scores of 72-76% while multilayer perceptron was least effective. All the models were remarkable in classifying positive PD cases with sensitivity scores going up to 97.56%, with SVM classifier at the pinnacle. However, ability to classify healthy controls from PD cases was insignificant throughout the board. Although, all the employed models delivered reasonable results in categorising PD patients in the presented dataset; access to more data, further study and advancement of these ML approaches could lead to enhanced diagnostic outcomes, supplementing to an improved understanding of Parkinson's disease.

</details>


### [26] [Variance-Optimal Counterfactual Policy Design for Sample-Efficient Online A/B Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24757)
*Shashank Gupta,Subhadeep Maji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对在线A/B测试中difference-in-means估计量方差大的问题，联合优化动作采样方式（logging policy）与估计量构造，提出方差最优的动作级logging策略及对偶优化框架，在模拟实验中显著提升ATE估计精度与统计功效，降低错误选择较差策略的概率，从而以更少流量做出可靠部署决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准A/B测试的difference-in-means估计量虽无偏但方差高：当处理策略与生产策略对相同动作的选择概率接近时，观测数据只带来噪声而几乎不含策略差异信息，导致需要大量流量和长时间才能检测出小幅处理效应。已有方差缩减方法要么在数据采集方案固定后才改进估计量，要么仅优化两策略间的单一路径分流（overlap-aware design），无法将流量定向到策略存在分歧的动作上。

Method: 联合优化动作采样策略与观测数据的使用方式（估计量构造）。第一，基于pairwise off-policy evaluation推导用于估计两策略间平均处理效应（ATE）的方差最优动作级logging policy，该策略对任意固定control variate具有闭式解。第二，利用历史日志联合优化logging policy与control variate，以进一步降低方差。

Result: 在模拟策略A/B测试中，所提出的设计在每一个评测设置下都比现有方差缩减方法更准确地估计平均处理效应，同时总体上提升统计功效，并降低选中较差策略的频率。

Conclusion: 通过将流量定向到策略分歧动作并联合优化logging policy与control variate，所提设计使从业者能以显著更少的在线流量达到可靠的部署决策。

Abstract: Online A/B testing is the standard approach for evaluating changes to user-facing systems, such as a new recommendation or ranking policy, before deployment. While the difference-in-means estimator of a standard A/B test is unbiased, it often suffers from high variance. When the treatment and production policies choose the same actions with similar probabilities, the resulting observations can add noise while providing little information about the difference between the policies. Consequently, A/B tests can require large amounts of traffic and long durations to detect small treatment effects. Existing variance-reduction methods mainly improve the estimator after the data-collection scheme has been fixed, and recent overlap-aware designs only optimize a single traffic split between the two policies; thereby, they cannot direct traffic to the actions on which the policies disagree.
  In this paper, we jointly optimize how actions are sampled and how the resulting observations are used to estimate the treatment effect. Our contributions are two-fold. First, building on pairwise off-policy evaluation, we derive the variance-optimal action-level logging policy for estimating the average treatment effect between two policies, which has a closed-form solution for any fixed control variate. Second, we use historical logs to jointly optimize the logging policy and a control variate to further reduce variance. Our experimental results in simulated policy A/B tests show that our designs estimate the average treatment effect more accurately than existing variance reduction methods on every evaluated setting, while generally improving statistical power and reducing how often the worse policy is selected. As a result, our designs enable practitioners to reach reliable deployment decisions with considerably less online traffic.

</details>


### [27] [Optimize the Cob, Then the Kernel: Optimizing Beam Search for Generative Recommendation Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23339)
*Shing Wai Pun,Kai Wu,Pengfei Xia,Hui Zang,Wei Guo,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对生成式推荐（GR）推理长输入、宽束搜索、极少固定输出token的特点，指出vLLM将各beam当作独立序列并逐步单独调用的缺陷，提出用Tree Attention沿序列维拼接beam以消除共享前缀的冗余KV访问，并在不写自定义算子的前提下将端到端时延最高降低25倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐推理与传统LLM服务差异显著：输入序列长、beam宽、输出token少且固定。现有引擎（如vLLM）将beam作为批内独立序列处理，对共享prompt前缀产生冗余KV cache访问；同时每个beam search生成步都被当作一次独立引擎调用，带来主机侧beam处理开销与PagedAttention中大量运行时KV块管理开销。

Method: 采用Tree Attention将各beam沿序列维拼接，消除冗余KV访问；并以预生成的部分注意力掩码、静态KV cache分配、循环展开的生成调度、TorchAir图编译、自动算子融合、词表约束解码等手段整体优化执行流程。全程不使用自定义kernel。

Result: 在Ascend NPU上，端到端时延相较vLLM最高降低25倍，生成时延与使用自定义kernel的SOTA方案相当。

Conclusion: 在求助于自定义算子开发之前，应先对生成流水线做整体（holistic）优化，即可获得与自定义kernel方案相当的收益。

Abstract: Generative recommendation (GR) inference differs substantially from conventional LLM serving, featuring long input sequences, wide beam search, and only a few fixed output tokens. Existing LLM serving engines such as vLLM process beams as batched independent sequences, causing redundant KV cache accesses over the shared prompt prefix. Furthermore, vLLM executes each beam search generation step as a separate engine invocation, incurring host-side beam processing overhead between decoding steps and substantial runtime KV block management in PagedAttention. Using Tree Attention, we concatenate beams along the sequence dimension to eliminate redundant KV accesses, and further optimize execution through pre-generated partial-attention masks, static KV cache allocation, loop-unrolled generation dispatch, TorchAir graph compilation, automatic operator fusion, and vocabulary-constrained decoding. Without the use of custom kernels, our approach reduces end-to-end latency by up to 25x over vLLM on Ascend NPUs, and achieves generation latency comparable to a SOTA solution using custom kernels, showing that the generation pipeline should be optimized holistically before resorting to custom kernel development.

</details>


### [28] [Confidence-Based Materials Data Quality Assessment via Domain Knowledge and Graph-Structured Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23342)
*Sihan Liaing,Ji Zhang,Kehan Wang,Zihao Gao,Wei Ma,Yangang Wang,Zongguo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于置信度的材料数据质量评估框架，将数据质量建模为连续置信度估计，融合领域规则与图结构学习，实现样本级评估与批次比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料机器学习依赖大规模异构数据，但真实数据库中测量误差、计算失败、缺失/重复记录及违反物理化学约束等问题难以识别，因为受损样本常保持统计合理性而非显式离群点。

Method: 构建九维特征质量先验与重复感知样本初始化（基于材料领域规则与统计证据校准）；通过样本-特征二部图传播与弱监督图注意力精化置信度；用Memory-GAT模块提供历史参考评分。

Result: 23个ODDS基准数据集上通用模块平均AUC-ROC达0.866；mp_20五次混合污染实验中规则校准将HistGradientBoostingRegressor测试R²从0.538±0.008提升至0.616±0.014，图精化使平均精度从0.943±0.004升至0.953±0.004，规则校准配置平均测试R²最高；MACE子集五次重复实验中规则筛选后R²由0.590±0.053升至0.880±0.027。

Conclusion: 所提置信度框架可有效评估材料数据质量，领域规则校准与图精化均显著提升下游建模性能，验证了连续置信度建模思路的有效性。

Abstract: The rapid development of materials machine learning relies on large-scale, heterogeneous datasets, yet model reliability is often compromised by imperfect data from diverse experiments, simulations, and database integration. Real-world materials databases may contain measurement errors, failed calculations, missing or duplicate records, and hidden violations of physicochemical constraints. Such problems are difficult to identify because corrupted samples may remain statistically plausible rather than appearing as explicit outliers. In this paper, we propose a confidence-based materials data quality assessment framework that integrates domain knowledge with graph-structured learning. Data quality is formulated as a continuous confidence estimation problem rather than a binary anomaly identification task, enabling sample-level assessment and historical-reference-based batch comparison. A nine-dimensional feature-quality prior and duplicate-aware sample initialization are calibrated using materials domain rules and statistical evidence. Confidence is further refined through sample-feature bipartite graph propagation and weakly supervised graph attention, while a Memory-GAT module provides historical reference scoring. Experiments on 23 ODDS benchmark datasets show that the generic module achieves an average AUC-ROC of 0.866. In five repeated mixed-contamination experiments on mp\_20, rule calibration improves HistGradientBoostingRegressor test $R^2$ from $0.538\pm0.008$ to $0.616\pm0.014$. Graph refinement increases average precision from $0.943\pm0.004$ to 0.953\pm0.004$, while the rule-calibrated configuration has the highest mean test $R^2$ among the evaluated screening configurations. Five repeated MACE subset experiments further show an increase in $R^2$ from $0.590\pm0.053$ to $0.880\pm0.027$ after rule-based screening.

</details>


### [29] [BiMa-ECGSR: reconstructing the 12-lead ECG from reduced lead sets with bidirectional state-space models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23345)
*Ugo Lomoio,Pierangelo Veltri,Pietro Hiram Guzzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 BiMa-ECGSR，用双向 Mamba 状态空间编解码器从少量可穿戴导联重建 12 导联 ECG（回归版 BiMa-SR 与扩散版 DiBiMa-SR）；在 PTB-XL 与两个外部队列上均显著优于最小二乘线性滤波，重建信号可被 12 导联分类器直接使用，但无法补充原始导联中不存在的信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴设备与监护仪通常只记录极少数 ECG 导联，而多数临床诊断工具均以 12 导联为前提，导致低导联记录难以直接接入现有诊断流程，因此需要从减少的导联集合重建完整 12 导联 ECG。

Method: 构建 BiMa-ECGSR：以双向 Mamba 状态空间编码器—解码器为主干，提供回归形式（BiMa-SR）与扩散形式（DiBiMa-SR）。在 PTB-XL 上训练六种输入集（I；II；I+II；I+II+V1；I+II+V2；I+II+V1+V4），推理时仅见实测导联；在 PTB-XL 测试折（2,198 条）及两个外部队列（Georgia 10,275 条；Chapman-Shaoxing/Ningbo 43,559 条）上评测，并以最小二乘线性滤波作为基线；同时用仅训练于真实 ECG 的 12 导联分类器评估重建信号的下游可用性。

Result: 仅用导联 II 时，11 个预测导联的合并中位 PCC 在 PTB-XL 上为 0.914（线性滤波 0.784；导联 I 为 0.906 vs 0.789）。对可由 I、II 精确导出的肢体导联不作比较，改以预测胸导联评估多导联输入：I+II 为 0.939 vs 0.849，I+II+V1+V4 为 0.979 vs 0.962。BiMa-SR 在全部 18 个"配置—队列"组合中均优于线性基线（提升 0.012–0.136），且随胸导联增加优势收窄。单导联 II 下回归优于扩散（DiBiMa-SR：单步 0.903，六步 DDIM 0.862）。12 导联分类器在真实 PTB-XL ECG 上超类宏平均 AUROC 为 0.912，由导联 II 重建信号上为 0.843，由 I+II+V1+V4 重建信号上为 0.908；外部数据中三或四导联重建与真实 ECG 差距在 0.004 以内。BiMa-SR 参数量 2.68 M，GPU 上每条 10 s 记录耗时 45.8 ms。

Conclusion: 重建可使低导联记录被现有 12 导联工具使用，多导联输入时重建质量与下游诊断性能接近真实 ECG；但重建不能增加实测导联中原本缺失的信息，单导联场景下精度与诊断性能仍有明显下降，且本文未报告外部队列上线性基线之外的系统对比之外的统计不确定性。

Abstract: Wearables and monitors record few electrocardiogram (ECG) leads; most diagnostic tools assume 12 leads. BiMa-ECGSR reconstructs the 12-lead ECG from reduced lead sets with a bidirectional Mamba state-space encoder--decoder, as regression (BiMa-SR) or diffusion (DiBiMa-SR). Models were trained on PTB-XL for six input sets (I; II; I+II; I+II+V1; I+II+V2; I+II+V1+V4), see only the measured leads at inference, and were tested on the PTB-XL test fold (2,198 records) and two external cohorts (Georgia, 10,275 records; Chapman-Shaoxing/Ningbo, 43,559). From lead II alone, the pooled median Pearson correlation (PCC) over the 11 predicted leads was 0.914 on PTB-XL, against 0.784 for a least-squares linear filter (lead I: 0.906 against 0.789). As limb leads are exactly derivable from I and II, multi-lead inputs were compared on predicted chest leads: 0.939 against 0.849 for I+II and 0.979 against 0.962 for I+II+V1+V4. BiMa-SR exceeded the linear comparator in all 18 configuration--cohort pairs (by 0.012--0.136), a margin that shrank as chest leads were added. From lead II, regression was more accurate than diffusion (DiBiMa-SR: 0.903 one-step, 0.862 with six DDIM steps). A 12-lead classifier trained only on real ECGs reached a superclass macro AUROC of 0.912 on real PTB-XL ECGs, 0.843 on reconstructions from lead II and 0.908 from I+II+V1+V4; externally, reconstructions from three or four leads scored within 0.004 of real ECGs. BiMa-SR (2.68\,M parameters) takes 45.8 ms per 10-s record on a GPU. Reconstruction makes reduced-lead recordings usable by 12-lead tools; it cannot add information absent from the measured leads.

</details>


### [30] [Implementing Binary Transformers with Approximate Memories with Permutation Invariance Enabled Selective (PIES) Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23358)
*Zhen Gao,Xiao Yin,Kexin Liang,Pedro Reviriego,Shanshan Liu,Fabrizio Lombardi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估二值化BERT在近似内存位错误下的可靠性，并提出利用置换不变性的选择性保护方案PIES，以极低开销恢复大部分精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二值权重可降低Transformer的存储、计算与功耗，近似内存能进一步省电但引入位错误；现有二值Transformer研究只关注任务性能，缺乏对内存错误下可靠性的研究。

Method: 以BERT为案例评估二值Transformer的可靠性；提出PIES（Permutation Invariance Enabled Selective）方案，利用Transformer对行列置换的不变性，将关键权重分组并进行选择性保护。

Result: 仅在较高误码率（BER=5%或10%）时才出现明显精度损失；PIES在BER高达10%时可恢复大部分性能损失，且开销显著低于传统纠错方案。

Conclusion: 结果验证了在资源受限低功耗边缘设备上，以近似内存配合激进的电压缩放运行二值Transformer的可行性。

Abstract: Quantization is an effective technique to reduce memory storage, computation complexity and power for Transformers, and the binarization of the weights is important for meeting these requirements. Approximate memory can further reduce the power for memory, but it also introduces errors. Current research for binary Transformers focuses on task performance, but the model dependable operation against errors on the memory has not been well studied. In this paper, we first evaluate the dependability of binary Transformers with the Bidirectional Encoder Representations from Transformers (BERT) as a case study. A large accuracy loss occurs only at high Bit Error Rates (e.g. BER = 5% or 10%). Then we propose a novel Permutation Invariance Enabled Selective (PIES) scheme to protect the binary weights at very low overhead. This scheme exploits the invariance of Transformers to permutations in the rows and columns to group the critical weights and perform selective protection on those weights. Simulation results show that PIES can recover most of the performance loss caused by bit errors at BERs as high as 10%, at an overhead significantly lower than traditional error correction schemes. These results confirm the feasibility of running binary Transformers using approximate memory with aggressive voltage scaling on resource-limited low power edge devices. The code for PIES is released on github (https://github.com/YangZhutian/binary-transformer-prefix-repair).

</details>


### [31] [TRACE-MAS: Diagnosing and Repairing Collaboration Failures in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23362)
*Zekai Chen,Yihan Sun,Xunkai Li,Xun Wu,Xinmo Jin,Yinlin Zhu,Haodong Lu,Xuaner Chen,Rong-Hua Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRACE-MAS：从"智能体级"异常检测升级为"交互级"故障诊断与修复，学习正常协作执行模式并做源感知归因，实现根因定位与最小干预修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 多智能体系统（MAS）的安全防护主要做智能体级异常检测；当故障源于智能体之间的交互（消息交换、任务依赖）时，此类方法不足，且检测到的异常未必指向真正的故障来源。

Method: 从良性轨迹中学习正常协作执行模式；检测智能体状态变化、消息转移与任务依赖演化上的偏离；通过源感知归因（source-aware attribution）识别应负责的执行元素；选择最小干预以恢复协作，同时保留任务关键能力。

Result: 在五个 MAS 基准上，根因定位准确率提升 60.2%，并在任务恢复上相较强基线 MAS 防护方法取得一致增益。

Conclusion: 交互级建模对 MAS 故障诊断至关重要；TRACE-MAS 以细粒度、可归因、最小干预的方式统一了诊断与修复，优于智能体级防护方案。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) rely on not only individual agent behaviors but also the interactions that connect agents, including message exchanges and task dependencies. Existing MAS safeguards mainly perform agent-level anomaly detection, which becomes insufficient when failures originate from agent interactions and detected anomalies do not necessarily reveal their true sources. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{TRACE-MAS}, a finer-grained framework for diagnosing and repairing MAS failures. TRACE-MAS learns normal collaborative execution patterns from benign trajectories and detects deviations in agent state changes, message transitions, and task dependency evolutions. It further identifies responsible execution elements through source-aware attribution and selects minimal interventions to restore collaboration while preserving task-critical capabilities. Experiments on five MAS benchmarks demonstrate that TRACE-MAS significantly improves root-cause localization accuracy by \textbf{60.2}\% and achieves consistent gains in task recovery over strong MAS safeguards.

</details>


### [32] [QDA: Closed-Loop LLM Quantization Design with Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23388)
*Xingyang Li,Tianlang Zhao,Song Han,Ligeng Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QDA：用LLM agent自动为每个模型与精度设计后训练量化配方，借助KL散度快速打分与可组合PTQ方法库，在多种推理模型和精度下超越最强公开配方，且无推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练量化的最佳配方因模型和精度而异，需专家为每个新模型重新设计，耗时且常次优。让LLM agent通过多轮“构建-评估”自动迭代看似可行，但单次量化推理模型评估需数十GPU小时，且跨代码库组合方法需手工移植到新架构，成本过高。

Method: 提出QDA系统，由agent为每个模型和精度设计量化配方。评估加速：用候选模型在agent未见过的推理轨迹上相对原模型的输出KL散度，几分钟内完成打分；对表现好的候选再用长生成检验重复循环等失效。构建加速：提供相互兼容、且适配新模型架构的公开PTQ方法库。

Result: 在四个推理模型上，W4A16、W4A8、W3A16下平均分别比最强公开配方提升0.9、1.4、2.2个百分点，每次搜索用64 GPU小时；在320B参数MoE模型GLM-5.3-Flash的NVFP4 W4A4上恢复FP8模型98.3%精度，优于最佳厂商checkpoint的96.9%；所有checkpoint可直接运行于现有serving kernel，无推理开销。

Conclusion: QDA表明LLM agent驱动的自动化PTQ配方设计可在可接受的搜索成本下稳定优于人工/厂商配方，并保持部署兼容性；作者将开源方法库、评估器与harness。

Abstract: Post-training quantization is the standard way to serve large language models at low cost. However, the best quantization recipe differs across models and precisions. As a result, experts must redesign it for every new model, which is time-consuming and often sub-optimal. Fortunately, an LLM agent can take over this design by refining the recipe over rounds of building and evaluating candidates, so that more rounds lead to better recipes. However, evaluating one quantized reasoning model takes tens of GPU-hours, and combining methods from different codebases means porting each to the new architecture by hand. We present QDA, a system in which an agent designs the recipe for each model and precision. To make evaluation fast, QDA scores each candidate in minutes by its output KL divergence from the original model on reasoning traces that the agent never sees, and checks promising ones with long generations to catch failures such as repetition loops. To make building fast, QDA provides a library of public PTQ methods that are compatible with one another and with new model architectures. Across four reasoning models, QDA improves accuracy over the strongest public recipe by 0.9, 1.4 and 2.2 percentage points on average at W4A16, W4A8 and W3A16, using 64 GPU-hours per search. On the 320B-parameter MoE model GLM-5.3-Flash at NVFP4 W4A4, it recovers 98.3% of the FP8 model's accuracy, against 96.9% for the best vendor checkpoint. Every QDA checkpoint also runs on existing serving kernels with no inference overhead. We will release the library, the evaluator and the harness.

</details>


### [33] [Which Tokens, in What Order, and How Fast? A Theoretical Analysis of On-Policy Distillation Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23397)
*Hubert Strauss,Liam Fowl,Noam Razin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从理论上分析了on-policy蒸馏（OPD）中目标选择（序列级 vs 词元级、不同散度）如何决定词元优先顺序与优化可行性，揭示了序列级OPD的"未来一致性偏差"导致的逆序学习现象，并证明前向KL散度比反向KL等f-散度更利于梯度优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD在实践中被广泛采用，但关于"在何种设置下应选择何种OPD目标（序列级还是词元级、哪种散度）"缺乏形式化理解。作者希望填补这一理论空白，明确不同OPD目标对训练动态的影响机制。

Method: 理论分析（formal analysis）：分析不同OPD目标（序列级/词元级、前向KL/反向KL及其他f-散度）如何支配词元的优先化顺序与效率；并在多个语言模型后训练设置中进行实验，验证理论发现对目标选择的启示。

Result: 1）序列级OPD存在"未来一致性偏差"，短视的词元级目标可避免该偏差；2）该偏差引发逆序学习：序列级OPD先匹配教师较后位置的词元，而在较早位置可能偏向教师在当前上下文下概率极低、因而通常不理想的词元；3）散度选择强烈影响OPD对梯度优化的适应性：前向KL具有良态的目标地形，而反向KL等其他f-散度即使学生与教师差异很大也可能出现梯度消失。

Conclusion: OPD目标的选择应结合学生模型、教师模型与具体任务的相互作用来权衡；词元级目标可规避序列级偏差，前向KL在优化上更稳健。研究为实践中OPD目标的选择提供了理论依据。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student language model to match a teacher model based on its own generations. When using OPD, one must choose whether to apply it at the sequence or token level and which divergence to minimize. However, despite the wide adoption of OPD in practice, the formal understanding of which OPD objective is appropriate for a given setting is limited. Toward addressing this gap, we theoretically analyze how the choice of OPD objective governs which tokens are prioritized, in what order, and how efficiently. Our analysis reveals that sequence-level OPD exhibits a future agreement bias, which myopic token-level objectives avoid. This bias induces a reverse learning order, in which sequence-level OPD first matches the teacher at later token positions, while at earlier positions it can favor tokens that are improbable under the teacher, and thus often undesirable. As for the choice of divergence, we prove that it strongly influences the amenability of OPD to gradient-based optimization. In particular, while the forward KL divergence enjoys a well-behaved objective landscape, other $f$-divergences, such as reverse KL, can suffer from vanishing gradients even when the student and teacher are far apart. Across language model post-training settings, we demonstrate the implications of these theoretical findings for which OPD objective is preferable, highlighting the importance of accounting for the interplay between the student, teacher, and task at hand.

</details>


### [34] [Stability Does Not Mirror Plasticity: A Module-Level View of Declarative and Procedural Learning in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23427)
*Jinjun Peng,Wailiang Zhao,Baishakhi Ray,Zhou Yu,Jufeng Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文在模块层面研究持续学习中的可塑性与稳定性，发现二者受模块功能角色的不同方面支配，形成"双重分离"与非对称动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对可塑性与稳定性的研究多在整模型层面、任务无关地进行，不同设置下结论相互矛盾。作者受认知科学中陈述性记忆与程序性记忆之分的启发，转而考察不同Transformer模块与不同任务类型之间可塑性/稳定性的差异。

Method: 构建可控合成环境以实现清晰测量；按模块（QK、VO、MLP）与任务类型（程序性 vs. 陈述性记忆）交叉分析可塑性；通过针对性的模块重置—恢复实验（reset-and-recovery）解释稳定性差异的来源，并在真实模型与任务上做验证。

Result: 发现双重分离：程序性任务上QK与VO投影最具可塑性，陈述性记忆任务上MLP最具可塑性。稳定性并不镜像可塑性：学习新程序时更新QK可保留过半已存陈述性记忆，更新MLP保留远少，更新VO约损失五分之四。重置实验表明：可塑性取决于模块是否最适配任务的主计算/存储，稳定性则取决于既有能力对该模块的全部依赖方式（包括经由该模块访问其他模块所存信息）。

Conclusion: 可塑性与稳定性依赖模块功能角色的不同侧面，从而提供了一种任务相关、模块级的持续学习视角；在真实模型与任务上亦大致重现该非对称动态。

Abstract: Plasticity and stability in continual learning are typically studied at the entire-model level and in a task-agnostic manner, yet prior work has reported divergent conclusions across settings. Inspired by cognitive distinctions between declarative and procedural memory, we instead study how plasticity and stability vary across transformer modules and task types. In a controlled synthetic environment with clean measurement, we show a double dissociation: query-key (QK) and value-output (VO) projections are the most plastic on procedural tasks, whereas MLPs are the most plastic on declarative memorization. Stability, however, does not simply mirror plasticity: when learning a new procedure, updating QK retains over half of the stored declarative memory, while updating MLPs retains far less and updating VO loses roughly four-fifths. Targeted module reset-and-recovery experiments explain this asymmetry: plasticity tracks which module is best suited to a task's primary computation or storage, whereas stability depends on all ways the existing capability relies on that module, including access to information stored in other modules. Experiments on a realistic model and tasks broadly echo these asymmetric dynamics. Overall, our work shows that plasticity and stability depend on different aspects of a module's functional role, offering a task-dependent, module-level perspective on continual learning.

</details>


### [35] [When Does Subspace Direction Matter for LoRA? Regime Analysis of the Magnitude Principle in Few-Shot Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23428)
*Nischal Subedi,Cencheng Shen,Peng Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoRA适配器初始输入子空间方向的重要性是"regime-dependent"的：当标注数据相对表示维度和激活子空间监督结构稀缺时，方向最关键；作者用尖峰协方差扰动分析与首步分析刻画这一机理，提出对齐系数ρ并以PivotLoRA验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期观点认为谱初始化（spectral initializers）仅通过更新幅度（update magnitude）带来收益。作者要修正这一认识，指出初始方向本身在特定条件下有独立且重要的作用，并刻画该依赖条件。

Method: 理论：尖峰协方差扰动分析刻画估计子空间的有限样本稳定性；LoRA训练首步分析解释子空间与任务对齐为何重要，并导出对齐系数ρ。方法：PivotLoRA——在预测相关位置用激活PCA初始化适配器，并在训练早期施加一次输出保持的正交pivot。

Result: 少样本BERT-base分类中，PivotLoRA在13种方法中平均排名最佳，30个设置中29个优于vanilla LoRA，尽管其输入因子初始化范数更小；ρ紧密追踪分类任务收益。扩展到7B/8B Llama解码器；增益随标注数据增多而衰减；在GSM8K与StrategyQA的completion-only推理上亦有提升。

Conclusion: 明确了初始化方向起作用的边界条件：数据稀缺且初始化子空间捕捉监督任务结构时方向最关键；子空间-任务对齐可作为训练前的低成本诊断指标，为LoRA初始化设计提供理论依据。

Abstract: We show that the importance of a LoRA adapter's initial input-subspace direction is regime-dependent, and we characterize this dependence in theory and experiment. This refines the recent view that spectral initializers help through update magnitude alone. Direction matters most when labeled data are scarce relative to the representation dimension and the activation subspace used for initialization captures supervised task structure. In this regime, the adapter's row span remains sensitive to its starting point, making task-aligned initialization especially useful. A spiked-covariance perturbation analysis characterizes the finite-sample stability of the estimated subspace. A first-step analysis of LoRA training explains why the subspace's alignment with the task matters and motivates an alignment coefficient $ρ$ that closely tracks gains across classification tasks. We test this account with PivotLoRA, which initializes adapters from activation PCA at prediction-relevant positions and applies one output-preserving orthogonal pivot early in training. In few-shot BERT-base classification, PivotLoRA achieves the best average rank among 13 methods and improves over vanilla LoRA in 29 of 30 settings, even though it starts from a smaller input-factor initialization norm. Additional classification experiments extend the evaluation to 7B and 8B Llama decoders. Classification gains diminish with more labeled data, consistent with the proposed regime dependence. A targeted extension to completion-only reasoning also shows improvements on GSM8K and StrategyQA. These findings identify conditions under which initialization direction matters, with subspace-task alignment offering a low-cost diagnostic before training for classification.

</details>


### [36] [Conformally Calibrated Adaptive Computation in Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23440)
*Abdul Qadir Ibrahim,Martin Burger,Abel Maviano Jonathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文以保形校准按输入在固定误差预算下选择傅里叶模态数或通道宽度，发现减少计算量并不必然降低推理时延，并主张用实测覆盖率与完整运行时间对匹配静态基线评价神经算子的自适应计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子中“少算”常被默认为“更快”，但模态截断或通道收窄未必带来可测的时延收益；现有工作缺乏对自适应计算策略的严格误差保障与公平的运行时比较，因此需要澄清计算量、误差预算与端到端延迟之间的差距。

Method: 采用保形校准，在每个输入上于固定误差预算内选择傅里叶模态数或通道宽度。提出两条规则：三角不等式包络，可同时对全部候选给出界；直接打分规则，处理候选误差非单调的情形，并借输入无关得分在同一边际保证下校准静态基线。在冻结模型与可交换性假设下，两规则均给出越界预测概率的上界，并在校准时不可行时选择弃权。实验在 Burgers 与 Darcy 流问题上使用三个训练种子。

Result: 模态选择减少了活跃谱支撑，但无可测延迟下降，因全网格变换与通道混合主导开销；通道收窄确能降低 Darcy 前向成本，但基于参考的选择须先计算稠密预测。仅依赖输入的二元宽度选择器省去该次前向，延迟为稠密的 0.52–0.95 倍；然而同规则校准的静态宽度往往同样快或更快，并在单独生成的 1000 例测试集上总体覆盖率更高，而选择器在过半设置中低于 90% 目标覆盖率。

Conclusion: 减少计算量不等于降低推理时间，自适应选择器的收益可能被参考前向与固定开销抵消。神经算子的自适应计算应以实测覆盖率和相对匹配静态比较器的完整运行时间来评价，而非仅报告计算量节省或名义延迟。

Abstract: Selecting less computation in a neural operator need not reduce inference time. We study this gap using conformal calibration to choose Fourier modes or channel width per input subject to a fixed error budget. A triangle-inequality envelope bounds all candidates simultaneously; a direct-score rule handles non-monotone candidate errors and, with an input-independent score, calibrates a static baseline under the same marginal guarantee. Under frozen-model and exchangeability assumptions, both rules bound the probability of issuing a prediction outside the budget and abstain when calibration is infeasible. On Burgers and Darcy flow across three training seeds, mode selection reduces active spectral support without a measurable latency reduction, because full-grid transforms and channel mixing dominate the cost. Narrowing channels does reduce Darcy forward cost, but reference-based selection must first compute the dense prediction. An input-only binary width selector avoids that pass and runs at 0.52-0.95 times dense latency; yet static widths calibrated under the same rule are often as fast or faster, and on a separately generated 1,000-example test set they generally achieve higher coverage, while the selector falls below its 90% target in more than half of the settings. Adaptive computation in neural operators should therefore be judged by achieved coverage and complete runtime against a matched static comparator.

</details>

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FACT实验框架，揭示推测解码中"更高接受率≠更快生成"的鸿沟，强调训练、候选分配与执行条件需联合评估，并以接受率与端到端延迟双指标衡量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码中，更好的草稿预测与更长的接受延续并不必然带来更快生成，现有评估多关注接受率而忽视分配策略与执行条件，缺乏对三者交互作用的系统性理解。
