Result: 早期步加权训练较均匀加权的detached训练提升接受率15–24%，但rollout代理交叉熵无法可靠地按接受率对drafter排序；置信度引导分配使加速比在HumanEval从1.46×升至2.02×、MT-Bench从1.31×升至1.75×；该收益可迁移至独立训练的drafter，但更强训练并未一致放大分配收益；仅改变宿主线程设置即可在不动接受率的情况下几乎消除加速。

Conclusion: 训练质量与候选分配是两个不完全互补的改进维度，且二者收益均受执行条件制约；因此应在受控执行条件下，同时以接受率和端到端延迟对推测解码进行联合评估。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates language-model generation by verifying multiple draft tokens in a single target-model pass. However, better draft predictions and longer accepted continuations do not necessarily produce faster generation. To examine this gap, we present FACT (Fixed-Budget Allocation and Cross-Step Training), an experimental framework for studying how drafter training, candidate allocation, and execution conditions jointly affect acceptance and latency. Using a lightweight feature-prediction drafter with a frozen Llama-3-8B-Instruct target under greedy decoding, we first examine how training translates into accepted tokens. Backpropagating through predicted-feature rollouts with greater weight on early steps improves acceptance by 15-24% over uniformly weighted detached training, yet average rollout proxy cross-entropy does not reliably rank drafters by acceptance. Beyond prediction quality, the placement of draft candidates also matters: with a fixed 256-node budget and matched per-depth widths, replacing static allocation with confidence-guided allocation increases speedup over autoregressive decoding from $1.46\times$ to $2.02\times$ on HumanEval and from $1.31\times$ to $1.75\times$ on MT-Bench. This allocation benefit extends to an independently trained drafter, but stronger training does not consistently amplify it, showing that the two improvements do not necessarily reinforce each other. Even these speed gains depend on execution conditions: changing only host-thread settings can nearly eliminate speedup without changing acceptance. Together, these findings show why training and allocation must be evaluated jointly using both acceptance and end-to-end latency under controlled execution conditions.

</details>


### [38] [Stability of Parallel Transport Convolution under Riemannian Metric Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23543)
*Hongnian Huang,Andrew Stump*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究平行移动卷积在黎曼度量扰动下的稳定性，证明了在紧致流形上、对连续输入信号与局部支撑的连续参考核，卷积输出在L²意义下连续依赖于C²拓扑下的黎曼度量。核心技术是综合割迹外的统一估计与任意小体积邻域上的积分界，并控制体积测度的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何卷积依赖底流形的几何结构，因此度量扰动下算法输出的稳定性是几何深度学习的基础性问题；平行移动卷积尤其依赖测地线、平行移动与指数映射等几何构造，其稳定性此前缺乏严格分析。

Method: 采用微分几何与分析的方法：建立测地线、平行移动、Jacobi场与指数映射的扰动估计，用于比较扰动前后的传输核；针对割迹导致的全局一致比较障碍，将割迹外的统一估计与任意小体积邻域上的积分界相结合，并估计黎曼体积测度的变化。

Result: 在固定紧致流形上，对连续输入信号与支撑于足够小法邻域内的连续参考核，在给定局部化与传输路径约定下，卷积输出在L²中连续依赖于C²拓扑下的黎曼度量，得到定性稳定性定理。

Conclusion: 该工作将黎曼几何构造的扰动理论与几何深度学习中空间卷积算子的分析联系起来，为平行移动卷积提供稳定性理论依据；但结论为定性稳定性，未给出显式收敛速率或定量误差界。

Abstract: Geometric convolutions on Riemannian manifolds depend on the geometry of the underlying domain, making stability under metric perturbations a fundamental question in geometric deep learning. This paper studies this question for parallel transport convolution through methods from differential geometry and analysis. On a fixed compact manifold, we establish that, for a continuous input signal and a continuous reference kernel supported in a sufficiently small normal neighborhood, the convolution output depends continuously in $L^2$ on the Riemannian metric in the $C^2$-topology, under the specified localization and transport-path conventions. The analysis develops perturbation estimates for geodesics, parallel transport, Jacobi fields, and exponential maps, and uses these estimates to compare the transported kernels. A central difficulty is the cut locus, which obstructs uniform comparison of the transport construction over the entire manifold. We address this difficulty by combining uniform estimates away from the cut locus with integral bounds on neighborhoods of arbitrarily small volume, while also controlling the change in the Riemannian volume measure. The resulting qualitative stability theorem connects perturbation theory for Riemannian geometric constructions with the analysis of spatial convolution operators in geometric deep learning.

</details>


### [39] [Safe to Omit, Worth Omitting? Keeping the Answer and Cutting Latency When an LLM Agent Skips Another's Message](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23570)
*Xinyang Li,Chih-Hsuan Yang,Xiaolong Ma,Anjir Ahmed Chowdhury,Vikram Vasudevan,Rajeev Thakur,Feng Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于接收方置信度与有限样本检验的"消息跳过"机制：在保证至多5%被跳过答案改变的前提下，让接收代理安全且有益地跳过帮助代理的消息（文本或KV cache）。在OpenBookQA、ARC-Challenge、MMLU-Pro、SQuAD、GSM8K及三对Qwen模型上验证，得到四条洞见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多代理系统中接收代理需等待帮助代理的消息，虽可提升表现但引入额外延迟。需要判断何时可以安全且有益地跳过消息：安全性指最终答案极少改变，有益性指节省的时间超过决策成本。同时需厘清"oracle上限"是否真能证明消息有效。

Method: 机制在无消息情况下读取接收代理的置信度，用有限样本统计检验认证一个阈值，使以高概率至多5%的被跳过答案发生改变；若无阈值通过检验则保持通信。评测覆盖OpenBookQA、ARC-Challenge、MMLU-Pro（三对Qwen模型）、跨家族代理、SQuAD与GSM8K，并设置预注册式前瞻测试（规则提前固定）。

Result: 四点发现：(1) oracle上限是弱证据——与答案内容无关的对照输入可复现oracle增益的中位数76%；(2) 可否跳过取决于消息改变答案的频率与可预测性而非其准确率，所有被认证的KV-cache路径都不如单独接收代理准确；(3) 文本消息有效的场景中，跳过保留大部分增益（四类设置下跳过59–80%，保留76–95%增益）；两项前瞻测试中分别跳过39.9%问题（保留12.55/12.80分增益）与68.4%问题（保留全部增益）；(4) 安全性不等于效率：batch size为1时跳过文本可省数百毫秒，跳过KV cache节省甚微。认证可扩展至SQuAD但不适用于GSM8K；11项未见问题测试中被跳过答案改变率的95%上界均低于5%。

Conclusion: 接收代理可在统计认证下安全、有益地跳过帮助代理消息；oracle上限不能作为消息有效性的可靠证据；跳过可行性由答案改变的可预测性决定而非消息准确率；延迟收益取决于消息形式（文本显著、KV cache有限）。该认证框架在多项选择与抽取式任务上成立，但在GSM8K等推理任务上不适用。

Abstract: In multi-agent systems, a receiver agent often waits for a helper agent's message (text or its latent KV cache), which can improve performance but adds latency. We study when a receiver agent can skip the message safely (the final answer rarely changes) and beneficially (the time saved exceeds the cost of deciding). Our mechanism reads the receiver agent's confidence without the message and uses a finite-sample test to certify a threshold at which, with high probability, at most 5% of skipped answers change; if none passes, the system keeps communicating. We test it on OpenBookQA, ARC-Challenge, and MMLU-Pro with three Qwen model pairs, on cross-family agents, and on SQuAD and GSM8K. Four insights emerge. First, oracle headroom is weak evidence that messages help: control inputs without answer-specific content, matched to change answers as often as real messages, reproduce a median 76% of the oracle's gain. Second, whether a message can be skipped depends on how often and how predictably it alters the answer, not on its accuracy; every certified KV-cache path is less accurate than the receiver agent alone. Third, where text messages help, skipping keeps most of their gain: on unseen questions in the four such settings, the policies skip 59 to 80% and keep an estimated 76 to 95% of the gain. In two prospective tests with all rules fixed in advance, our mechanism skips a three-agent system's verifier on 39.9% of sealed questions, keeping 12.55 of its 12.80-point gain, and a 32B helper on 68.4% of held-out questions, keeping its whole gain. Fourth, safety does not determine efficiency: at batch size one, skipping text saves hundreds of milliseconds per query and skipping KV cache far less. Certification extends to SQuAD but not to GSM8K. In eleven tests on unseen questions, every certified policy we could test kept its change rate's 95% upper bound below 5% under our answer parsers.

</details>


### [40] [Efficient Variance-Aware Reinforcement Learning in Time-Inhomogeneous Linear MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23596)
*Qingyue Zhao,Qiwei Di,Jiafan He,Heyang Zhao,Kaixuan Ji,Quanquan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 VA-LSVI-UCB++ 算法，在情节式非时齐线性 MDP 中实现依赖方差的遗憾界 \tilde{O}(d√(H·min{Var*_K, Var^π_K}) + d^5 H^4)，消除先前工作中的 \tilde{O}(K^{1/4}) 多项式加性项，并在确定性环境退化为与 K 无关的 \tilde{O}(d^5 H^4)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性 MDP 的方差感知分析（Li and Sun, 2023; Huang et al., 2023）依赖较粗糙的集中步骤，即使确定性环境仍保留多项式 \tilde{O}(K^{1/4}) 加性项；而线性混合 MDP 已具备 \tilde{O}(1) 确定性情形开销，线性 MDP 的最坏情形近极小极大结果为 \tilde{O}(d√(H^3K) + d^7 H^8)。因此需要一种计算高效、能同时匹配方差自适应与最坏情形最优的算法。

Method: VA-LSVI-UCB++：在 LSVI-UCB 框架上对乐观、悲观与平方值函数估计器同时进行三路 reference-advantage 分解，并结合方差自界定（variance self-bounding）与 Freedman 型值差论证，收紧悲观与二阶矩置信半径达 \tilde{O}(dH) 倍，并消除逆方差下界（inverse-variance-floor）引发的爆炸。

Result: 得到遗憾界 \tilde{O}(d√(H·min{Var*_K, Var^π_K}) + d^5 H^4)：可自适应于最优值函数与所播放策略值函数累计条件方差二者的较小值；恢复最坏情形极小极大主项 \tilde{O}(d√(H^3K))；在确定性环境中退化为 \tilde{O}(d^5 H^4)，无 K 的多项式依赖。

Conclusion: 该工作为线性 MDP 提供了计算高效的方差依赖遗憾分析，弥合了方差自适应界与最坏情形最优界之间的差距，关键贡献在于三路分解与 Freedman 型论证所实现的置信半径精细化。

Abstract: We study variance-dependent reinforcement learning in episodic time-inhomogeneous linear Markov decision processes (MDPs) with feature dimension $d$, horizon $H$, and $K$ episodes. While recent works have established variance-dependent regret with $\tilde{O}(1)$ deterministic-regime overhead for linear mixture MDPs (Zhao et al., 2023) and worst-case nearly minimax optimal regret $\tilde{O}(d\sqrt{H^3 K} + d^7 H^8)$ for linear MDPs (He et al., 2023),, existing variance-aware analyses for linear MDPs (Li and Sun, 2023; Huang et al., 2023) rely on a coarse concentration step whose resulting regret guarantees retain a polynomial $\tilde{O}(K^{1/4})$ additive term even in deterministic environments. We propose VA-LSVI-UCB++, a computationally efficient algorithm that achieves the variance-dependent regret bound $\tilde{O}(d\sqrt{H\min\{\mathrm{Var}_K^*,\mathrm{Var}_K^π\}}+d^5H^4)$, where $\mathrm{Var}_K^*$ and $\mathrm{Var}_K^π$ denote the cumulative conditional variances of the optimal value function and the played policies' value functions along the realized trajectory. Our result adapts to the minimum of the two realized trajectory variances, recovers the worst-case minimax optimal leading term $\tilde{O}(d\sqrt{H^3 K})$, and collapses to $\tilde{O}(d^5 H^4)$ in deterministic environments, free of polynomial-in-$K$ dependency. At the core of our analysis is a simultaneous three-way reference-advantage decomposition for the optimistic, pessimistic, and squared value function estimators coupled with variance self-bounding and Freedman-type value-difference arguments, which tightens the pessimistic and second-moment confidence radii by a factor of $\tilde{O}(d H)$ and removes the inverse-variance-floor blowup.

</details>


### [41] [DuoCL: Dual-Path Online Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23598)
*Jebacyril Arockiaraj,Dhruv Parikh,Rajgopal Kannan,Viktor Prasanna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出双路径在线持续学习框架DuoCL：快速解析路径持续累积每个minibatch的特征统计，慢速路径定期自适应骨干，并在算力受限下优于梯度与解析基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线持续学习中数据到达速率独立于训练速度；算力不足时梯度法更新频率下降、适应性受限，而解析法虽开销低但冻结骨干无法适应分布漂移。

Method: 双路径框架：快速解析路径对每个到达minibatch累积特征统计；慢速路径周期性更新骨干，并按可用算力预算调整采样数量而不中断快路径学习；引入特征对齐以补偿累积分类器统计的漂移。

Result: 四个数据集、三种算力预算下，在线准确率较各设置中最强梯度基线平均提升9.67个百分点；分布漂移下持续优于解析学习，CIFAR-C上最高提升4.00个百分点；在disjoint class-incremental、domain-incremental与Si-Blurry设置中准确率均超过已有方法。

Conclusion: 将解析学习的低开销与骨干的周期性自适应结合，可在算力受限的在线数据流中同时保持快速更新与表征适应性。

Abstract: Online continual learning (OCL) requires learning from a data stream whose arrival rate is independent of model training speed. When training cannot keep pace, computationally expensive gradient-based methods update less frequently, limiting adaptation. Analytic methods reduce this overhead, but their frozen backbones cannot adapt representations to changing distributions. We propose DuoCL, a dual-path OCL framework whose fast analytic path accumulates feature statistics from every incoming minibatch while its slow path periodically adapts the backbone. The slow path adjusts its sample count to the available compute budget without interrupting fast-path learning. Feature alignment compensates for drift in the accumulated classifier statistics. Across four datasets and three computational budgets, DuoCL improves online accuracy by an average of 9.67 percentage points over the strongest gradient-based baseline in each setting. DuoCL consistently outperforms analytic learning under distribution shifts, with gains of up to 4.00 percentage points on CIFAR-C, and achieves higher accuracy than prior methods across disjoint class-incremental, domain-incremental, and Si-Blurry settings.

</details>


### [42] [Entropy-Regularized Integrated Gradients for Stable Feature Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23600)
*Rukmangadh Sai Myana,Sumit Kumar Jha,Yanzhao Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出熵正则化积分梯度（ERIG），用最大熵softmax加权削弱积分路径上的梯度不规则性，在几乎不增加算力的情况下将归因稳定性提升约47倍、定位性能提升约24%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路径型归因方法（如IG）对输入扰动敏感：即使模型预测不变，不可感知扰动也会大幅改变归因图，影响归因的可信度与可复现性。

Method: ERIG在积分路径上用带温度τ的最大熵softmax加权，内在平滑梯度不规则性；借助模型及其梯度的Lipschitz性质，将扰动下的归因变化分解为τ无关的模型响应项与界以O(1/(2τ))下降的权重变化项，其比值R(τ)刻画梯度信息与归因稳定性的权衡；并将经典噪声估计器改造为LINV-C与LINV-P两项归因噪声指标。

Result: 在ImageNet与Oxford-IIIT Pet上、11种CNN/Transformer骨干网络中，ERIG排名保真度指标与次优方法可比，定位提升约24%，归因稳定性平均提升约47倍，相对IG最高达四个数量级，额外计算开销可忽略。

Conclusion: ERIG通过熵正则化加权在保持归因保真度的同时显著提升扰动稳定性与定位质量，兼具理论界与低计算成本，并提供了可量化归因噪声的新评估维度。

Abstract: Path-based attribution methods like Integrated Gradients (IG) are widely used to interpret neural networks, yet imperceptible input perturbations can drastically alter their attribution maps while leaving model predictions unchanged. We introduce Entropy-Regularized Integrated Gradients (ERIG), a path-based attribution method that intrinsically attenuates gradient irregularities along the integration path using maximum-entropy softmax weighting with temperature parameter $τ$. We quantify the effect of the entropy-regularized weighting on attribution stability by using the Lipschitz properties of the model and its gradients. We decompose the attribution change under an input perturbation into a model-response term, whose bound is $τ$-independent, and a weight-variation term, whose bound decreases at rate $\mathcal{O}(1/(2τ))$. Their ratio $R(τ)$ characterizes the trade-off between gradient information and attribution stability. We further add a new dimension to attribution evaluation by adapting classical noise estimators into two metrics, LINV-C and LINV-P, that quantify attribution noise. Across eleven CNN and Transformer backbones on ImageNet and Oxford-IIIT Pet datasets, our experiments show that, relative to the next-best method, ERIG remains comparable on ranking-based fidelity metrics while improving localization by approximately 24% and attribution stability by approximately 47-fold on average, reaching up to four orders of magnitude improvement over IG, all at negligible additional computational cost.

</details>


### [43] [JEPA as a Transferable Optimizer: Learning and Understanding Black-Box Optimization from Reward-Agnostic Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23608)
*Mayukh Debnath,Manan Roy Choudhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出JEPA-PS，一种基于联合嵌入预测架构的学习型优化器，仅需一次前向传播即可从短评估历史中提议候选解，并在15个控制环境中与12种优化器对比，同时分析其依赖的历史信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型优化器多以“是否超越已有方法”为评价标准，缺乏对其从搜索历史中使用了何种信息的互补性探究。

Method: 用参数向量与标量回报在随机rollout上预训练JEPA-PS，其潜在预测目标为轨迹中的下一个点而非回报或梯度；测试时读取短评估历史，单次前向传播输出候选，无需任务描述、奖励塑形或测试时梯度。

Result: 在15个控制环境、12种优化器（进化方法、贝叶斯优化、CMA-ES、LLM优化器）上，与调优的经典基线相当；Swimmer上优于LLM优化器2倍以上且单步成本低71倍；可零样本迁移；零模型对比与解码器训练消融显示潜在表示携带超越简单历史统计的信息，且所有训练型解码器变体均劣于默认冻结表示；历史依赖随任务而变：平滑地形用全轨迹，崎岖地形几乎仅用最优已见点；该记忆需求与FDC弱正相关（r=0.112, n=7），但独立奖励排序敏感性探针未能佐证（r=-0.024, n=7）。

Conclusion: JEPA-PS以低成本单步推理实现有竞争力的黑盒优化，其核心发现是优化器所需的历史记忆量取决于任务地形的光滑性，而非简单地由奖励排序信息决定。

Abstract: Black-box optimization evaluates candidates through simulations, rollouts, or experiments, often without gradients. Learned optimizers are usually judged by whether they outperform existing methods. We ask a complementary question: what information does a learned optimizer use from its search history? We introduce JEPA-PS (JEPA Policy Search), based on a Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture. It is pretrained on random rollouts using parameter vectors and scalar returns. Its latent-prediction objective predicts the next point in an optimization trajectory rather than its reward or gradient, allowing us to examine what was tried and how well it performed. At test time, JEPA-PS reads a short evaluation history and proposes a candidate in one forward pass, without task descriptions, reward shaping, or test-time gradients. We evaluate it on 15 control environments against 12 optimizers, including evolutionary methods, Bayesian optimization, CMA-ES, and an LLM optimizer. JEPA-PS is competitive with tuned classical baselines and outperforms the LLM optimizer by more than 2x on Swimmer at 71x lower per-step cost. It also transfers zero-shot to environments excluded from pretraining. Null-model comparisons and decoder-training ablations indicate that its latent representation carries information beyond simple history statistics; every trained decoder variant performs worse than the default frozen representation. Our main finding is that the history JEPA-PS uses depends on the task. On smooth landscapes it uses the full trajectory, whereas on rugged landscapes the best-seen point carries nearly all the signal. This memory requirement has a weak positive correlation with fitness-distance correlation (FDC; r=0.112, n=7), while an independent reward-ordering sensitivity probe does not corroborate the relationship (r=-0.024, n=7). Code: https://github.com/mdebnath22/jepa-props.

</details>

Abstract: How much the teacher talks and when are among the most informative and costly-to-measure signals of teaching. Yet, classroom audio is among the most sensitive data an AI system can process: recordings capture minors and fall under GDPR, preventing raw audio from leaving the recording device. We present a teacher-student segmentation pipeline designed to meet exactly this constraint: label-free at inference, running on a recording device, and requiring no per-session calibration. The pipeline chains a lightweight voice activity detector, compactly pretrained speaker embeddings, agglomerative clustering, and a small logistic regression that assigns the teacher role based on session-relative structure rather than voice identity. We show that our structural cue transfers across classrooms, languages, and acoustic conditions.
  In leave-one-teacher-out cross-validation on 292 recordings from 63 teachers across English and German corpora, our pipeline achieves teacher F1 of 0.81 and 0.87, respectively, outperforming both enrollment-based baselines and a 30-second supervised enrollment ceiling. On a commodity smartphone, its performance matches pyannote, a strong neural diarization tool, while running 4.8x faster at comparable peak memory at a real-time factor of ~0.18. We also find that transcribing filtered audio only marginally increases teacher's word error rate from $0.26$ to 0.28. Residual failures concentrate where teacher and student voices are not acoustically separable. However, we show how to flag these sessions label-free at inference so that they can be routed to human review, enabling selective, privacy-preserving deployment.

</details>

*George Giapitzakis,Ankur Moitra,Andrej Risteski,Dhruv Rohatgi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 绕过路径空间分析可缓解扩散采样误差累积，且在Poincaré不等式下采样器近乎达到最优总变差误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散采样理论分析通过误差累积刻画整条逆向扩散路径的定律，导致采样误差（以总变差距离衡量）随过程horizon多项式增长，学习误差易复合。

Method: 避开"路径空间"分析，直接分析输出样本的定律误差；在p满足Poincaré不等式时缓解误差累积，并将该结果作为更一般框架的特例：对任意p，将误差度量从总变差距离松弛为不"见证"多模态性的较弱度量。进一步从信息论角度研究给定有界误差的学得score时对p的可辨识精度。

Result: 对满足Poincaré不等式的p，缓解了扩散采样的误差累积；对任意给定Poincaré常数上界，扩散采样器已近乎达到最优的总变差误差。

Conclusion: 绕过路径空间分析可显著改进扩散采样的误差界；在Poincaré条件下扩散采样在总变差意义下接近信息论最优。

Abstract: Diffusion modeling is a popular method of sampling from a complex, high-dimensional distribution $p$ using learned approximations of its (noised) score functions. Generating a single sample requires simulating the reverse diffusion process, which requires many queries to the learned scores. Thus, a priori, the learning errors can easily compound. Indeed, in existing theoretical analyses, the sampling error (as measured in total variation distance to $p$) accumulates polynomially with the horizon of the stochastic process. This is a consequence of bounding the error of the output law by the error of the law of the entire reverse diffusion path.
  In this paper, we demonstrate that by circumventing the "path-space" analysis, the error accumulation of the diffusion sampler can be mitigated when $p$ satisfies a Poincaré inequality. Moreover, we achieve this result as a special case of a broader framework that applies to any distribution $p$, but relaxes the error metric from total variation distance to a weaker metric capturing all sample statistics that do not "witness" the multimodality of $p$.
  We then zoom out from analyzing the diffusion sampler to the information-theoretic question: given a learned score with some error bound, how accurately can we identify $p$? We show that, for any given bound on the Poincaré constant of $p$, the diffusion sampler already nearly achieves optimal total variation error.

</details>

*Shoma Yukawa,Meghna Roychowdhury,Arnab Sanyal,Chengwei Zhou,Shreyas Sen,Gourav Datta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EEG2MCU是一个"感知-执行"协同设计框架，将电极数量作为硬件设计变量，通过任务感知的导联选择与INT8分块生成/多级存储复用，在Cortex-M7上实现高维EEG的高效嵌入式推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴EEG推理不仅受模型规模限制，还受"通道相关的感知前端"制约：高维输入必须在紧凑的下游计算前完成处理。因此需要同时优化传感端的通道（电极）数量与执行端的计算/存储，而非仅压缩模型。

Result: 在Cortex-M7 MCU上，相对基线峰值SRAM降低11.38x（其中62→19电极的导联精简贡献1.71x），延迟降低2.57x，能耗降低2.48x，同时保留全导联精度的94–96%。

Conclusion: 将感知前端（电极数）与执行端（分块生成与存储布局）联合优化，可在严苛的嵌入式资源约束下实现实用的高维EEG推理，并显著降低存储、延迟与能耗而精度损失有限。

Abstract: Wearable EEG inference is constrained not only by model size, but also by the channel-dependent sensing front end that must process high-dimensional inputs before compact downstream computation. We present EEG2MCU, a sensing-to-execution co-design framework that treats electrode count as a hardware design variable through task-aware montage selection. EEG2MCU retains a compact time-frequency representation and generates only the INT8 regions required by each first-layer tile, avoiding full input materialization. Halo reuse and access-aware placement across Flash, SRAM, and tightly coupled memory further reduce redundant generation and data movement. On a Cortex-M7 MCU, EEG2MCU reduces peak SRAM by 11.38x relative to the baseline, including a 1.71x reduction from the 62-to-19 electrode montage, while reducing latency by 2.57x and energy by 2.48x. It retains 94-96% of full-montage accuracy, demonstrating practical high-dimensional EEG inference under tight embedded-resource constraints.

</details>

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个三层基准协议，用于验证智能体检索生成的协变量在随机实验异质性效应分析中的可容许性，区分检索来源泄漏与编码器权重泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体可通过检索快速变化的本地上下文来扩展随机实验的协变量空间，从而刻画谁最能从干预中获益；但这类协变量极难验证：个体处理效应不可观测、检索泄漏可能把处理后期信息带入名义上的处理前摘要、编码器泄漏可能在时间过滤后仍注入处理后期预训练知识。

Method: 在“摘要来源—编码器权重”双通道信息界指导下，设计三层基准协议：(i) 通过处理前日期过滤检验检索可容许性；(ii) 用秩加权平均处理效应（RATE）评估异质性验证；(iii) 用按时间版本化的编码器、未来事实探针与权重扰动检验编码器可容许性。以乌干达青年机会项目为锚，跨11个数据集比较“会话隔离式检索”（沙盒、有界预算的多次智能体运行）与“持久工作区队友”（GrokBot，每研究构建经日期核验的单一语料且对结果盲）两种流程。

Result: 工作区队友在11个数据集中有6个（纯文本）和7个（组合特征）取得更高平均RATE比值，但配对检验显示无任一流程普遍占优。即使固定检索文本，编码器选择也显著改变异质性信号。时间版本化探针未发现明确的权重泄漏证据，但单记录适配器扰动分析显示，已记忆的未来事实一旦存在便持续保留，直至注入噪声使模型输出退化。

Conclusion: 检索的时间过滤是必要但不充分的：验证智能体生成的协变量需同时考察来源出处、样本外处理效应信号与编码器权重。

Abstract: Agents can expand the covariate space of randomized experiments by retrieving fast-moving local context missed by slow-changing survey baseline covariates, helping characterize who benefits most from an intervention. Verifying such covariates is difficult: individual treatment effects are unobserved, retrieval leakage can pull post-treatment information into nominally pretreatment summaries, and encoder leakage can inject post-treatment pretraining knowledge into representations even after temporal filtering of inputs. Guided by a two-channel information bound separating summary provenance from encoder weights, we propose a three-layer benchmark protocol: (i) retrieval admissibility via pre-treatment date filtering; (ii) heterogeneity validation scored rank-weighted average treatment effects (RATEs); and (iii) encoder admissibility checked with chronologically vintaged encoders, future-fact probes, and weight perturbations. Across 11 datasets anchored by Uganda's Youth Opportunities Program, we compare session-isolated retrieval (sandboxed, bounded-budget agent runs per study) against a persistent workspace teammate (xAI's GrokBot) that builds one date-verified corpus per study while blinded to outcomes. The workspace teammate yields higher mean RATE ratios in 6/11 datasets for text alone and 7 for combined features, though paired tests show neither workflow universally dominates. Even on fixed retrieved text, encoder choice significantly shifts heterogeneity signal. Chronologically vintaged probes show no clear evidence of weight leakage, but single-record adapter perturbation analyses show that memorized future facts, once present, persist until injected noise degrades model output. Temporal filtering of retrieval is necessary but insufficient: verifying agent-generated covariates requires jointly examining source provenance, out-of-sample treatment-effect signal, and encoder weights.

</details>


### [48] [What Interpolators Store and What They Express: An Information-Theoretic Framework for Benign Overfitting and Label-Free Model Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23630)
*Nikolaos Nikolaou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出信息论框架，将插值学习器对样本的处理分为"存储"与"表达"两部分，证明所有插值器的存储量恒为 nH(Y|X)，无法区分，而表达信息 V 可上下界超额风险并解释良性过拟合，进而给出无需验证标签的模型选择准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过参数化模型能完美拟合噪声标签却仍泛化良好，经典复杂度度量与信息论泛化界（含 CMI 界）均无法解释这一现象，需要新的理论工具区分不同插值学习器。

Method: 建立信息论分解：存储（模型保留的训练标签信息）与表达（在新输入处到达预测的样本信息）。先刻画理想模型——对损失 l 的最小复杂度 l-充分表示即 Bayes act，其熵为最小充分复杂度（推广并修正 boosting 的"无损最大压缩"观点）；再证明插值器存储恰为 nH(Y|X) 比特；最后用表达信息 V（训练样本上预测的熵）加模态偏置项给出超额风险的上下界。

Result: 存储与基于存储的界（含 CMI）无法区分插值器；表达信息 V 可精确（二分类情形）上下界超额风险；良性过拟合等价于 V 趋于零，统一涵盖最近邻、平均、最小范数最小二乘与双下降，并把 V 与偏差-方差分解、模型分歧联系起来；在 15 个噪声数据集上，用两个不相交半样本估计的 V 在选择遗憾上比同成本留出验证低 60%，以四分之一成本达到 5 折交叉验证效果，同成本下优于按条件互信息选择。

Conclusion: 表达信息 V 是刻画插值学习器泛化的关键量，兼具理论解释力与实用价值：无需留出标签即可做模型选择，显著优于传统验证与 CMI 准则；文中亦刻画了其失效情形并给出补救措施。

Abstract: Overparameterized learners can fit noisy training labels perfectly and still predict well, which neither classical complexity measures nor information-theoretic generalization bounds explain. We develop an information-theoretic framework that separates two things an interpolating learner does with its sample: storage, the information the trained model retains about its training labels, and expression, the information about the sample that reaches its predictions at fresh inputs. We first characterize the ideal model: for a loss l, the least complex l-sufficient representation is the Bayes act, whose entropy is the minimal sufficient complexity; this generalizes, and corrects, an earlier "lossless maximal compression" view of boosting. We then show that every interpolator stores exactly nH(Y|X) bits of label noise, so storage, and bounds built on it (including CMI bounds), cannot tell interpolators apart. Expression can: excess risk is bounded above and below by the expressed information V, the entropy over training samples of the prediction at an input, plus a modal-bias term, exactly so for binary labels. Benign overfitting is therefore vanishing expression, a picture that covers nearest-neighbour rules, averaging, minimum-norm least squares and double descent, and that links V to classical bias-variance decompositions and to model disagreement. Finally, V gives a model-selection criterion that needs no held-out labels. Among overparameterized learners on 15 noisy datasets, V estimated from two disjoint halves has 60% less regret than holdout validation at the same cost and matches 5-fold cross-validation at a quarter of its cost; at matched cost it also beats selection by conditional mutual information. We characterize when it fails and give remedies. Code: https://github.com/nnikolaou/GLMC

</details>


### [49] [FairPGT: Partitioned Graph Transformers for Dual-Level Fair Graph Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23631)
*Siamak Ghodsi,Amjad Seyedi,Sahar Tahmasebi,Eirini Ntoutsi,Sören Auer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 FairPGT，一种基于 METIS 分区的图 Transformer，通过路由 token 连接各分区以降低注意力二次开销，并在聚类分配层（互信息项）与注意力层（归一化跨组注意力比 nCGAR + 自蒸馏）双重约束公平性，实现公平属性图聚类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 属性图聚类在社区与受保护群体重合时会产生人口统计学偏差；图 Transformer 的同组注意力会放大该偏差，而全局注意力又限制可扩展性，故需同时兼顾效率与公平。

Method: 1) 将注意力限制在 METIS 分区内，并以路由 token 跨区连接，把注意力的二次复杂度按分区数分摊；2) 采用图感知的自训练目标引导聚类，并证明其与标准 DEC 目标的偏差有界；3) 分配层：互信息项促进聚类分配的人口统计均等；4) 注意力层：用归一化跨组注意力比（nCGAR）经自蒸馏引导注意力趋于群体中立；5) 理论证明 nCGAR 修正使注意力趋向中立，并给出防止过冲的条件。

Result: 在 7 个基准上，FairPGT 在三个最大图（Credit、Pokec-n、Pokec-z）上取得最佳聚类性能与统计均等性，统计均等差距约为 DMoN 的十分之一，且仅在一个数据集上被帕累托支配；在受控随机块模型上，当社区与受保护群体完全重合时，NMI 达到推导的精确公平聚类上界的 97%。

Conclusion: 强对齐条件下，仅做分配层公平会使注意力变得群体异配，而仅做注意力层公平可提升中立性但不足以保证聚类公平，说明两层公平约束互补且必要；代码已开源。

Abstract: Clustering on attributed graphs can produce demographically biased clusters, particularly when communities align with protected groups. Graph transformers can amplify this bias through same-group attention, while global attention limits scalability. We propose FairPGT, a partitioned graph transformer for fair graph clustering. FairPGT restricts attention to METIS partitions and connects them through routing tokens, which divides the quadratic cost of attention by the number of partitions. A graph-aware self-training target guides clustering, with a provably bounded deviation from the standard DEC target. Fairness is imposed at two levels: a mutual-information term promotes demographic parity in cluster assignments, while a normalized cross-group attention ratio (nCGAR) guides attention toward group neutrality through self-distillation. We prove that the nCGAR correction moves attention toward neutrality and establish a condition preventing overshooting. Across seven benchmarks, FairPGT achieves the best clustering performance and statistical parity on the three largest graphs (Credit, Pokec-n, Pokec-z), with a statistical-parity gap approximately ten times lower than DMoN, and is Pareto-dominated on only one dataset. On controlled stochastic block models, FairPGT attains 97% of the derived upper bound on NMI for exactly fair clustering when communities and protected groups coincide. These graphs further show that, under strong alignment, assignment-level fairness alone makes the attention group-heterophilous, whereas attention-level fairness alone improves neutrality without ensuring fair clusters. Code for reproducibility is available at https://github.com/SiamakGhodsi/FairPGT.

</details>


### [50] [Toaster: Nonlinear Memory from Cascaded Linear Memories and Target Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23648)
*Zeman Li,Ali Behrouz,Yuan Deng,Praneeth Kacham,Rami Al-Rfou,Meisam Razaviyayn,Vahab Mirrokni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Toaster 通过"目标传播"把两层非线性记忆拆成两个可用 delta-rule 核的线性记忆，兼顾深度记忆的表达力与线性记忆的训练效率，在 0.2B–1.6B 规模上全面优于 KDA、Titans 并匹配注意力基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性循环记忆（DeltaNet、KDA）每 token 一次 delta-rule 更新、整块乘积具精确 WY 形式因而训练快，但只能存一个矩阵；深度记忆（TTT-MLP、Titans）存小型 MLP、表达力更强，却无 WY 形式、训练慢。作者希望弥合二者：既获得非线性记忆的容量，又保留 delta-rule 核的高效训练。

Method: 基于"线性层的一次梯度步即一次 delta-rule 写入"这一事实，用两个线性记忆夹一层激活构造两层非线性记忆。用目标传播把输出误差经第二层记忆反投影，为隐层记忆提供目标值；每块只近似一次跨层信号，之后各层均可在未修改的 delta-rule 核上运行。默认仅对输出层跑该核、隐层每块更新一次；消融中两层都跑效果更佳。

Result: 在匹配骨干与训练 token 预算下，0.2B–1.6B 五个规模在 C4、PG19 上损失均低于 KDA；C4 困惑度在各规模匹配或略优于注意力基线；1.2B 时低于 Titans。与全局注意力的 3:1 混合中困惑度最低（C4 低 0.18、PG19 低 0.44）。最大规模 13 任务三样本平均准确率 51.84%（KDA 50.52%、Titans 50.04%）。长度外推至 20K（5 倍训练长度）仍优于 KDA。145M 骨干上，与 Titans 记忆宽度匹配时训练步时不超过 KDA 的 1.33 倍，32K 上下文下比测试的 Titans 配置快 2.8–5.5 倍。

Conclusion: Toaster 以"线性记忆 + 目标传播"组合出可高效训练的非线性循环记忆，在质量上稳定超越 KDA/Titans 并对齐注意力基线，同时保持接近线性记忆的训练开销，为深度记忆的实用化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Recurrent memories let language models process long sequences with a fixed-size state. Linear memories such as DeltaNet and KDA store one matrix and take one delta-rule step per token. The product of a chunk of such steps has an exact WY form, so they train fast. Deep memories such as TTT-MLP and Titans store a small MLP, which can hold maps that no matrix can, but their update has no WY form. Toaster bridges the two by building on a known fact: a gradient step on a linear layer is a delta-rule write. It builds a two-layer nonlinear memory from two linear memories with an activation between them. We use target propagation to give the hidden memory its values by projecting the output error back through the second memory. Given a chunk's cross-layer signals, approximated once per chunk, each layer can then run on an unmodified delta-rule kernel. Our default runs this kernel on the output layer and updates the hidden layer once per chunk. Running it on both layers gives a better model in our ablation. At matched backbones and training-token budgets, Toaster has lower loss than KDA at all five tested sizes (0.2B-1.6B non-embedding parameters) on both C4 and PG19. Its C4 perplexity also matches or slightly improves on the attention baseline at every size. At 1.2B it is also below Titans. In a 3:1 hybrid with global attention, the Toaster hybrid has the lowest perplexity of the four hybrids tested, and it is 0.18 (C4) and 0.44 (PG19) below the attention baseline. At the largest size, Toaster's mean three-shot accuracy on 13 tasks is 51.84%, compared with 50.52% for KDA and 50.04% for Titans. In length extrapolation, Toaster's loss stays below KDA's up to 20K tokens (5x the training length). On the 145M backbone, at a hidden width matched to Titans' memory, Toaster takes at most 1.33x KDA's training-step time and is 2.8-5.5x faster than the tested Titans configurations at 32K context.

</details>


### [51] [Communicative On-Policy Distillation: Distilling Black-Box Teacher Capabilities through Communication for Long-Horizon Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23673)
*Lirui Luo,Kelong Mao,Xinwei Yang,Rongqing Li,Luyu Chen,Kieran Wong,Yudong Guo,Xinrui Wang,Jiayin Zhu,Gu Simiu,Sulong Xu,Cong Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出COPD（通信式在线策略蒸馏），用自然语言通信替代教师logits，把黑箱强模型能力蒸馏进小模型学生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）依赖教师logits，而纯文本黑箱模型不暴露logits，导致该方法无法用于此类教师；需寻找替代蒸馏信号。

Method: 以自然语言通信为蒸馏媒介：学生在任务执行中自主决定何时咨询黑箱教师，并用开放式自然语言提问（无预设模板与干预时间表）；将成功的学生-教师交互保留为特权参考，通过在线策略自蒸馏监督新的无顾问学生rollout，并与任务奖励强化学习联合优化。

Result: 在CAMG-II上取得最强黑箱OPD基线收益的2.46倍；所学通信能力可泛化到Terminal-Bench 2.1与SWE-Bench Pro V2，在无顾问访问条件下提升任务成功率。

Conclusion: 自然语言通信可作为黑箱教师蒸馏的有效载体，且经通信获得的能力可脱离顾问独立迁移。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) is a powerful paradigm for improving language-model agents on long-horizon tasks, yet its conventional formulation relies on teacher logits that text-only black- box models do not expose. To address this limitation, we introduce Communicative On-Policy Distillation (COPD), which uses natural-language communication as a medium for distilling a stronger model's capabilities into a smaller student. During task execution, the student au- tonomously decides when to consult a black-box teacher and formulates open-ended natural- language requests without predefined question templates or intervention schedules. Successful student-teacher interactions are retained as privileged references to supervise fresh adviser-free student rollouts through on-policy self-distillation, jointly with task-reward reinforcement learning. On CAMG-II, COPD achieves 2.46 times the gain of the strongest black-box OPD baseline. The capabilities it learns through communication generalize to Terminal-Bench 2.1 and SWE-Bench Pro V2, improving task success without adviser access.

</details>


### [52] [Simpler Tiny Recursive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23677)
*Torben Glass,Zhengxi Zhang,Paul Swoboda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STRM，用单状态、均匀更新的简化递归取代TRM/HRM复杂的双层嵌套递归，在不牺牲泛化的前提下提升训练速度与最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TRM（HRM的简化版）虽在数独、迷宫最短路等谜题任务上效果显著，但其递归结构复杂：含高层与低层两个状态、嵌套循环递归，且随所处循环位置不同而使用不同输入输出签名的网络调用；这些机制主要用于避免记忆与过拟合。作者希望用更简单的递归实现同等或更好的泛化。

Method: STRM采用单一状态和统一更新的递归结构。关键设计包括：(1) 对状态使用不同速率的指数移动更新（exponential moving updates），并在训练中对速率加入抖动（jitter）；(2) 仅在慢更新通道（slow-updating channels）上读取解。实验设置与TRM保持一致的数据、参数量与计算量。

Result: 在Sudoku-Extreme上较TRM提升3.8分，PPBench提升8.4分，Maze-Hard持平；在ARC-AGI-1与ARC-AGI-2上相较已发表TRM结果分别提升5.4分与0.5分。

Conclusion: 简化的单状态递归设计STRM足以保持泛化能力，同时加快训练收敛并改善最终表现，说明TRM中复杂的嵌套双状态机制并非必需。代码将在论文录用后开源。

Abstract: Small models that execute deep looped computational graphs have shown great experimental results on puzzle-like problems like Sudoku, finding shortest paths through mazes, etc. The most well-known one is TRM (tiny recursive model), a simplification of HRM (hierarchical reasoning model). However, the underlying recursion is complicated and involves a high-level and a low-level state, a nested loop recursion, and the network calls with different input and output signatures depending on where we are in the loops. These mechanisms are needed to avoid memorization and overfitting. We propose STRM, a simplified design with a single state and a uniform update. STRM does not sacrifice generalization, improves training speed and final performance. Our key architectural choices that allow our simpler recursion to work are exponential moving updates on the states with varying rates that are jittered during training and readout of the solution on slow-updating channels only. We test STRM on Sudoku-Extreme, PPBench and Maze-Hard with the same data, parameters and compute as TRM: we improve over TRM by $3.8$ points on Sudoku-Extreme and $8.4$ points on PPBench and match it on Maze-Hard. On ARC-AGI-1 and ARC-AGI-2, STRM improves over the published TRM results by $5.4$ and $0.5$ points. We will release our code upon acceptance of the paper.

</details>


### [53] [Variable Length Diffuse Everything: Arbitrary Length Generative Modeling on Arbitrary State Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23684)
*Kevin Rojas,Felix X. -F. Ye,Molei Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VL-DE 提出一种无需填充的变长多模态生成框架：借助插入式机制与可加型生成器的时间依赖处理，在任意状态空间上得到可计算的训练目标与高阶采样器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散/流模型多假设数据维度或长度固定；布局生成等真实任务涉及变长多模态数据（如连续框坐标+离散类型）。已有方法要么填充到固定长度，要么引入专门目标显式建模长度，增加复杂度且限制通用性。

Method: 提出 VL-DE（Variable Length Diffuse Everything）通用框架，在任意状态空间上实现变长多模态生成。以插入式（insertion-based）机制替代填充，并建立在有原则的理论框架上以获得可处理的训练目标；利用其生成器具有可加形式（additive form）的特性，恰当处理时间依赖，设计高阶采样器。

Result: 在布局生成与分子生成任务上的大量实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: VL-DE 以简洁统一的框架解决了变长多模态生成问题，避免了填充与额外长度目标带来的复杂性，并可通过可加结构获得更优采样，具备较好的通用性。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow-based generative models have achieved remarkable results across diverse applications, but most assume data of fixed-dimension/length. Meanwhile, real-world problems such as layout generation often involve variable-length, multimodal data, such as continuous bounding box coordinates paired with discrete types. Existing methods typically address variable length by padding data to a fixed size or by introducing specialized objectives to model lengths explicitly, adding complexity and limiting generality. We propose Variable Length Diffuse Everything (VL-DE), a simple and general framework for variable-length multimodal generation on arbitrary state spaces. Instead of padding, VL-DE handles variable length through an insertion-based mechanism grounded in a principled theoretical framework, yielding a tractable training objective. In addition, by utilizing the fact that VL-DE is based on a generator with an additive form, we develop a higher-order sampler by appropriately handling the time dependence. Extensive experiments on layout and molecule generation demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [54] [Learning through Discernment: when a small language model learns from user feedback on the job, and when it decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23716)
*Sirisilp Kongsilp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究小型端侧模型通过LoRA在夜间从自身尝试中持续学习,比较真实结果、用户表态与模型自评三种反馈信号,并引入独立的"反馈感"判别器。结果显示:仅当判别器能剔除失败样本且用户可观测结果时,用户反馈学习才有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧小模型有望通过夜间从日常工作中自我学习而持续改进,但缺乏真实结果时,必须由某个机制决定哪些尝试值得学习;而模型自评和用户反应都可能出错,因此需要系统考察不同反馈信号对持续学习的影响。

Method: 采用Qwen3 0.6B与1.7B模型加LoRA适配器,每晚在被判别器接受的尝试上微调,在WebShop与τ²-bench上模拟最多一年的127个"life"。对比真实结果、用户明示判定、模型自评,以及独立的冻结"feedback sense"(三个版本)读取用户反应的效果。

Result: 自评:先提升后衰退,与适配器无关。误把礼貌性放弃当成功的sense在5个life中崩溃3个;以"先判断用户是否给出明确判定"为设计的分辨型sense在4/5个life中两周内接近oracle,随后因干净但稀少的教训而衰减,至第60天退化到自评水平。在用户无法看到结果的τ²-bench上,sense能正确读取反应,但80%的教训实为被客户致谢的失败。97个life中,失败尝试在教训中的占比可预测结果(Spearman ρ = -0.81)。

Conclusion: 实验中,从用户处学习仅在判别器能排除失败、且用户可观测真实结果时成立;偏斜的教训数据还可能使模型能力收窄。

Abstract: Small language models on devices could keep improving by learning, overnight, from their own daily work. Without a true outcome, however, something must decide which attempts are worth learning from, and both the model's own judgment and the user's reaction can be wrong. We study a small model (Qwen3 0.6B and 1.7B) with a LoRA adapter, fine-tuned each night on the attempts a judge accepts, over 127 lives of up to a year on WebShop and $τ^2$-bench. We compare the true outcome, the user's stated verdict and the model's own judgment with a separate, frozen "feedback sense" that reads the user's reaction, built in three versions. Judging itself, the model improved and then decayed, over a year and whatever its adapter. A sense that took polite give-ups for success collapsed 3 of 5 lives; one built for discernment, which first asks whether the user gave a clear verdict, kept 4 of 5 near the oracle for two weeks, then faded on clean but scarce lessons, to the level of self-judging by day 60. When the user cannot see the outcome ($τ^2$-bench), the sense read reactions correctly, yet 80% of its lessons were failures the customer had thanked. Across 97 lives, the share of failed attempts among the lessons predicted the outcome (Spearman $ρ$ = -0.81). In our experiments, learning from users held up only where the judge kept failures out and the user could see the outcome; lopsided lessons may also narrow the model.

</details>


### [55] [HypoLoop: Revising Weight-Editing Plans from Scientific Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23718)
*Mengran Li,Xichen Yan,Bo Li,Chengyang Zhang,Jinfeng Xu,Zheng Lin,Zelin Zang,Zhen Lei,Jiebo Luo,Victor C. M. Leung,Stan Z. Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HypoLoop 提出一类原生权重编辑方法，将“改哪些参数、由哪些误差驱动修正、用什么拟合规则”等选择与实验反馈闭环连接，并在22个科学任务上验证以实验证据重塑更新问题本身的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学实验持续暴露预测误差，但有效更新取决于哪些参数被改变、哪些误差驱动修正。现有更新方法通常将这些选择固定，未与实验反馈联动，因此需要把更新问题本身也交给实验证据来决定。

Method: 构建 HypoLoop 原生权重编辑方法族：由固定、数值自适应、LLM 引导三类策略生成编辑计划；数值求解器计算权重补丁；内部验证选择部署的预测器；并利用连续观测与验证记录迭代修订可编辑位置与拟合规则。实验覆盖22个科学任务，并在 DeepSeek 与 Claude 两个模型上验证。

Result: Burgers 任务上，在参数预算匹配条件下重分配可编辑位置，误差较固定位置分别降低 58.80% 与 47.72%；DIODE 上采用正残差加权，在保持同样33个可编辑坐标时内部交叉验证 AbsRel 降低 13.35%；多轮修订完整计划相较固定初始计划最终 AbsRel 再降 10.59%；SpaceNet7 与 Make3D 显示被接受的编辑可改善空间预测。

Conclusion: 实验证据能够塑造更新问题本身——可编辑位置、误差加权与拟合规则的选择均值得由反馈动态决定，从而在同等参数预算下获得显著改进，并具备跨任务与跨模型的泛化性。

Abstract: Scientific experiments continually reveal prediction errors. Effective updates depend on which parameters change and which errors drive the correction. We introduce HypoLoop, a family of native weight-editing methods that connects these choices to experimental feedback. Fixed, numerically adaptive, and LLM-guided policies produce editing plans; numerical solvers compute weight patches, and internal validation selects the deployed predictor. Successive observations and validation records support revisions to editable locations and fitting rules. Experiments cover 22 scientific tasks and both DeepSeek and Claude. On Burgers, reallocating a matched parameter budget reduces error by 58.80% and 47.72% relative to fixed locations. On DIODE, positive-residual weighting reduces absolute relative error (AbsRel) by 13.35% in internal cross-validation, with the same 33 editable coordinates. Revising the complete plan across rounds lowers final AbsRel by 10.59% relative to keeping the initial plan fixed. SpaceNet7 and Make3D connect accepted edits to improved spatial predictions. These results show how experimental evidence can shape the update problem itself. Code: https://github.com/limengran98/HypoLoop.

</details>


### [56] [Toward Out-of-Distribution Generalization in Neural AC Power Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23729)
*Ojas Sanghi,Anvita Bhagavathula,Alvaro Carbonero,Priya L. Donti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 系统评估物理约束损失、多电网训练与扰动式数据增强对三种GNN潮流模型从小电网到未见500节点电网零样本迁移的影响：物理约束损失优于纯监督目标；小电网多电网训练仅与最佳单电网持平；边属性扰动增强可提升迁移最多54.6%；随机线路/发电机丢���无稳定收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可再生能源、极端天气与数据中心负荷使电力系统运行不确定性上升，传统潮流求解器在大规模下可靠性受限；机器学习代理模型受制于大电网训练数据生成成本过高，因此分布外泛化（尤其小电网训练→大电网部署的零样本迁移）成为关键，而现有小电网模型缺乏该能力。

Method: 以三种基于图神经网络的潮流模型为对象，在较小系统上训练并零样本迁移到未见过的500节点系统，系统对比三类干预：物理约束（physics-informed）损失、多电网联合训练、基于扰动的数据增强（边属性扰动、随机线路/发电机丢弃）。

Result: (1) 物理约束损失相比纯监督目标提升迁移性能；(2) 小电网上的多电网训练可与最佳单电网训练持平，但无法缩小与目标电网直接训练之间的差距；(3) 边属性扰动增强将迁移性能提升最多54.6%；(4) 随机线路/发电机丢弃不能稳定带来性能提升。

Conclusion: 物理约束与针对性数据增强是提升跨规模零样本迁移的有效手段，而多电网训练与结构随机丢弃收益有限；大规模电网部署需继续缩小与目标电网训练的差距。

Abstract: Growing uncertainty in power system operations from renewables integration, extreme weather, and data center loads challenges the reliability of traditional power flow solvers at scale. Machine learning-based proxies are a potentially promising alternative, but the prohibitive cost of generating training data for large grids makes out-of-distribution generalization a key capability for deployment; currently, models trained on small grids lack that capability. In this work, we systematically evaluate how physics-informed losses, multi-grid training, and perturbation-based data augmentation improve the zero-shot transfer of three graph neural network-based power flow models from smaller systems to a larger, unseen 500-bus system. We find that (1) physics-informed losses improve transfer relative to purely supervised objectives; (2) multi-grid training on smaller systems matches the best single grid but does not close the gap to target-grid training; (3) perturbation-based augmentation on edge attributes improves transfer by up to 54.6%; and (4) random line/generator dropout does not consistently improve performance.

</details>


### [57] [Same Rank, Different Needs: Rethinking LoRA Redistribution in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23742)
*Jinqian Chen,Jihua Zhu,Chang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RASA即插即用框架，用接收端加权响应的无中心PCA替代谱截断，使聚合LoRA更新在接收方秩约束下全局最优地保留响应能量，并配合谱参考聚合去除个性化初始化偏移，在多数据集上显著提升异构联邦LoRA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦LoRA中，将聚合更新下发到各客户端的秩预算内时，现有方法保留其主奇异方向。但最能重构更新矩阵的方向未必最能保留该客户端输入上的响应，低能量方向对特定接收方可能更重要。作者形式化该错配，并证明矩阵最优截断相对于同秩的另一读出可产生任意更大的响应误差。

Method: RASA利用紧凑的对角输入二阶矩，对接收端加权的更新响应执行无中心PCA，得到对秩约束接收端目标全局最优的读出，并在对角度量下保证保留的响应能量不低于谱截断。同时提出谱参考聚合，剔除个性化初始化偏移、保留本地学习增量。RASA为即插即用框架，可配合多种联邦LoRA方法。

Result: 在DomainNet-100、NICO++、GLUE与SuperNI上，跨资源、任务与数据异构性均有效：配合FlexLoRA在DomainNet-100上提升3.23个百分点；SuperNI已见任务ROUGE-L最高提升8.33，未见任务提升4.37，开销适度。

Conclusion: 接收端感知的子空间读出优于矩阵最优谱截断，能更有效地传递知识；RASA作为通用插件在异构联邦微调中稳定增益且开销可控。

Abstract: Federated low-rank adaptation (LoRA) enables clients with different resource budgets to collaboratively fine-tune foundation models. To distribute an aggregated update within each client's rank budget, existing methods commonly retain its leading singular directions. However, directions that best reconstruct the update matrix need not best preserve its responses on a particular client's inputs: a lower-energy direction may matter more to that recipient. We formalize this mismatch and show that matrix-optimal truncation can incur arbitrarily larger response error than another readout of the same rank. Motivated by this result, we propose Receiver-Aware Subspace Adaptation (RASA), a plug-in framework for receiver-aware knowledge transfer. RASA uses compact diagonal input second moments to perform uncentered PCA of receiver-weighted update responses. The resulting readout is globally optimal for the rank-constrained receiver objective and preserves at least as much response energy as spectral truncation under the diagonal metric. RASA also introduces spectral-reference aggregation, which removes the personalized initialization offset while retaining the locally learned increment. Experiments on DomainNet-100, NICO++, GLUE, and SuperNI demonstrate RASA's effectiveness across methods under resource, task, and data heterogeneity. In particular, RASA improves DomainNet-100 accuracy by 3.23 percentage points with FlexLoRA and achieves gains of up to 8.33 ROUGE-L points on seen SuperNI tasks and 4.37 on unseen tasks with modest overhead.

</details>


### [58] [Interpreting Timestamp Permutations in Dynamic Link Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23753)
*Ilia Karpov,Kirill Koloskov,Valeriy Kuleshov,Dmitry Ilvovsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将"时间戳置换带来的性能差"分解为训练历史效应与评估历史效应，并引入"输入暴露"度量，证明TCL在MOOC上的8分差距主要来自训练条件而非评估历史。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作用时间戳置换检验动态链路预测器是否利用时间信息，但单一性能差无法区分训练历史改变、评估历史改变或两者共同作用，导致结论不可解释。

Method: 提出交叉式诊断设计（crossed diagnostic），分别操控训练历史与评估历史以分离两种效应；同时度量输入暴露（input exposure），检验历史改变是否真正改变了预测器的输入；并以固定权重下改变历史、抑制显式时间编码、部分置换等对照实验加以验证。

Result: MOOC上TCL（二元交叉熵训练）在任一评估历史下，原始时间顺序训练相比置换训练约提升8个成对准确率点，而固定权重下改历史仅0.03点；该小历史对比源于68.37%查询输入未变、其余多数排序稳定以及得失抵消。原始评估历史下抑制显式时间编码使训练对比从8.03降至3.64点，配对减少4.39点（95%区间[3.68,5.09]，六种子）。DyGFormer训练与历史对比分别为22.34与0.38点（两种子），尽管其输入暴露更大。

Conclusion: 交叉诊断可将单一置换差距转化为关于训练条件与模型响应的可解释陈述，但未揭示时间学习的内部机制。

Abstract: Timestamp permutations are used to test whether dynamic link predictors exploit temporal information, but a performance gap can reflect changes in training, evaluation history, or both. We separate these effects with a crossed diagnostic and measure input exposure: whether altered histories actually change the predictor's inputs. On MOOC, TCL trained with binary cross-entropy gains approximately eight pairwise-accuracy points from original-chronology training under either evaluation history, while changing history at fixed weights gives only 0.03 points. The small history contrast reflects unchanged inputs for 68.37% of queries, stable rankings for most others, and cancellation of gains and losses. Under original evaluation history, suppressing explicit time encoding reduces the training contrast from 8.03 to 3.64 points, a paired reduction of 4.39 points (95% interval [3.68, 5.09]) across six seeds. DyGFormer shows corresponding training and history contrasts of 22.34 and 0.38 points across two seeds, despite greater exposure. Partial history permutations further separate nominal intervention strength, input changes, and aggregate accuracy. These comparisons turn a single permutation gap into interpretable statements about training conditions and model responses, while leaving the internal mechanisms of temporal learning open.

</details>


### [59] [When Normal-Only Updates Are Not Enough: Test-Time Adaptation in Reconstruction-Based Time-Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23761)
*Jianhuan Mao,Mengxiao Zhu,Yue Pei,Lei Li,Haogang Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文通过受控干预实验验证：即使TTA仅使用基准中标注为正常的样本进行在线更新，也并不能可靠提升时间序列异常检测性能，表明改进样本选择不足以解决基于重建的TSAD的TTA问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署后正常运行条件会漂移，需对时间序列异常检测器做测试时自适应（TTA）；但同一数据流可能含异常，故在线更新前需决定哪些测试样本可用于自适应。作者由此提出更基本的问题：即便更新严格限制在基准标注为正常的样本上，TTA是否仍能可靠改善异常检测？

Method: 采用受控干预（controlled intervention）：将实用的更新选择器替换为仅使用基准中标注为"正常"样本的规则（oracle式选择），其余在线自适应流程保持不变。实验覆盖9类基准数据集族、4种优化器配置、5个随机种子（共180种设置），并辅以替代TTA变体与基准标签的轻度扰动检验。

Result: 三点主要发现：(1) 仅用基准标注正常样本进行自适应并不能可靠提升检测效果；(2) 实用TTA失败在干预后呈两种不同响应——180种设置中63种恢复出明显检测增益，90种仍无明显增益；(3) 更新在各设置下确实降低了即时重建目标，但这种降低并未稳定转化为更好的异常排序能力。两种失败模式在替代自适应变体及轻微标签扰动下均持续存在。

Conclusion: 仅改进样本选择并非基于重建的时间序列异常检测TTA的完整解决方案；即便具有理想化的正常样本筛选，重建目标的即时下降也不等价于检测性能提升，需从自适应目标/机制层面重新审视TTA设计。

Abstract: Normal operating conditions can evolve after deployment, motivating test-time adaptation (TTA) of time-series anomaly detectors using incoming data. Yet the same stream may contain anomalies, so TTA with online updates must decide which test samples should be used for adaptation. This leaves a more basic question: even if updates are restricted to samples labeled as normal in the benchmark, does TTA reliably improve anomaly detection? We study this question with a controlled intervention that replaces the practical update selector with a rule that uses only samples labeled as normal in the benchmark, while keeping the rest of the online adaptation procedure unchanged. Across nine benchmark families, four optimizer configurations, and five random seeds, we obtain three main findings: (1) using only samples labeled as normal in the benchmark does not reliably improve detection; (2) practical TTA failures exhibit two distinct responses to this intervention, with 63 of 180 settings recovering a clear detection gain while 90 still show no clear detection gain; and (3) the updates reduce their immediate reconstruction objective across the evaluated settings, yet these reductions do not consistently lead to better anomaly ranking. Both failure patterns persist across alternative practical adaptation variants and modest perturbations of the benchmark labels. Together, these findings show that improving sample selection alone is not a complete solution to reconstruction-based TSAD TTA.

</details>


### [60] [A Systematic Analysis of a Small Optimal Subspace for Physics-Informed Neural Networks: $n$-DAS $\subset \mathbb{R}^p$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23773)
*Ahmad AliAhmad,Khemraj Shukla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出按梯度对齐而非按Hessian特征值大小选取的"主导对齐子空间"(DAS)，在PINN基准上证明收敛只需低维、与梯度对齐的Hessian子空间即可匹配全空间训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有子空间优化方法（如基于主导/体部分解耦的Dom-SGD、Bulk-SGD）以特征值幅值定义子空间，以及基于能量分数阈值的Krylov自适应方法，均无法刻画优化实际发生的方向，在低阶模态上表现不佳。作者认为这是结构性问题：最大曲率方向一般并非训练移动的方向。

Method: 对Hessian做完整特征分解，但依据梯度对齐度（而非特征值幅值）筛选方向，构造主导对齐子空间DAS；在DAS内进行受限训练并配合线搜索；以PINNs为测试床，借助抛物型、椭圆型、双曲型微分算子诱导严重病态、各向异性的损失地形；并将分析扩展到DAS内的Gauss-Newton。

Result: 在Poisson、热传导、波动与粘性Burgers基准上，DAS受限训练（配线搜索）即使投影Hessian仍呈病态，也能匹配全空间训练的收敛效果，表明收敛并不需要全部Hessian特征方向，仅需低维且与梯度对齐的子集。

Conclusion: Hessian特征值幅值不是选取优化子空间的正确准则，梯度对齐度才是；DAS既是理解优化动力学的分析工具，也为设计子空间感知优化器提供基础，Gauss-Newton在DAS内的分析进一步指明未来方向。

Abstract: The search for a low-dimensional subspace that governs neural network training has been a persistent goal in optimization. Yet subspaces defined by Hessian eigenvalue magnitude, such as the dominant/bulk decomposition underlying Dom-SGD and Bulk-SGD, have repeatedly failed to capture where optimization takes place, and adaptive Krylov-based alternatives built on energy-fraction thresholds show promise but still struggle at lower eigenmodes. We argue this failure is structural: the directions of highest curvature are not, in general, the directions along which training moves. We introduce the dominant aligned subspace (DAS), constructed from a full eigendecomposition of the Hessian but selected by gradient alignment rather than eigenvalue magnitude, as a tool for understanding optimization dynamics and a foundation for designing methods that exploit them. To expose this structure under the hardest conditions, we use physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) as a testbed: differential operators from the parabolic, elliptic, and hyperbolic classes induce severely ill-conditioned, anisotropic loss landscapes. Across Poisson, heat, wave, and viscous Burgers benchmarks DAS-restricted training with a line search matches full-space training even where the projected Hessian remains ill-conditioned, showing empirically that convergence does not require all Hessian eigendirections but only a low-dimensional, gradient-aligned subset. We extend the analysis to Gauss-Newton within the DAS and discuss what this structure implies for future subspace-aware optimizers.

</details>


### [61] [TabBioMed: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Biomedical Tabular Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23779)
*Pau Mateo-Bernadó,Saivenkata Nagavyjayanthi Polapragada,David Bonet,Pol Arbiol Rakuljic,Laia Mogas Pladevall,Marçal Comajoan Cara,Jesus Gonzalez-Ferrer,Jordi Abante,Daniel Mas Montserrat,David Haussler,Alexander G. Ioannidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TabBioMed——包含94个生物医学表格数据集的大规模基准，并评测23个模型，发现表格基础模型整体最优但优势依赖数据情景，树模型与调优MLP在成本约束下仍有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习在生物医学预测中进展迅速，但针对结构化（表格）生物医学数据的评测仍高度碎片化，尤其缺乏面向高维、大样本、缺失值与类别不平衡等特征的统一评测框架。

Method: 构建TabBioMed基准：汇集94个精选公开生物医学表格数据集，覆盖电子健康记录、药物发现、基因组学、转录组学、蛋白质组学、代谢组学、单细胞组学与系统生物学；通过统一且数据集感知的预处理与评测框架整合，支持分类、回归与多目标任务的可复现比较；评测23个模型，涵盖经典基线、梯度提升树、神经表格架构与表格基础模型。

Result: 表格基础模型在家族层面取得最强预测性能，但其增益呈情景依赖；树模型与调优多层感知机仍具很强竞争力，在推理时或训练成本受限时常占优。

Conclusion: 生物医学表格学习强烈依赖上下文，最优模型选择取决于数据情景、任务类型与计算预算；作者开源了数据集与划分、预处理代码、基线与结果，为实际模型选择与后续方法研究提供可复现基准。

Abstract: Recent advances in machine learning have accelerated biomedical prediction, but evaluation on structured biomedical data remains fragmented, especially for datasets with high dimensionality, large sample size, missingness, and class imbalance. We introduce TabBioMed, a large-scale benchmark of 94 curated public biomedical tabular datasets spanning electronic health records, drug discovery, genomics, transcriptomics, proteomics, metabolomics, single-cell omics, and systems biology. TabBioMed unifies these datasets through a standardized, dataset-aware preprocessing and evaluation framework, enabling reproducible comparison across classification, regression, and multi-target tasks. We benchmark 23 models across classical baselines, gradient-boosted trees, neural tabular architectures, and tabular foundation models. Foundation models deliver the strongest family-level predictive performance. However, their gains are regime-dependent: tree-based models and tuned multilayer perceptrons remain highly competitive, often dominating under inference-time or training-cost constraints. Overall, TabBioMed shows that biomedical tabular learning is strongly context-dependent, with optimal model choice depending on data regime, task type, and computational budget. We release TabBioMed with curated datasets and splits, preprocessing code, baselines, and results, providing a reproducible benchmark for practical model selection and future methods development in biomedical machine learning, available at https://github.com/AI-sandbox/TabBioMed.

</details>


### [62] [CAFE-AQI: Coverage-Aware Day-Ahead AQI Forecasting with Early Warning and Conformal Uncertainty in Bangladesh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23815)
*Md Rafid Islam,Rafsan Jany,Rafid Bin Zaman,Mohammad Fahim Islam,Hafiz Abdur Rahman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出覆盖感知的次日最大AQI预测框架CAFE-AQI，针对孟加拉国数据覆盖突变与城市标签重复（103标签仅61条轨迹）问题进行去重加权，并给出次日空气质量预警与预测区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有次日AQI预测通常假设城市记录完整且相互独立；但对孟加拉国大规模数据集审计发现2022年8月后覆盖范围急剧扩张，且103个城市标签仅对应61条唯一AQI轨迹，重复记录会污染训练与评估，导致模型性能被高估。

Method: 按时间划分为训练（2022–2023）、校准（2024）、测试（2025）；对相同轨迹加权以抑制重复记录主导；融合时间特征、污染物特征与五近邻空间上下文；设置类别平衡的预警头估计AQI≥151概率；采用风险条件化保形校准输出预测区间；并以整块空间留出验证迁移性。

Result: 在去重平衡的2025年数据上，MAE 9.76、RMSE 15.04、R² 0.910；MAE较持续性基线低3.63（95% CI: 3.49–3.76），且在全部61条轨迹组上误差均更低；不健康预警F1 0.908、AUROC 0.982；名义90%区间实际覆盖92.09%；整块空间留出训练时MAE为10.66。

Conclusion: 数据重复与覆盖漂移是可造成评估偏差的关键隐患，去重加权与覆盖感知建模能显著提升次日AQI预测与预警的可靠性；轻量模型在未见地点具备可接受的迁移能力，保形校准区间略偏保守。

Abstract: Next-day air quality forecasting often assumes city records are equally complete and independent. However, an audit of a large Bangladesh dataset revealed a sharp coverage expansion after August 2022 and showed that 103 city labels correspond to only 61 unique AQI trajectories. To address these issues, this paper presents CAFE-AQI, a coverage-aware framework for forecasting next-day maximum AQI. It also estimates the probability of Unhealthy-or-worse conditions (AQI >= 151). Data were divided chronologically into training (2022-2023), calibration (2024), and testing (2025). To prevent duplicate records from dominating training and evaluation, identical trajectories were weighted. The framework combines temporal and pollutant features with a five-neighbor spatial context. It also includes a class-balanced warning head and risk-conditional conformal calibration aimed at providing early warnings and prediction intervals. Evaluation on the duplicate-balanced 2025 data gave an MAE of 9.76, an RMSE of 15.04, and an R-squared value of 0.910 for CAFE-AQI. Its MAE was 3.63 AQI lower than persistence (95% CI: 3.49-3.76), with lower errors in each of the 61 trajectory groups. For unhealthy-air warnings, the F1-score was 0.908 and the AUROC was 0.982. The nominal 90% intervals covered 92.09% of observations. When an entire spatial block was excluded from training, the MAE remained 10.66, showing that the lightweight model transferred reasonably well to unseen locations.

</details>


### [63] [Online State-Representation Adaptation under Synthetic Interaction-Cue Remapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23836)
*Bin Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在一个单步合成任务中，比较持续在线更新的logistic状态预测器与在迁移时冻结的同一预测器，发现在线索-预测关系变化时，在线更新显著提升晚期动作准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当线索所预测的内容发生变化时，继续更新已学到的映射可能帮助智能体选择恰当的动作；作者希望检验在线适应本身对动作选择的贡献，并厘清动作阈值选择与反馈信息量对结果归属的影响。

Method: 设计单步合成任务，在6个留出环境中评估10种条件，每条件1296次配对运行、共1296000条迁移记录。核心对比为持续从状态标签学习的logistic状态预测器与其在迁移时冻结的版本；并引入直接策略（相同预测、不同动作阈值）、上下文老虎机（利用所选动作的奖励）以及基于发展历史的权重与中性权重的对照。

Result: 晚期动作准确率：在线更新93.39%，冻结系数64.63%，配对差异28.76个百分点（95%环境自助区间[8.73, 53.19]），且在全部6个测试环境中均更优。直接策略达95.65%；上下文老虎机达94.23%并累积更高效用。历史权重较中性权重降低累积效用（差异-3.45，区间[-4.48, -2.43]）。

Conclusion: 在线适应确实提升晚期动作准确率且跨环境稳健；但动作阈值选择与反馈中可用的状态信息会显著改变结果，因而部分收益不能单独归因于在线适应。

Abstract: When cues change what they predict, continuing to update a learned mapping may help an agent choose the appropriate action. We tested this in a one-step synthetic task by comparing a logistic state predictor that continued to learn from state labels with the same predictor frozen at transfer. We evaluated ten conditions in six held-out environments, with 1,296 paired runs per condition and 1,296,000 transfer records in total. Late action accuracy was 93.39% with online updates and 64.63% with frozen coefficients, a paired difference of 28.76 [8.73, 53.19] percentage points (95% environment-bootstrap interval). A direct policy reached 95.65% using the same predictions with a different action threshold. A contextual bandit reached 94.23% and earned more cumulative utility than the adaptive predictor. The bandit used the reward of its chosen action, which in this task reveals the true state when combined with that action. Weights based on development history lowered cumulative utility relative to neutral weights (difference -3.45 [-4.48, -2.43]). The adaptive predictor outperformed its frozen counterpart in late accuracy in each of the six test environments. The other comparisons show how the choice of action threshold and the information available in feedback affect what can be attributed to online adaptation.

</details>


### [64] [Beyond Cross-Modal Reconstruction: Participant-Level Evaluation of sEMG-Kinematic Coupling in Post-Stroke Gait](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23856)
*Rafsan Jany,Md Rafid Islam,Mohammad Fahim Islam,Hafiz Abdur Rahman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究用冻结的双向模型检验肌电与步态运动学之间的被试特异性神经肌肉耦合，发现模型的跨模态重建主要依赖被试共享的步相模式，而非个体化生理耦合，需被试级对照才能将重建精度视为个体生理证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的跨模态重建常被视为生理耦合的证据，但它可能只反映所有被试共享的时间模式。作者因此质疑：冻结的双向模型是否真正捕捉到了被试特异性的神经肌肉耦合。

Method: 在82名健康被试和43名卒中后被试（留出集）上评估冻结双向模型。采用完全移除源模态、跨被试源替换，以及基于周期位置-年龄-性别的模板基线三种对照；并将学习到的双侧不对称性与透明的波形/导数不对称性比较，在重复嵌套交叉验证下预测POMA评分。

Result: 完全移除源模态使RMSE分别上升6.90%（肌电→运动学）和11.38%（反向）；跨被试替换仅上升1.68%和1.67%，模板基线反而使RMSE下降2.89%和0.55%。POMA预测中，步速、年龄、性别基线的MAE为3.693，加入绝对值和带符号学习不对称性后分别为4.229和4.427；20次重复评估中，所有不对称性表征均劣于协变量基线，且经协变量校正与FDR校正后无局部学习特征显著。

Conclusion: 模型主要依赖共享的步相模式，未体现明确的被试特异性耦合或额外临床价值；在将重建精度作为个体化生理证据之前，需要进行被试级对照。

Abstract: Accurate cross-modal reconstruction is often treated as evidence of physiological coupling, although it may simply reflect temporal patterns shared across participants. We tested whether frozen bidirectional models captured subject-specific neuromuscular coupling between electromyographic activity and gait kinematics in 82 held-out able-bodied and 43 post-stroke participants. Complete source-modality removal and cross-participant source substitution, along with a cycle-position-age-sex template baseline, were applied. Learned bilateral asymmetry was also compared with transparent waveform and derivative asymmetry for predicting Performance-Oriented Mobility Assessment (POMA) scores in the case of repeated nested cross-validation. Complete source removal increased reconstruction RMSE by 6.90% for electromyography-to-kinematics and 11.38% in the reverse direction. In contrast, cross-participant substitution increased RMSE by only 1.68% and 1.67%, respectively, while the template reduced RMSE by 2.89% and 0.55%. For POMA prediction, walking speed, age, and sex achieved an MAE of 3.693, compared with 4.229 and 4.427 after adding absolute and signed learned asymmetry, respectively. In all of the 20 repeated evaluations, all asymmetry representations performed worse than the covariate baseline. No localized learned feature remained significant after covariate adjustment and false-discovery-rate correction. The model mainly relied on shared gait-phase patterns, with no clear subject-specific coupling or added clinical value. Participant-level controls are therefore needed before reconstruction accuracy is treated as evidence of individualized physiology.

</details>


### [65] [A Rank-One Bypass of Fixed Linear Erasure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23880)
*Jai Kumar Sharma,Vanshaj Khattar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 线性概念擦除所保证的"不可读"仅限于当前特征，若上游模型可被修改，且擦除层存在一条对下游分类器可见的方向，攻击者即可用秩一仿射更新把目标分数写入该方向并穿过擦除层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性概念擦除只能保证目标概念对指定线性分类器族在当前内部特征上不可读；但在开放权重发布中，攻击者可修改产生这些特征的上游层。作者希望研究固定仿射擦除层、允许上游模型变化时，擦除保证是否仍然有效，从而厘清"线性受保护性"与"抗篡改性"的区别。

Method: 固定一个仿射擦除层，仅允许其上游模型变化。核心判据是：只要擦除层传输了至少一个被允许的下游分类器可见的方向，攻击者就能把目标分数写入该方向并穿过该层。作者证明该条件既是充分也是必要的，且对非恒定线性目标分数，所需改动为一个秩一仿射更新。实验在六个 CIFAR-10 类对任务上进行。

Result: 该路径恢复了几乎所有擦除前的目标性能，且可在数值容差内保持已部署良性线性头的分数不变。攻击代价方面：在8个目标标签、2000个无标注校准样本下，10%的平均相对特征编辑可达到79.8%测试准确率，相当于拟合分数相对随机增益的91.2%；更严格的编辑上限会显著降低成功率。

Conclusion: 线性受保护性仍是关于当前表示的有效保证，但本身不构成对可编辑开放权重模型的抗篡改保证；评估概念擦除时需同时考虑上游可编辑性。

Abstract: Linear concept erasure can make a target unreadable to a specified family of linear classifiers applied to a model's current internal features. In an open-weight release, however, an attacker can modify the layers that produce those features. We study a fixed affine erasure layer whose upstream model is allowed to change. If the layer transmits even one direction visible to an allowed downstream classifier, the attacker can write a target score into that direction and carry it through the layer. We show that this condition is also necessary, and that for a nonconstant linear target score the required change is a rank-one affine update. Across six CIFAR-10 class-pair tasks, the route restores nearly all pre-erasure target performance and can leave a deployed benign linear head's scores unchanged to numerical tolerance. We then price the attack: with eight target labels and 2,000 unlabelled calibration examples, a 10% mean relative feature edit yields 79.8% test accuracy, which is 91.2% of the fitted score's gain over chance; tighter edit limits sharply reduce success. Linear guardedness therefore remains a valid guarantee about the current representation, but it is not by itself a tamper-resistance guarantee for an editable open-weight model.

</details>


### [66] [Kernel Ridge Attention: Nonlinear Memory with Selective Forgetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23896)
*Haowei Chen,Yichen Wang,Congfu Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Kernel Ridge Attention (KRA)：一种递归序列混合器，用学习的非线性特征与协方差引导的岭回归递推更新实现关联记忆，配合内容相关的Ornstein–Uhlenbeck转移控制保留与不确定性恢复；具备精确时间核解释、与上下文长度无关的推理状态，并通过关联块映射与显式伴随支持并行训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力与递归模型的序列记忆要么推理状态随上下文长度增长、要么缺乏非线性自适应记忆的理论基础。作者希望构建一种既有原理支撑、又能保持常数推理状态并支持高效并行训练的递归序列混合器。

Method: 1) 以递归岭回归存储键–值关联，利用历史键之间的相关性决定新观测如何修改记忆（协方差引导更新）；2) 引入内容依赖的Ornstein–Uhlenbeck转移，控制记忆保留并为未来写入恢复不确定性；3) 推导该构造的精确时间核解释；4) 设计关联块映射与显式伴随（adjoint），在保持递推语义的前提下实现并行训练。

Result: 在受控语言建模实验中，KRA的预测质量优于注意力与递归基线；在评估的递归模型中自然联想回忆更强；并取得实用的分布式训练吞吐。

Conclusion: KRA为非线性、自适应序列记忆提供了一个有原理基础且工程可行的框架：常数规模推理状态、精确核解释与可并行训练兼得，并在语言建模与联想回忆任务上表现良好。

Abstract: Kernel Ridge Attention (KRA) is a recurrent sequence mixer that combines learned nonlinear features with covariance-guided memory updates. It stores associations through recursive ridge regression, using correlations among past keys to determine how new observations modify memory. A content-dependent Ornstein--Uhlenbeck transition controls retention and restores uncertainty for future writes. This construction admits an exact temporal-kernel interpretation and maintains an inference state whose size is independent of context length. Associative block maps and explicit adjoints support parallel training while preserving the recursive update. In controlled language-modeling experiments, KRA improves prediction quality over attention and recurrent baselines, strengthens natural associative recall over the evaluated recurrent models, and achieves practical distributed training throughput. These properties provide a principled foundation for nonlinear, adaptive sequence memory.

</details>


### [67] [PGU-Decoder: Prior-Guided Unified Decoding for Industrial Road-Network Map Updating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23936)
*Yuguang Ning,Bingbing Kang,Nan Zhao,Zhixuan Li,Ze Zhao,Dongdong Liu,Wenjian Zhu,Zekai Liu,Junhui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PGU-Decoder，一个面向路口内路网生成、合流/分流检测与可行驶区域检测三类任务的统一解码框架；通过点级先验查询与关系图注意力实现实例级建模，并在中国360+城市规模化验证，落地高德后三任务质量分数>98%、人工核验成本降低95%、更新频率由周级提升至小时级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路网地图的及时更新对导航与自动驾驶至关重要，而路口内路网生成、合流/分流检测、可行驶区域检测分别覆盖拓扑、几何与属性三类关键任务。分离式流水线导致规则维护、质量保证与人工核验重复，显著抬高运营成本，因此需要统一的语义预测框架。

Method: 提出生产级统一解码框架PGU-Decoder：先验查询模块（PQM）在路网几何采样点上构建点级先验查询，并在整个解码过程中保持固定参考点以维持位置先验，使模型专注周边上下文；将点级表示聚合为实例级表示；用关系图注意力网络（RGAT）建模实例间依赖；最后由任务专用头输出各任务预测。

Result: 在中国360余城市的大规模自动化路网地图更新中，PGU-Decoder一致优于7个基线；部署于高德地图后，三项任务质量分数均超过98%，人工核验成本下降95%，更新频率从每周提升至每小时。

Conclusion: 结果表明，统一解码范式可在单一框架内同时覆盖拓扑、几何与属性任务，避免多流水线重复开销，是一种实用且高性价比的工业级路网地图更新方案。

Abstract: Timely road-network map updates are essential to navigation and autonomous driving. Three key tasks are intra-intersection road network generation, merging/diverging detection, and drivable-area detection, covering topology, geometry, and attributes, respectively. Separate pipelines duplicate rule maintenance, quality assurance, and manual verification, increasing operational costs. We propose PGU-Decoder, a production-grade unified decoding framework for semantic prediction across these tasks. A Prior Query Module (PQM) constructs point-level prior queries from sampled locations on road-network geometries. Fixed reference points preserve positional priors throughout decoding, allowing the model to focus on surrounding context. Point-level representations are aggregated into instance-level representations, and a Relational Graph Attention Network (RGAT) captures inter-instance dependencies. Task-specific heads then generate predictions. We validate PGU-Decoder in large-scale automated road-network map updating across more than 360 cities in China, where it consistently outperforms seven baselines. Following deployment at AutoNavi Map, it achieves quality scores above 98% on all three tasks, reduces manual verification costs by 95%, and increases update frequency from weekly to hourly. These results demonstrate a practical and cost-effective industrial solution for road-network map updating.

</details>


### [68] [OMNIS+: Semantic RAN Slicing and Quantization for Dynamic Split Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23977)
*Langtian Qin,Avishkar Dnyaneshwar Kawadgave,Ian Andrew Harshbarger,Leïla Nasraoui,Carla Fabiana Chiasserini,Marco Levorato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OMNIS+：面向多小区边缘计算的语义RAN切片框架，基于动态拆分神经网络，联合动态压缩与自适应量化实现任务卸载，并用多智能体分布式优化求解MD与ES的耦合决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘计算通过任务卸载让资源受限设备运行ML应用，RAN切片负责联合供给通信与计算资源。但现有RAN切片框架依赖静态计算模型，无法利用现代神经架构带来的动态语义数据表示能力。

Method: 提出OMNIS+，采用新一代动态拆分神经模型；引入动态神经压缩+自适应量化形成跨通信负载与计算维度的决策空间，并提出"box"量化方案以增强在不同压缩率下对传输比特错误的鲁棒性。针对部分可观测性与节点目标差异，建模两个相互依赖的优化问题：(i) MD在QoS与队列稳定性约束下，联合调整动态拆分DNN分支与小区关联以最大化推理精度；(ii) 各ES分配带宽、GPU频率与MCS，在小区容量约束下最大化关联MD的最小推理精度。求解采用多智能体分布式优化：MD作为回溯感知Lyapunov控制下的因果上下文多臂老虎机智能体，ES采用块坐标下降进行资源分配。

Result: 相较最先进基线，OMNIS+将QoS违约概率最多降低1.75（倍），并获得更优的精度—违约权衡。

Conclusion: 动态语义RAN切片与动态拆分神经模型相结合，可在多小区边缘计算中有效提升鲁棒性与精度—可靠性权衡。

Abstract: Edge computing enables resource-constrained devices to execute machine learning (ML) applications via task offloading, with radio access network (RAN) slicing playing a crucial role in provisioning joint communication and computing resources. However, existing RAN slicing frameworks rely on static computing models, limiting their ability to exploit dynamic semantic data representations enabled by modern neural architectures. To address this limitation, we propose OMNIS+, a semantic RAN slicing framework for multi-cell edge computing built upon a new generation of dynamic split neural models. OMNIS+ incorporates dynamic neural compression paired with adaptive quantization for task offloading, providing a versatile decision space across communication payload and compute dimensions. We leveraged a novel ``box'' quantization scheme that markedly enhances resilience to transmission bit errors across diverse compression rates. Considering partial system observability and distinct node objectives, we formulate two interdependent optimization problems: (i)~mobile devices (MDs) maximize inference accuracy under quality-of-service (QoS) and queue-stability constraints by jointly adapting dynamic split deep neural network (DNN) branches and cell association; and (ii)~each edge server (ES) allocates bandwidth, graphics processing unit (GPU) frequency, and modulation and coding scheme (MCS) to maximize the max--min inference accuracy among associated MDs under cell capacity limits. We solve these problems using a multi-agent distributed optimization framework, where MDs act as causal contextual multi-armed bandit (MAB) agents under backlog-aware Lyapunov control, while each ES executes resource allocation via block coordinate descent (BCD). Compared with state-of-the-art baselines, OMNIS+ reduces the QoS violation probability by up to 1.75 while achieving a better accuracy--violation trade-off.

</details>


### [69] [Rethinking Fixed-Horizon Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24001)
*Tianxiang Chen,Jingyuan Zhou,Weiming Yang,Kaidi Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现隐式CoT推理扩展至预设全长度反而降低准确率，提出以"构造性进展"刻画推理收益，并用轻量方法估计剩余进展、自适应调整隐式推理长度，在不重训LLM的情况下最多提升7.2个百分点准确率并减少29.0%隐式计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐式推理器（latent reasoner）在推理时通常运行固定的隐式推理长度（latent horizon），直觉上更长长度应带来更多推理机会；但作者发现把隐式CoT扩展到预设全长实际上会损害推理性能，因此需要重新审视固定长度的隐式推理范式。

Method: 作者将隐式CoT划分为不同功能阶段：早期计算形成前沿推理状态（frontier reasoning states），后期计算主要重组已有候选并可能削弱对正确候选的支持。据此以"已实现构造性进展"（realized constructive progress）与"剩余构造性进展"（remaining constructive progress）刻画隐式CoT的推理收益；实验发现已实现进展越高当前准确率越高，剩余进展越多则继续推理的未来收益越大。基于此提出一种轻量方法，估计剩余推理进展并在推理过程中自适应调整隐式推理长度，无需重训LLM。

Result: 在多个代表性隐式CoT推理器与多个算术基准上，方法使推理准确率最多提升7.2个百分点，同时隐式计算量最多减少29.0%，且无需重新训练LLM。

Conclusion: 固定长度的隐式推理并非越长越好，后期计算可能无效甚至有害；通过监测并估计构造性进展来动态确定隐式推理长度，可同时提升准确率与计算效率，为隐式CoT推理提供了新的自适应范式。

Abstract: Existing latent reasoners typically run for a preset latent horizon during inference. Intuitively, a longer horizon should provide more opportunity for reasoning. However, in this work, we find that extending latent Chain-of-Thought (CoT) to the full preset horizon may in fact degrade reasoning performance. We observe that latent CoT evolves through distinct functional stages, with earlier computation forming frontier reasoning states, while later computation mainly reorganizes existing candidates and can weaken gold candidate support. These findings motivate a rethink of fixed-horizon latent inference. To address this, we characterize the reasoning benefits of latent CoT by realized and remaining constructive progress. Empirically, we find that higher realized constructive progress corresponds to higher current reasoning accuracy, while more remaining progress corresponds to larger future gains from continued latent CoT. To this end, we introduce a lightweight method that estimates the remaining reasoning progress and adaptively adjusts the latent horizon during inference. Across representative latent CoT reasoners and multiple arithmetic benchmarks, our method improves reasoning accuracy by up to 7.2 percentage points while reducing latent computation by up to 29.0%, without retraining the LLM.

</details>


### [70] [Algorithmic Market Making Can Lead to Rough Volatility Through the Order Book](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24028)
*Sai Niranjan Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将做市商建模为基于未知oracle的目标价差与一阶梯度调整的"desk"，在信息驱动的异步minibatch噪声下，其连续极限为Dean–Kawasaki型守恒SPDE，波动驱动订单流，多尺度回顾窗口诱导幂律衰减记忆，从而自然产生rough volatility。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究显示市场波动路径比布朗运动更粗糙，理论上可由接近不稳定、记忆幂律衰减的订单流产生；但订单簿动态主要由算法做市主导，这种订单流如何从做市行为中涌现尚不清楚。本文旨在给出一个与做市微观机制一致的解释。

Method: 每个做市商（desk）用一阶梯度规则将其报价与中间价的距离调整向未知oracle给定的目标距离；不同desk不同步行动，仅在其oracle相关信息到达时更新，各自采样特征回顾期。整体规则等价于带信息驱动(mini-batch)噪声的随机梯度下降。取连续极限后，订单簿成交量服从保守型SPDE（Dean–Kawasaki方程），其涨落驱动订单流；不同desk的回顾期异质性导致订单流具有幂律衰减的持久记忆，再经标准标度论证得到rough volatility。

Result: 在模拟agent和约6个月、约7.9亿笔订单的Binance数据上验证了模型的关键预测；在以做市商为主的流动市场（如Binance永续合约）中，订单簿行为与模型描述的desk调整机制一致。

Conclusion: 算法做市的信息驱动异步更新机制可在微观层面一致地生成rough volatility：Dean–Kawasaki型守恒涨落与异质回顾期记忆共同构成机制链条，且经仿真与大规模实证数据支持。

Abstract: Existing work suggests that markets can show volatility with paths rougher than those of Brownian motion. In theory this can happen when the order flow is close to instability with memory that decays as a power law. However, it is not fully clear how such order flow could arise from the predominantly algorithmic market making that characterizes order book dynamics. We show one such consistent mechanism. We treat each market maker as a desk that uses a first-order gradient rule to move the distances of its orders from the mid price toward target distances provided by an unknown oracle. We assume that distinct desks do not act synchronously in time but act whenever new information relevant to their respective oracles arrives, which each desk samples over its own characteristic lookback horizon. Across all desks the rule therefore takes the form of stochastic gradient descent (SGD) with information-driven (mini-batch) noise. In the continuum limit this update rule makes the volume of the order book follow a conservative stochastic partial differential equation (the Dean--Kawasaki equation) whose fluctuations drive the order flow. The distinct horizons of the desks further induce a persistent memory in the order flow that decays as a power law. Standard scaling arguments then lead to rough volatility. We confirm the key predictions of the model on simulated agents and on six months of Binance data with about 790 million orders. In liquid markets governed mainly by market makers, such as the perpetual futures of Binance, the order books are consistent with how our desks change the book.

</details>


### [71] [From Data to Valves: A Self-Learning, Production-Grade Edge Stack for Sustainable HVAC Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24052)
*Abbas Raza Ali,Muhammad Hamza Rehman,Xu Li,Muhammad Ajmal Siddiqui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一套部署于单台NVIDIA Jetson Orin边缘网关、可持续在线自适应的HVAC学习控制生产栈：含分机组预训练transformer预测器、共享策略的DSAC冷水阀控制智能体，以及符合ASHRAE Guideline 14的变点回归基线用于节能量核算；给出18个月酒店实测的收敛与精度结果及在104台机组上的影子运行与资源测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习的HVAC控制器多止步于仿真，少数实际部署者通常采用离线训练、部署即冻结、云端重训的范式，缺乏在楼内设备上持续自适应并自我验证效果的实用方案。
