Method: 三组件同栈运行于每栋楼的Jetson Orin网关：(a) 紧凑transformer编码器预测器，按设备组各一、带可学习机组嵌入，预测各空气侧机组下一周期温度，按5分钟一周期做单步梯度的预序列学习，并由五道独立门控限制异常读数影响；(b) 分布型soft actor-critic智能体，跨机组类型共享策略，将实测状态映射为冷冻水阀位，受不可被学习组件放松的结构性安全包络约束；第二代每晚仅用记录转移在设备端学习，引入行为锚定与critic校准项，以四项无模型指标评分并经原子热切换上线；(c) 受ASHRAE Guideline 14约束的变点回归基线提供反事实，用于节能量认证。

Result: 某酒店18个月数据上，预测器从随机权重一周内收敛，空气处理机组、新风机组、风机盘管MAE分别为0.37、0.39、0.11 °C，无重训事件；另一酒店基线在46天留出期复现误差在0.07%以内；RL智能体已完成仿真campaign并在104台机组上影子运行；报告共享15 GB设备上各服务的实测延迟与内存。

Conclusion: 研究给出系统层面的部署经验与资源画像，并明确界定当前证据已确立与尚未确立的结论边界；RL策略尚未进入闭环实际控制，仅处于影子模式，节能量认证依赖基线而非策略上线收益。

Abstract: Learned HVAC controllers rarely leave the simulator, and those that do are typically trained offline, frozen at deployment and retrained from the cloud. We present a production stack in which three learning components run on one NVIDIA Jetson Orin gateway per building, adapt continuously from the building's own data, and verify their own effect. (a) A compact transformer-encoder forecaster, one per equipment group with learned per-unit embeddings, predicts every air-side unit's next-cycle temperature and learns prequentially, one gradient step per five-minute cycle, behind five independent gates that bound the influence of any corrupt reading. (b) A distributional soft actor-critic agent, one shared policy across unit types, converts the measured state into a chilled-water valve position under a structural safety envelope that no learned component can soften; its second generation learns nightly on the device from recorded transitions alone, with a behaviour anchor and critic calibration terms, is scored by four model-free metrics and promoted by atomic hot-swap. (c) A change-point regression baseline held to ASHRAE Guideline 14 supplies the counterfactual against which savings are certified. On eighteen months of data from a hotel the forecaster converges from random weights within a week and reaches 0.37, 0.39 and 0.11 degree C MAE for air-handling, fresh-air and fan-coil units with no retraining event; on a different hotel the baseline reproduces a 46-day held-out period to within 0.07%. The RL agent has completed its in-simulator campaign and runs in shadow on 104 units; we report measured latency and memory of every service on a shared 15 GB device, the systems lessons of the campaign, and precisely what the evidence does and does not yet establish.

</details>


### [72] [Faster to the Wrong Answer: The Price of Local Updates in Federated Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24057)
*Molly Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文刻画了联邦学习中局部计算在给定条件数约束下可达到的最小最坏情形偏差，精确给出每单位异质性的偏差上界 $(L-\mu\chi)/(2\mu L\sqrt{\chi})$，并证明小步长局部梯度下降与近端更新可达该界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部计算能改善联邦更新的条件数，但会引入偏差，使其不动点偏离全局极小点。作者希望在预设的条件数约束下，量化偏差与条件数改善之间的最优权衡，从而明确局部计算的可达极限。

Method: 考虑强凸二次客户端目标，Hessian 特征值位于 $[\mu,L]$；局部更新表示为共享的正谱滤波器。以全局极小点处梯度的均方根度量客户端异质性。对一致代理条件数上界 $\chi\in[1,L/\mu]$，推导每单位异质性的精确最坏偏差。分析中将任意维、含非交换 Hessian 的种群归约为标量曲率上的分布，构造最优性证书与至多三个标量客户端的最坏情形实例；并将 sharp 近端偏差界推广到二次型之外的光滑强凸目标，量化不精确局部求解带来的误差。

Result: 得到精确最坏情形偏差 $(L-\mu\chi)/(2\mu L\sqrt{\chi})$；证明足够小步长的局部梯度下降与近端更新可达该界；给出降维归约、最优性证书与三客户端最坏构造；sharp 近端偏差界可扩展至一般光滑强凸目标，并给出不精确局部求解的误差量化。

Conclusion: 局部计算带来的条件数改善与不动点偏差存在可精确刻画的权衡，所给界是最优且可实现的；通过降维归约可将高维异质性问题化简为低维标量情形，为联邦优化中局部步数与近端正则化设计提供理论基础。

Abstract: Local computation can improve the conditioning of federated updates but may also introduce bias, causing their fixed point to differ from the global minimizer. We characterize the minimum worst-case bias achievable under a prescribed conditioning bound. We consider strongly convex quadratic client objectives with Hessian eigenvalues in $[μ,L]$ and local updates represented by a shared positive spectral filter. Client heterogeneity is measured by the root-mean-square gradient at the global minimizer. For a uniform surrogate condition number bounded by $χ\in[1,L/μ]$, we establish the exact worst-case bias per unit heterogeneity, $(L-μχ)/(2μL\sqrtχ)$, and show that local gradient descent with sufficiently small step sizes and proximal updates attain this bound. Our analysis reduces populations of arbitrary dimension, including noncommuting Hessians, to distributions over scalar curvatures. This reduction provides optimality certificates and worst-case constructions involving at most three scalar clients. We further extend the sharp proximal bias bound to smooth strongly convex objectives beyond quadratics and quantify the error introduced by inexact local solves.

</details>


### [73] [Replay, Don't Resample: Off-Policy Consolidation of Long-Horizon RL Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24059)
*Zhenrui Yue,Gongxiaohui Chen,Huimin Zeng,Honglei Zhuang,Jiashuo Sun,Zhen Han,Dong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 MRPD：直接重放各领域专家 RL 训练日志进行多专家策略蒸馏，无需重新采样环境，约以训练专家 1/7 的算力完成合并，并在推理、搜索、SQL 三个长程智能体任务上超过在线 RL 与 MOPD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多教师同策略蒸馏（MOPD）合并专家代价高：学生需在每个环境中重新采样，专家需为每个 token 打分，且学生继承最终专家的漂移。作者认为专家 RL 过程已产出了合并所需的一切（rollout、采样概率、奖励及其学习顺序）。

Method: MRPD 按专家学习顺序重放其训练日志：学生模型在日志前缀上重新计算策略梯度（利用已打分的轨迹与采样概率），并在相同前缀上蒸馏最终专家的分布；因遵循专家自身轨迹，训练-推理失配小、学生处于 clip 范围内，故重放虽源于 off-policy，合并全程近似 on-policy。训练器为唯一进程，不采样、不调用环境或奖励模型。

Result: 合并成本约为训练专家的 1/7；在 reasoning、search、SQL 三个长程智能体域上得到最优学生模型，显著优于在线 RL 基线，并以远低于 MOPD 的成本超过同来源专家的 MOPD。消融显示重放轨迹贡献主要增益，策略梯度项与蒸馏项均有正向作用；日志的“顺序”比其数量与奖励更重要。

Conclusion: 专家训练日志应被视为一种课程（curriculum）而非数据混合（data mixture）；重放式合并是高效、有效的多专家整合范式。

Abstract: Frontier post-training follows a common recipe: train one expert per domain with reinforcement learning (RL), then consolidate the experts into one model via multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD). Such consolidation is expensive: the student samples in every environment again, an expert scores every sampled token, and the student inherits the drift of the final experts. Yet we argue that the experts' RL runs have already produced what consolidation needs: rollouts with their sampling probabilities and rewards, in the order the experts learned from them. We propose multi-expert replay policy distillation (MRPD), which replays these training logs through the student in the experts' order. At each step, the policy gradient is recomputed on these scored trajectories, and the final experts' distributions are distilled at the logged prefixes. Following the experts' own trajectory reduces the training-inference mismatch and keeps the student within the clip range, so the replay is off-policy in origin but quasi-on-policy throughout consolidation. MRPD samples nothing and calls no environment or reward model: the trainer is the sole process, and consolidating the experts costs only about a seventh of the compute used to train them. On three long-horizon agentic domains (reasoning, search and SQL), MRPD yields the best student model, substantially outperforming online RL baselines, and surpassing MOPD from the same experts at a fraction of MOPD's cost. Our analysis on the same logs shows that the replayed trajectories carry most of the gain, and that both the policy gradient and the distillation term add to it. Most interestingly, the order of the logs matters more than their amount or rewards, suggesting that expert training logs are a curriculum, not a data mixture.

</details>


### [74] [FDM+: A Framework for Constrained Control of the 2D Radiative Transport Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24070)
*Hualing Zhong,Shuai Wang,Hui Xie,Heng Yong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FDM+，通过解耦预测架构、高效参数化策略与条件分布设计空间探索，改进冻结扩散模型在二维辐射传输方程约束控制中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将冻结扩散模型(FDM)用于二维辐射传输方程的约束控制面临三大挑战：控制变量与状态函数维度不一致、传统数值求解器与深度学习模型的分辨率不匹配、以及解的非唯一性。

Method: 三项改进：(1) 解耦预测架构处理维度异质性；(2) 高效参数化策略解决分辨率不匹配，并推导总体误差界，分析参数化误差与模型估计误差对性能的联合影响；(3) 利用扩散模型学到的条件分布进行设计空间探索以应对多解问题。

Result: 系列数值实验表明，FDM+在重建精度与设计灵活性上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: FDM+有效克服了FDM在维度、分辨率与多解性方面的障碍，为辐射传输方程的约束控制提供了兼顾精度与灵活性的方案。

Abstract: Applying the frozen diffusion model (FDM) [29] to the constrained control of the 2D radiative transport equation presents several challenges. These include the dimensional difference between control variables and state functions, the resolution mismatch between traditional numerical solvers and deep learning models, and solution non-uniqueness. To address these obstacles, we propose a novel approach, FDM+, which includes three key improvements. Firstly, a decoupled prediction architecture is introduced to address the dimensional heterogeneity. Secondly, an efficient parameterization strategy resolves the resolution mismatch. Meanwhile, we derive a total error bound to analyze how the parameterization error and the model estimation error jointly affect the performance. Thirdly, the conditional distribution learned by the diffusion model is leveraged to perform design space exploration for multi-solution problems. A series of numerical experiments demonstrate that FDM+ outperforms existing methods in reconstruction accuracy and design flexibility.

</details>


### [75] [VESPA: Verifiable Symbolic Policy Adaptation for Safe Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24083)
*Paul König,Sascha Marton,Stefan Lüdtke,Christian Bartelt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VESPA利用形式验证器给出的反例作为训练信号，对决策树策略进行定向微调，使不安全策略变得安全，显著提升验证安全性效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全强化学习中，策略常违反安全约束，被形式验证判定为不安全。传统做法需从零重训练并反复验证直到偶然通过，训练与验证代价高昂、效率低下。

Method: 提出VESPA框架，对决策树策略进行定向微调。将形式验证的反例（counterexamples）作为训练信号，在反例上微调现有策略，使其学会规避违反安全约束的行为。

Result: 在CartPole上，平均验证安全步数从63.8提升至至少92.7，两轮迭代即达到无counterexample循环十轮的效果；在MountainCarContinuous上，60次运行中53次将不安全策略转为安全策略。

Conclusion: 验证器产生的反例是有效的训练信号，可系统性地提升策略安全性，避免从零重训练的浪费。

Abstract: Safe reinforcement learning (RL) aims to prevent agents from taking actions that are dangerous or destructive to their environment. This is especially important for safety-critical domains, such as robotics or autonomous driving. Formal verification can be used to prove agent safety through safety constraints. However, in practice, many policies do violate safety constraints, and thus are verified to be unsafe. Since the goal is to obtain a safe policy, we would need to retrain a new policy from scratch and then verify it again until a candidate happens to pass. Both training and verification are usually not cheap, which makes this process inefficient. We introduce VESPA ($\textbf{VE}$rifiable $\textbf{S}$ymbolic $\textbf{P}$olicy $\textbf{A}$daptation), a framework for targeted fine-tuning of decision tree policies. We show that using counterexamples for policy adaptation can make existing unsafe policies safe, or at least make considerable progress towards safety. Targeted fine-tuning of the existing policy on those counterexamples enables it to learn how to avoid violating safety constraints. We thus use the result of a formal verification as a training signal for fine-tuning. On CartPole, VESPA raises the mean number of verified safe steps from 63.8 to at least 92.7, reaching after two iterations what the same loop without counterexamples reaches after ten. On MountainCarContinuous, VESPA turns an unsafe policy into a safe policy in 53 of 60 runs. We conclude that counterexamples from a verifier are an effective training signal, which can be utilized to systematically improve the safety of policies. Our implementation is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/VESPA-Submission/

</details>


### [76] [UCLIR: Unsupervised Continual Learning using Independent Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24093)
*Kamel Aouaidjia,Yanchao Duan,Zihui Zhen,Xin Li,Yongfei Lv,Chongsheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 受神经科学"指数距离规则(EDR)"启发，本文提出对多个小型、独立学习的互补表征施加权重正则化，比正则化单一大型共享表征更能缓解持续学习中的灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正则化类持续学习方法通过惩罚项约束对旧任务重要的参数来模仿神经可塑性，但随着任务数量增加，保留早期任务知识的效果下降。主因是新任务更新分布于整个网络，而稠密连接导致参数高度耦合、相互依赖。EDR表明神经连接数随物理距离增加而减少，故学习天然具有局部相关性，区域间更新较少依赖任务。

Method: 将输入分解为多个独立学习的成分，用独立的自动编码器（independent autoencoders）通过无监督正则化分别学习输入的不同部分；新任务仅引发特定表征内的局部参数更新，从而限制干扰。推理阶段使用线性分类器进行评估。

Result: 在5-Splits MNIST、5-datasets序列、5-Splits CIFAR-10 和 10-Splits Tiny ImageNet 等任务序列上取得与监督方法和基于回放（replay）方法相竞争的结果，代码开源。

Conclusion: 对多个小型独立互补表征做权重正则化优于对单一大型共享表征正则化，可有效减少任务间干扰、提升知识保留；将EDR的局部性原理引入持续学习是可行且有效的设计思路。

Abstract: In continual learning, regularization-based methods aim to mimic neuroplasticity by updating network weights to learn new tasks while constraining changes to parameters that are important for previously learned tasks through penalty terms. However, as the number of tasks increases, preserving knowledge from earlier tasks becomes less effective. This is mainly because updates for new tasks are distributed across the entire network, where parameters are highly interdependent due to the dense connectivity between neurons. Inspired by the Exponential Distance Rule (EDR), which states that the number of neural connections decreases with increasing physical distance, learning is naturally locally correlated while inter-area updates remain less task-dependent. Motivated by this principle, in this paper we demonstrate that applying weight regularization to multiple small and independently learned complementary representations is more effective than regularizing a single large shared representation. By decomposing the input into independently learned components, learning new tasks induces only localized parameter updates within specific representation, which limit interference and improve knowledge retention across tasks. We employ independent autoencoders that learn different parts of the input independently through unsupervised regularization. At inference time, a linear classifier is used for evaluation. The experimental results on a sequence of tasks using 5-Splits MNIST, sequence of 5-datasets, 5-Splits CIFAR-10, 10-Splits Tiny ImageNet shows competitive results with supervised and replay-based methods. Code is available.

</details>


### [77] [The 7th International Verification of Neural Networks Competition (VNN-COMP 2026): Summary and Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24114)
*Tobias Ladner,Konstantin Kaulen,Stanley Bak,Julien Girard-Satabin,Taylor T. Johnson,Edoardo Manino,ThanhVu H Nguyen,Ben Wooding,Haoze Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该报告总结了第七届国际神经网络验证竞赛（VNN-COMP 2026）的组织规则、基准、参赛工具与结果，旨在以可扩展性和速度为维度客观比较神经网络验证领域的最新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络验证领域方法众多，缺乏统一、客观的横向比较标准；需要以可扩展性和运行速度为指标，公平地评估各类验证工具的实际能力。

Method: 采用标准化设置保障公平性：神经网络使用ONNX格式、规范使用VNNLIB格式、统一硬件环境（所有工具由组织者在AWS上运行），并使用工具作者提供的参数配置进行评测。

Result: 报告汇总了本届竞赛的规则、基准集、参赛工具及各工具的性能结果，并提炼出竞赛中的经验与教训。

Conclusion: VNN-COMP 2026通过标准格式、标准硬件与作者指定参数的方式，实现了对神经网络验证工具在可扩展性与速度方面的可复现、客观比较，为领域发展提供了参考基准与实践经验。

Abstract: This report summarizes the seventh International Verification of Neural Networks Com- petition (VNN-COMP 2026), held as part of the Symposium on AI Verification (SAIV) co-located with the International Conference on Computer-Aided Verification (CAV) at FLoC 2026 in Lisbon, Portugal. The goal of the competition is to provide an objective comparison of the state-of-the-art methods in neural network verification, in terms of scal- ability and speed. Along this line, we used standard formats (ONNX for neural networks and VNNLIB for specifications), standard hardware (all tools are run by the organizers on AWS), and tool parameters provided by the tool authors. This report summarizes the rules, benchmarks, participating tools, results, and lessons learned from this competition.

</details>


### [78] [QuantAtlas-OM: Taming Neural Spectral Bias in Long-Horizon Spatiotemporal Ocean Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24124)
*Hao Wu,Yuan Gao,Tong Wu,Fan Xu,Fan Zhang,Jinhan Li,Kun Wang,Zhenyu Ma,Bryan Dai,Xiaomeng Huang,Qingsong Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 QuantAtlas-OM，将长时序演化限制在低波数潜流形上以保证稳定，并把全分辨率涡旋/锋面残差通过外接旁路直送解码器，显著缓解自回归长程预测的谱偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归神经网络在长程时空预测中存在谱偏差：随 rollout 推进高频细节衰减，场被模糊化并导致长程预报失稳。海洋动力学的能量集中于数十公里尺度的中尺度涡与锋面，问题尤为突出。其根源是大尺度相位演化与小尺度能量保持被耦合在同一特征空间中——粗网格演化抹平高波数涡旋，细网格演化放大相位误差并引发 rollout 不稳定。

Method: 尺度-谱解耦框架：深层时间演化仅作用于低波数潜流形以保持长期稳定；全分辨率涡旋与锋面残差经外部旁路直接路由至解码器。演化核心将非局部注意力与分辨率不变的膨胀空间门控相结合，适用于不同物理区域。

Result: 在三条西边界流上，Day 120 沿 k^-3 级串保留 43.7%–86.5% 的中尺度谱能量；Day 60 异常相关系数 0.81–0.88，黑潮 Day 120 达 0.66（为最佳基线的 2.5–7.7 倍）。0.25° 训练后零样本迁移至全球 0.125° 网格（Day 10 空间相关 0.81）；在全球 0.25°、23 层三维海洋模拟（120 天）与海气耦合预报（60 天）中，16 项指标 15 项第一，60–120 天盐度 RMSE 降低 13%–27%。

Conclusion: 显式的尺度-谱解耦能有效抑制长程自回归预测的谱偏差，在保持中尺度能量的同时维持 rollout 稳定性，并具备跨分辨率零样本迁移与全球三维/耦合预报的泛化能力。

Abstract: Autoregressive neural networks exhibit spectral bias in long-horizon spatiotemporal prediction. As rollouts progress, high-frequency details decay, blurring the predicted fields and destabilizing long-range forecasts. Ocean dynamics offer a particularly stringent test because kinetic energy is concentrated in mesoscale eddies and fronts spanning tens of kilometers. This degradation stems from coupling large-scale phase evolution and small-scale energy retention in a shared feature space. Evolution on a coarse grid smooths out high-wavenumber eddies, whereas evolution on a fine grid amplifies small-scale phase errors and triggers rollout instability. We introduce QuantAtlas-OM (QuantAtlas Ocean Model), a scale-spectral decoupling framework that confines deep temporal evolution to a low-wavenumber latent manifold for long-term stability while routing full-resolution eddy and front residuals through an outer bypass to the decoder. Across physical regimes, the evolution core pairs non-local attention with resolution-invariant dilated spatial gating. Across three western boundary currents, QuantAtlas-OM retains $43.7\%$ to $86.5\%$ of mesoscale spectral energy along the $k^{-3}$ cascade at Day $120$, sustains anomaly correlation coefficients of $0.81$ to $0.88$ at Day $60$, and reaches $0.66$ at Day $120$ in the Kuroshio ($2.5\times$ to $7.7\times$ the best baselines). Trained at $0.25^\circ$, QuantAtlas-OM transfers zero-shot to a global $0.125^\circ$ grid ($0.81$ Day-$10$ spatial correlation) and leads global $0.25^\circ$, $23$-level 3D ocean simulations ($120$ days) and coupled ocean-atmosphere forecasts ($60$ days), ranking first on $15$ of $16$ metrics and cutting $60$- to $120$-day salinity RMSE by $13\%$ to $27\%$ across global ocean basins.

</details>


### [79] [SoU-RHS: Bayesian Structured Sparsity for Compact Architecture Recovery in CNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24166)
*Rafael Mouallem Rosa,Julyan Arbel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SoU-RHS：将RHS与SoU-SGL结合的贝叶斯结构化稀疏方法，从过参数化CNN中稳定恢复紧凑架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高参数化CNN性能强但内存、计算与部署成本高，需要从大型参考模型中恢复紧凑架构。

Method: 在Regularized Horseshoe Shrinkage (RHS)基础上引入Sources of Uncertainty Sparse Global-Local机制(SoU-SGL)，构成贝叶斯结构化稀疏过程，强化活跃/非活跃滤波器之间的分离。

Result: 在不同过参数化初始宽度与合理剪枝阈值范围内，恢复出的紧凑架构总规模与逐层分配相似；在标准图像分类基准上比确定性结构化稀疏基线更小，精度具竞争力，所有配置NLL更低，ECE总体更低。

Conclusion: 该过程识别的是适配数据的紧凑结构，而非仅对各个初始模型分别压缩。

Abstract: Highly parameterized convolutional neural networks often achieve strong predictive performance, but their memory, computation, and deployment costs motivate the search for methods that recover compact architectures from large reference models. We propose SoU-RHS, a Bayesian structured-sparsity procedure that augments Regularized Horseshoe Shrinkage (RHS) with the so-called Sources of Uncertainty Sparse Global-Local mechanism (SoU-SGL). The resulting model sharpens the separation between active and inactive filters, providing an effective mechanism for architecture recovery. A key property of SoU-RHS is stability: across different overparameterized starting widths and under a reasonable range of pruning thresholds, it recovers compact architectures with similar total size and layer-wise allocation. This supports the interpretation that the procedure is identifying a compact structure adapted to the data, rather than merely compressing each initial model separately. Across standard image-classification benchmarks, SoU-RHS recovers substantially smaller architectures than deterministic structured-sparsity baselines, while maintaining competitive accuracy, achieving lower negative log-likelihood in every evaluated configuration and generally lower expected calibration error.

</details>


### [80] [Adaptive Action Horizon Selection via Flow-Field Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24178)
*Idan Ben-Ami,Arnon Netzer,Sarah Keren,Idit Diamant*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 DUAC：一种无需重训练、利用预训练 VLA 流向量场的后验协方差闭式解，从单次生成的动作块中提取轨迹条件不确定性，用于自适应动作块长度选择，在多个机器人基准上实现高成功率与低不确定性估计延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型需要可靠的不确定性估计以支持自适应执行。现有方法（如 Adaptive Action Chunking, AAC）通过对多个采样的动作块估计动作熵，带来额外推理开销，且混淆了轨迹内不确定性与不同策略间的多样性，因而对有效但不同的动作策略过于敏感。

Method: 基于流后验协方差的闭式表达，DUAC 从预训练 VLA 的流向量场中提取以轨迹为条件的预测不确定性；仅使用单次生成的动作块，通过 Jacobian-vector 乘积计算动作级不确定性分数，无需重训练或多次端点采样，属事后（post-hoc）方法。

Result: DUAC 与 AAC 高度相关，但捕捉不同信息：AAC 衡量采样块间的全局离散度，DUAC 衡量执行轨迹附近的局部不确定性，因此对多样但有效的策略不敏感。在 LIBERO、LIBERO-Pro、LIBERO-Plus 和 RoboCasa 上，基于 DUAC 的自适应视界选择在对比方法中表现较优，兼具高任务成功率与低不确定性估计延迟。

Conclusion: 流向量场可作为机器人决策中高效、以轨迹为条件的预测不确定性来源；DUAC 为 VLA 自适应执行提供了一种低成本且有效的不确定性量化方案。

Abstract: Reliable uncertainty estimates are important for adaptive execution in vision-language-action (VLA) models. Existing approaches such as Adaptive Action Chunking (AAC) estimate action entropy from multiple sampled action chunks, introducing additional inference cost and capturing both uncertainty within an action trajectory and diversity across alternative strategies. We introduce Divergence-based Uncertainty-aware Adaptive Action Chunking (DUAC), a post-hoc method that extracts trajectory-conditioned predictive uncertainty from the vector field of a pretrained VLA. Building on a closed-form flow-posterior covariance formulation, DUAC derives action-level uncertainty scores from a single generated chunk using Jacobian-vector products, without retraining or multiple endpoint samples. DUAC strongly correlates with AAC, while capturing distinct information: AAC measures global dispersion across sampled chunks, whereas DUAC measures local uncertainty around the trajectory selected for execution. Consequently, DUAC is less sensitive to diversity among distinct but valid action strategies. Across LIBERO, LIBERO-Pro, LIBERO-Plus, and RoboCasa, DUAC-based adaptive horizon selection performs favorably among the evaluated methods, combining strong task success with low uncertainty-estimation latency. These results establish the flow vector field as an efficient, trajectory-conditioned source of predictive uncertainty for robot decision making.

</details>


### [81] [Learning with Unlearning: Synaptic Role Partitioning for Simultaneous Continual Learning and Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24205)
*Mohammad Ali Vahedifar,Behzad Bozorgtabar,Qi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LwU 将持续学习与机器遗忘统一到突触级别，通过双重要重打分把网络划分为四个区域并施加不同更新规则，在无需重训练的情况下同时实现保留与删除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习要求模型获取新知识而不遗忘旧知识，机器遗忘要求模型精确销毁指定知识而不损伤其余部分。二者作用于同一组权重，针对一方的编辑会损害另一方，因此难以集成到同一系统中。

Method: 提出 Learning with Unlearning（LwU）框架，在单个突触层面化解冲突。对每个权重分别针对"需保留数据"与"需删除数据"计算两个重要性分数，据此将网络划分为四个区域并施加独立更新规则：仅需保留的权重被冻结；仅需删除的权重被清零并作为自由容量归还网络；争议权重沿与保留梯度正交的投影方向执行梯度上升步；剩余容量通过特权教师的自蒸馏学习新任务。删除为事后完成，无需重训练。

Result: 在持续学习与机器遗忘两类任务上，LwU 达到或超过最强基线；在自建的物理人工智能（PAI）数据集上验证了实际可用性；在图像生成任务及危险内容移除任务中均优于最接近的基线（见 Fig.1）。

Conclusion: 通过在突触级别显式分区并分配差异化更新规则，可在统一框架内同时满足持续学习与机器遗忘这两个相互冲突的目标，且无需额外重训练，具备实际部署可行性。

Abstract: Continual Learning (CL) asks a model to acquire new knowledge without forgetting what it already knows. Machine Unlearning (MU) asks it to destroy specific knowledge without damaging the rest. The challenge is integrating them in the same system; the two objectives pull on the same weights, and an edit made for one harms the other. To address this, we propose Learning with Unlearning (LwU), a unified framework that resolves the conflict where it occurs: at the individual synapse. LwU scores every weight twice, once against the data to keep and once against the data to delete, and partitions the network into four zones with separate update rules. Weights that only require retention are frozen. Weights that only require deletion are cleared and returned to the network as free capacity. Contested weights take a gradient-ascent step projected orthogonally to the retention gradient. The remaining capacity learns the incoming task by a privileged teacher via self-distillation. Deletion is served post hoc, and no retraining is required. Across both CL and MU jobs, LwU outperforms or matches the strongest baseline. Its real-world practicality has been demonstrated on the Physical Artificial Intelligence (PAI) dataset we conducted. Additionally, in the image generation task and in removing dangerous content, it outperformed the closest baseline, as shown in Fig.1.

</details>


### [82] [Continuous Noisy-Loop Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24213)
*An T. Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文刻画了"高效学习与推断"与"紧凑概率电路"之间的本质区别：在方形网格相邻隐自旋的噪声一致性观测模型下，给出极小极大Hellinger风险率Θ(min{1, dN^{-2/3}})，并证明满足该统计效率的估计器不必然对应小规模的分解式概率电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分统计效率（样本复杂度与推断时间）与表示紧致性（概率电路规模）。现有工作常默认"能高效学习/推断"意味着存在紧凑电路，作者希望量化这一区别，明确在何种可靠性条件下需要多大的电路规模，以及KL近似误差的最优阶。

Method: 设定为已知方形网格（d条边）上相邻隐自旋一致性的连续噪声读数；已知边缘分布与有界Lipschitz信道。构造样条估计器并在固定正可靠性下达到极小极大速率；构造指数规模混合模型去除可靠性下界要求。对非负可分解电路给出节点下界，对KL近似给出上下界（叶子作用域ℓ≤αd），并用局部环恒等式把联合散度与可识别信道乘积联系起来。

Result: 极小极大平方Hellinger风险为Θ(min{1, dN^{-2/3}})。样条估计器在多项式算术时间达到该速率（需固定正可靠性下界），指数规模混合可去除该要求，但固定正可靠性下非负可分解电路需要exp(Θ(√d))节点（即使叶子仅用≤αd坐标）。最优KL近似误差为Θ(d/max{log d, √ℓ})，节点数≤d^r、叶子作用域ℓ≤αd（r>1, α<2/3）。三种固定掩码下联合条件KL最优阶为d/log d（ℓ≤(log d)^2）。因式化证据保持平面零场Ising模型结构，给定局部积分时精确查询代价为O(d^{3/2})算术运算加一次开方。

Conclusion: 统计上的高效学习与推断并不蕴含紧凑的概率电路表示；紧致性需要额外结构或指数级规模代价。局部环恒等式将联合散度、可识别信道乘积以及弱信道恢复与密度估计分离开来，为电路规模下界与近似误差刻画提供统一框架。

Abstract: Efficient learning and inference need not imply a compact probabilistic circuit. We quantify this distinction for continuous noisy readings of agreement between adjacent latent spins on a known square grid with $d$ edges. With known margins and bounded Lipschitz channels, minimax squared Hellinger risk is $Θ(\min\{1,dN^{-2/3}\})$ from $N$ independent vectors, without a reliability lower bound. A spline estimator reaches this rate in polynomial arithmetic time given a fixed positive reliability bound; an exponential-size mixture removes that requirement. At fixed positive reliability, nonnegative decomposable circuits need $\exp(Θ(\sqrt d))$ nodes for fixed total-variation error below one, even when each leaf uses at most $αd$ coordinates, $0<α<1$. Optimal Kullback--Leibler (KL) approximation error is $Θ(d/\max\{\log d,\sqrt\ell\})$ in either direction with at most $d^r$ nodes and leaf scopes $\ell\leqαd$, for fixed $r>1$ and $0<α<2/3$. Node counts exclude leaf computation. For three fixed masks, optimal joint conditional KL has order $d/\log d$ when $\ell\leq(\log d)^2$. Local cycle identities relate joint divergence to identifiable channel products and separate weak-channel recovery from density estimation. Factorized evidence preserves a planar zero-field Ising model: given local integrals, exact queries cost $O(d^{3/2})$ arithmetic operations and one root extraction.

</details>


### [83] [Towards Reliable Scaling Laws for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24214)
*Johannes Hog,Neeratyoy Mallik,Steven Adriaensen,Jan Hendrik Metzen,Tom Viering,Danny Stoll,Frank Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究TabPFNv2类表格基础模型（TFM）的缩放定律是否可外推至更大算力并跨评估设置泛化；发现合成与真实尺度行为存在差异，评估数据集过少会导致系统性乐观偏差，且算力最优缩放需同步扩展合成预训练数据规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缩放定律广泛用于预测基础模型性能与指导算力分配，但表格基础模型（TFM）具有特殊性：预训练依赖可配置分布生成的近乎无限的合成数据，而最终在真实数据集上评估。因此需要检验其缩放定律能否可靠外推、能否跨评估设置泛化。

Method: 基于nanoTabPFN开源训练流程，分析TabPFNv2类模型的缩放行为，对比合成数据与真实数据上的尺度趋势，并考察评估数据集数量对配置选择的影响，以及预训练中采样行数与特征数上限、合成预训练数据集规模范围对缩放结论的作用。

Result: 合成与真实世界的缩放行为存在差异；精确拟合缩放定律并不必然给出对未见数据集的可靠指导；使用过少评估数据集选配置会系统性高估性能；算力最优缩放需在模型规模与训练算力之外同步扩大合成预训练数据集规模范围；提高预训练采样的最大行数与特征数可提升性能并使趋势更可预测。

Conclusion: TFM缩放定律的外推与泛化存在局限，评估设计（数据集数量与多样性）和数据规模范围是配置选择与算力分配的关键；这些洞见可用于训练更高效的TabPFNv2类模型。

Abstract: Scaling laws are widely used to predict foundation model performance and guide compute allocation. However, tabular foundation models (TFMs) differ from conventional foundation models in that they are pretrained on virtually unlimited synthetic data from configurable distributions, while ultimately being evaluated on real-world datasets. We investigate whether scaling laws for TabPFNv2-style models reliably extrapolate to larger compute budgets and generalize across evaluation settings. Our analysis reveals differences between synthetic and real-world scaling behavior and shows that accurately fitting a scaling law does not necessarily yield reliable prescriptions on unseen datasets. In particular, selecting configurations using too few evaluation datasets leads to systematically over-optimistic performance estimates. We further demonstrate that compute-optimal scaling requires expanding the range of synthetic pretraining dataset sizes alongside model size and training compute. Increasing the maximum numbers of rows and features sampled during pretraining improves performance and produces more predictable scaling trends. These insights guide the training of more efficient TabPFNv2-like models, using the open-source training pipeline of nanoTabPFN.

</details>


### [84] [TRESS: A Fixed-Size History Representation for Irregular Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24257)
*Shusen Ma,Yangchun Xie,Xudong Zuo,Haizhou Liang,Yun-Bo Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRESS是一种面向不规则多变量时序的预测模型，用原始时间戳保留少量最近观测（8-16条）并配以全历史统计摘要，通过查询条件事件抬升模块与通道特定解码器预测对最新值的修正量，在四个基准上取得最低MSE/MAE并将CPU推理时间降低2.12-2.60倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定数量的"最近观测"在不同变量上可能跨越数分钟到数天，时间口径不一致；而更早的测量仍能反映最新值对该变量是否异常。现有方法难以在记录持续增长时保持恒定大小的表示，并有效利用观测时间间隔与预测时域信息。

Method: 提出TRESS（Timestamped Recent Events and Statistical Summaries）：(1)表示层保留各变量最新若干观测的原始时间戳，并对每个变量全历史做统计摘要，使表示规模在记录增长时保持不变；(2)查询条件事件抬升模块依据观测年龄与预测时域，对相对最新值的偏差进行加权；(3)通道特定解码器将上述特征与历史统计量、跨变量共享摘要结合，输出对最新值的修正预测。

Result: 每个变量仅保留8-16条观测时，TRESS在四个基准上均取得最低平均测试MSE与MAE；单次编码历史后连续服务32个查询时点，四个数据集上CPU推理时间缩短为基线的1/2.12至1/2.60。

Conclusion: 以原始时间戳保留少量近期事件并结合全历史统计摘要的紧凑表示，足以支撑高精度、低延迟的多变量时序预测，且计算成本不随记录增长而膨胀。

Abstract: A fixed number of recent observations may span minutes for one variable and days for another. Earlier measurements can still reveal whether the latest value is unusual for that variable. We propose TRESS (Timestamped Recent Events and Statistical Summaries), a forecasting model that retains the latest observations at their original times and summarises the full history of each variable. For a given set of variables and event budget, this representation stays the same size as the record grows. Its query-conditioned event lifting module weights deviations from the latest value according to the age of each observation and the forecast horizon. A channel-specific decoder combines the resulting features with historical statistics and a summary shared across variables to predict a correction to the latest value. Retaining 8 to 16 observations per variable, TRESS records the lowest mean test MSE and MAE among the methods compared on all four benchmarks. Serving 32 successive query times from one encoded history reduces CPU inference time by a factor of 2.12--2.60 across the four datasets. The code is at https://github.com/SSMa913/NICLab-TRESS.

</details>


### [85] [Element-Wise Femoral Stress Prediction: A Hybrid Graph Neural Network Surrogate for Finite Element Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24274)
*Sikha O K,Abdollah Moslemi,Elham Alizadeh,Ludovic Humbert,Miguel A. Gonzalez Ballester,Jerome Noailly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种GCN-SAGE-Residual混合图神经网络框架，利用图结构有限元数据预测股骨应力，在14个模型对比与消融实验中取得最佳性能（R²=0.8022，MAE=2.879 MPa），为基于FEM的骨科分析与骨折风险评估提供可扩展、低计算成本的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 髋部骨折导致严重残疾与死亡率，随人口老龄化及骨脆性增加而发病率上升。有限元分析（FEA）可评估股骨强度，但计算成本高，限制临床可用性；传统机器学习代理模型虽预测快速，却无法刻画有限元网格的空间与拓扑结构。

Method: 构建基于图神经网络的混合框架用于股骨应力预测，输入为图结构化的有限元建模（FEM）数据。提出GCN-SAGE-Residual架构，融合图卷积、邻域聚合与残差学习，以建模网格单元间的空间依赖与应力传播。开展主对比实验（14个模型，涵盖经典ML方法与基线GNN架构），并进行针对性架构消融，分离各组件的贡献。

Result: 所提混合架构在所有评估模型中预测性能最强，R²=0.8022，MAE=2.879 MPa；消融实验验证了各设计组件的有效性，表明引入网格拓扑信息对生物力学预测任务具有重要作用。

Conclusion: 该框架为基于FEM的骨科分析与骨折风险评估提供了可扩展且计算高效的替代方案，代码已在GitHub公开。

Abstract: Hip fractures represent a significant clinical challenge because they cause excess disability and mortality, and have a growing incidence in aging populations, as bone fragility increases. Although finite element analysis (FEA) provides biomechanical assessment of femoral strength, its clinical applicability is mainly limited by high computational cost. Conventional machine learning (ML) approaches have been explored as surrogate models for FEA; predictions are fast but fail to capture the spatial and topological structure of finite element meshes. To address these limitations, this study proposes a hybrid Graph Neural Network (GNN)-based framework for femoral stress prediction using graph-structured finite element modeling (FEM) data. The proposed GCN-SAGE-Residual architecture combines graph convolution, neighborhood aggregation, and residual learning to model spatial dependencies and stress propagation across mesh elements. We systematically evaluated 14 models in a primary comparison, spanning classical ML methods and baseline GNN architectures, complemented by a targeted architectural ablation that isolates the contribution of each component of the proposed design. Evaluation results demonstrate that the proposed hybrid architecture achieves the strongest predictive performance among all evaluated models. The proposed hybrid model achieves the best performance with an R^2 score of 0.8022 and mean absolute error of 2.879 MPa, highlighting the importance of incorporating mesh topology in biomechanical prediction tasks. The proposed framework provides a scalable and computationally efficient alternative for FEM-based orthopedic analyses and fracture risk assessment. The code is publicly available on GitHub.

</details>


### [86] [Supervised Hebbian Class Networks: Learning Without Backpropagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24314)
*John Loverich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出监督式Hebbian类网络（SHCN），无需梯度计算与反向传播即可训练MLP和卷积网络：每层神经元被赋予类别标签，各层同步训练，激活值直接给出类别预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规避反向传播与梯度计算的计算与生物学合理性代价，探索一种类监督信号驱动的局部Hebbian式学习规则，同时省去独立训练的分类器。

Method: 对学习层中每个神经元分配一个类别标签，连接仍沿用全连接或卷积结构；采用跨层共享的基于类别的监督规则，并结合适配Hebbian活性乘积的Lion优化器；所有层在整个训练过程中联合训练。

Result: 在MNIST上以多层感知机验证、在CIFAR-10上以卷积网络验证，各层激活可直接用于类别预测，无需单独训练的分类器。

Conclusion: 无梯度的监督Hebbian式训练可在MLP与CNN上端到端运行，层内激活即预测，为免反向传播的监督学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces Supervised Hebbian Class Networks as a method for training multi- layer perceptrons and convolutional networks without gradient calculations or backpropa- gation. Each neuron in a learned layer is assigned a class, while connections follow the usual fully connected or convolutional pattern. The method combines a class-based supervision rule shared across layers with a Lion optimizer adapted to Hebbian activity products. All layers train together throughout the run, and their activities provide class predictions di- rectly, without a separately trained classifier. The experiments test the method on MNIST with a multilayer perceptron and on CIFAR-10 with a convolutional networ

</details>


### [87] [Structure-Preserving Neural Approximation of Convex, Semiconvex, and Quasiconvex Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24325)
*Yeachan Park,Yeoneung Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 输入凸神经网络(ICNN)的结构约束可推广到半凸、拟凸与可凸变换函数;在紧凸域上$C^{1,1}$凸函数取得sharp $W^{1,p}$ minimax速率$m^{-1/d}$,并给出有界宽度ICNN的精确表示,拟凸情形构造IqCNN并获得$L^\infty$逼近保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICNN通过架构约束全局强制凸性,但实际目标函数常不满足凸性(半凸、拟凸、可凸变换)。需要厘清"结构保持型逼近"框架能推广到何种函数类、以何种速率逼近,并识别使其成立的机制。

Method: 对紧凸域上$C^{1,1}$凸函数分析max-affine($m$个仿射片)逼近的$W^{1,p}$ minimax误差;构造max-affine函数的有界宽度ICNN精确表示;通过二次提升处理半凸函数、单调复合处理可凸变换函数;对一般Lipschitz拟凸函数建立基于仿射投影单调响应的有限探针定量逼近理论并构造IqCNN;将结果推广至Softplus、ELU等ReLU可迁移光滑激活;用基于PDE的逼近问题做数值对比(参数匹配MLP、验证集调优的形状惩罚)。

Result: $C^{1,1}$凸函数在max-affine逼近下达到sharp速率$m^{-1/d}$,且max-affine函数可由宽度有界、复杂度关于$m$线性的ICNN精确表示;同一$W^{1,p}$速率经二次提升与单调复合分别延伸至半凸与可凸变换函数;Lipschitz拟凸函数获得IqCNN的$L^\infty$逼近保证;结果覆盖ReLU可迁移光滑激活。数值实验显示无需形状惩罚调参即保证全局结构有效性,精度具竞争力。

Conclusion: 可恢复的凸潜结构是使梯度级结构保持逼近超越凸性适用范围的关键机制;ICNN类框架在保持结构有效性的同时具备理论最优的逼近速率与竞争性精度。

Abstract: Input convex neural networks provide a natural way to enforce convexity globally through architectural constraints. We study how this structure-preserving approximation framework extends to semiconvex, quasiconvex, and convex transformable functions. For $C^{1,1}$ convex functions on compact convex domains, we establish the sharp $W^{1,p}$ minimax rate $m^{-1/d}$ under max-affine approximation with $m$ affine pieces, and show that every max-affine function admits an exact bounded-width ICNN representation with complexity linear in $m$. The same $W^{1,p}$ approximation rate extends to semiconvex functions through quadratic lifting and to convex transformable functions through monotone composition. For general Lipschitz quasiconvex functions, we develop a quantitative finite-probe approximation theory based on monotone responses to affine projections and obtain a structure-preserving IqCNN with an $L^\infty$ approximation guarantee. We further show that these approximation results extend to ReLU-transferable smooth activations, including Softplus and ELU. Numerical experiments on PDE-based approximation problems compare the proposed architectures with parameter-matched MLPs and validation-tuned shape penalties, demonstrating global structural validity without penalty tuning while maintaining competitive approximation accuracy. Overall, the results identify recoverable convex latent structure as the key mechanism enabling gradient-level structure-preserving approximation beyond convexity.

</details>


### [88] [Distribution-Free Uncertainty Quantification for Weather Forecasting: Conformal Prediction Under Spatiotemporal Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24329)
*Mariya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文对网格化2m温度预报的8种区间构造方法做基准测试，发现各方法虽能达到接近名义的边际覆盖率，但日间覆盖率的方差是独立假设下的3–10倍且漏报在时间上聚集；这种超出主要源于"整片大误差日"，现有方法无法同时优化过度离散与区间得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测仅保证有限样本、无分布假设的边际覆盖，即某点长期覆盖率，而不刻画给定某天覆盖在时空上的联合行为。作者希望评估：在网格化天气预报中，"边际覆盖达标"是否掩盖了空间与时间上的覆盖依赖结构，并比较不同区间构造方法在此维度上的表现。

Method: 在5.625° ERA5网格上，对2m温度在6小时至10天共8个提前期的预报做基准测试；使用一个刻意简化的卷积预报器和ECMWF业务HRES。2017–2018标定，2019–2021测试。比较8种区间构造（高斯、合并/逐格分裂共形、按季节、ACI、滚动窗口）。除覆盖、宽度、区间得分外，引入依赖诊断：空间过度离散指数、日空间覆盖分位数、相对"打乱天数"零假设的漏报持续性。并构造两类诊断性修正：按日归一化误差的oracle，以及由季节项与已验证误差预测的日尺度因子。

Result: 逐格方法达到接近名义的边际覆盖，但日覆盖率方差为独立细胞情形的约3–10倍，漏报在时间上聚集；两类效应随提前期增长（CNN更明显），HRES亦存在，且为所有方法共有。按日误差均值的oracle可消除CNN 81–92%、HRES 93–98%的过量离散，说明"大面积误差日"是主因。日尺度预测因子在不加宽区间的情况下降低过度离散指数，但区间得分改善至多0.6%；HRES 12小时–3天中，其过度离散最低而ACI或季节条件标定的区间得分最好，无方法两者兼优。

Conclusion: 边际覆盖达标并不意味着覆盖在时空上表现良好；覆盖过度离散与漏报聚集是网格预报中共形区间构造的普遍、随提前期加剧的问题，主要来自日尺度的大范围误差。单一日尺度校准难以同时改善过度离散与区间得分，需在方法设计中显式考虑联合（时空）覆盖。研究限于单一变量、粗网格、再分析目标与两种预报器，外推需谨慎。

Abstract: Conformal prediction gives finite-sample, distribution-free marginal coverage and has begun to be applied to gridded weather forecasts. Marginal coverage concerns the long-run fraction of covered points at a location, not how coverage behaves jointly across space and time on a given day. I benchmark eight interval-construction procedures (Gaussian, pooled and per-cell split conformal, per-season, adaptive conformal inference and rolling windows) for gridded 2 m temperature forecasts at eight lead times from 6 hours to 10 days, using ERA5 at 5.625 degree resolution, a deliberately modest convolutional forecaster and ECMWF's operational HRES, with calibration in 2017-2018 and testing in 2019-2021. Alongside coverage, width and interval score, I report dependence diagnostics: a spatial overdispersion index, quantiles of daily spatial coverage and miss persistence relative to a shuffled-days null. Per-cell methods reach near-nominal marginal coverage, yet the day-to-day variance of the fraction of cells covered is roughly 3 to 10 times what independent cells would give, and misses cluster in time; both effects grow with lead time for the CNN, also appear for HRES, and are shared by all methods. An oracle that divides each day's normalised errors by that day's mean removes 81 to 92 percent of the excess dispersion for the CNN and 93 to 98 percent for HRES, so days of large errors across much of the grid account for most of it. A day-scale predicted from seasonal terms and already-verified errors lowers the overdispersion index without widening intervals but improves interval score by at most 0.6 percent; on HRES from 12 hours to 3 days it has the lowest overdispersion index while ACI or season-conditional calibration has the best interval score, so no method is best on both. The study covers one variable, one coarse grid, a reanalysis target and two forecasters.

</details>


### [89] [OpenGNS: An Open Dataset of the Gradient Noise Scale across Vision, Language, and Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24331)
*Marcel Aach,Jiangtao Wang,Stefan Kesselheim,Andreas Lintermann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 发布OpenGNS数据集（400余条梯度噪声尺度GNS轨迹，覆盖ResNet/GPT/DiT三类架构），并系统研究GNS与超参数、规模、训练进程及临界批大小CBS的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNS可用于近似神经网络训练中的CBS，已见于前沿模型技术报告，但缺乏跨现代架构的公开测量数据集，阻碍了系统性研究。

Method: 构建OpenGNS数据集：图像分类（ResNet）、自回归语言建模（GPT）、图像生成（DiT）三个域，模型规模11M–1B参数，在muP下进行密集批量大小与学习率扫描，共400余条GNS测量轨迹；基于该数据集分析GNS对超参数、规模与训练进程的依赖及其与CBS的关系。

Result: GNS表现为一致的“训练温度”：在muP下等损失时跨模型规模保持稳定；GNS与CBS的关系存在任务相关偏差——分类任务倾向高估CBS，生成任务倾向低估CBS，且该方向在常见CBS定义下一致。

Conclusion: 公开数据集填补了GNS系统性研究的空白，表明GNS是规模稳定的训练指标，但其对CBS的映射具有任务依赖性，需谨慎用于批大小设定。

Abstract: The Gradient Noise Scale (GNS) metric measures the signal-to-noise ratio of gradients in stochastic gradient descent and, as shown by McCandlish et al. (2018), can be used to approximate the Critical Batch Size (CBS) during neural network training. Despite its appearance in technical reports of frontier models, no open dataset of GNS measurements across modern architectures exists, limiting systematic study of the metric. We close this gap by releasing OpenGNS, a dataset of more than 400 GNS measurement trajectories spanning three domains: image classification with ResNets, autoregressive language modeling with Generative Pre-trained Transformers (GPTs), and image synthesis with Diffusion Transformers (DiTs), covering models from 11M to 1B parameters trained under Maximal Update Parameterization (muP) with dense batch-size and learning-rate sweeps. Building on this dataset, we study how the GNS depends on hyperparameters, scale, and training progress, and how it relates to the CBS. We find that the GNS behaves as a consistent "temperature of training", remaining stable across model sizes under muP at equivalent loss, and our results suggest task-dependent biases in its relation to the CBS: a tendency to overestimate the CBS for classification and to underestimate it for generative tasks, consistent in direction across common CBS definitions. The dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/olympique-marcel/OpenGNS

</details>


### [90] [Tell Me How Sure You Are: Communicating Uncertainty in Auxiliary Evidence to Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24351)
*Soumyajit Chatterjee,Baturalp Buyukates,Abhirup Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文首次系统性证明：在提示中向基础模型（LLM/VLM）传递上下文的不确定性信息，可作为有用线索显著提升下游决策性能，宏观F1最高提升26.18个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多数提示方法直接丢弃上下文（如对物理世界的表征）所固有的不确定性，作者认为这类不确定性本身携带对下游决策有益的附加线索，因此需要系统研究"不确定性通信"的价值。

Method: 将决策问题形式化为零样本、由基础模型辅助的图像分类任务，并辅以基于文本的辅助特征；选取该设定是因为其具备定义明确的辅助属性、可量化的关联不确定性以及清晰的正确性判定。使用三个数据集、五个下游基础模型，广泛评估由人类和习得模型在观察或预测辅助证据时产生的不确定性。

Result: 在所有实验设置中，传递不确定性均显著提升下游LLM分类性能，宏观F1相比不传递不确定性信息时最高提升26.18个百分点；进一步分析表明，辅助不确定性中包含关于下游决策正确性的预测性信息。

Conclusion: 不确定性应被视为有用的上下文信息，未来基础模型推理流程应更系统地将不确定性通信纳入设计。

Abstract: In many queries, the context information provided to generative foundation models, such as LLMs and VLMs, is inherently uncertain, including its representations of the physical world. While conventional prompting discards the associated uncertainty, this paper, for the first time in the literature, systematically demonstrates that communicating such uncertainty in context provides useful additional cues for downstream decisions and yields improvements across a wide range of settings.
  We formulate the decision-making problem as a zero-shot foundation model-assisted image classification task supported by auxiliary text-based characteristics. We choose this due to the availability of well-defined auxiliary properties, associated uncertainty, and unambiguous task correctness. Using five downstream foundation models across three datasets, we extensively evaluate uncertainty arising from both humans and learned models when observing or predicting auxiliary evidence. Across all settings, we find that communicating uncertainty substantially improves downstream LLM classification performance: macro-F1 improves up to $26.18$ percentage points compared to when the uncertainty information is not communicated.
  We further observe how the foundation models process such uncertainty information and find that auxiliary uncertainty contains predictive information about the correctness of downstream decisions. Together, these results establish uncertainty as useful contextual information and motivate more systematic consideration of uncertainty communication in future foundation-model inference pipelines.

</details>


### [91] [VAFL: A Vertical Analytic Federated Learning Mechanism for Efficient Crowdsourced Services via Closed-Form Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24367)
*Yajiang Huang,Anfeng Liu,Tian Wang,Mianxiong Dong,Houbing Herbert Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出VAFL纵向分析联邦学习框架，用正则化最小二乘在固定本地表征上拟合分类/回归头，并给出全维等效求解与两种宽度约简策略的代数性质与实验权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能服务依赖多机构互补信息，但无法集中原始数据；需在纵向联邦场景下以低开销方式完成分析与预测建模。

Method: VAFL框架：在固定本地表征上以正则化最小二乘拟合分类与回归头；采用智能体辅助的受保护计算流程，支持两方与多方协作；提出全维等效求解，将分块因子直接作用于目标矩阵，避免构造完整逆；研究两种宽度约简策略——带系数提升的局部谱投影，以及保留协作子空间外局部分量的全维局部拟合加低秩跨方校正。

Result: 建立了两种宽度约简策略的代数性质并分析其计算适用范围；在五个基准上实验，展示精度—开销权衡、与多种冻结骨干网络的兼容性，并确定有效工作点与压缩极限。

Conclusion: VAFL可在不汇集原始数据的前提下高效实现纵向联邦分析建模，全维等效求解降低拟合开销，宽度约简策略在精度与成本间提供可选折中点。

Abstract: Intelligent services often rely on complementary information held by different organizations, motivating Vertical Federated Learning without centralized raw-data pooling. We propose VAFL, a Vertical Analytic Federated Learning framework that fits classification and regression heads on fixed local representations through regularized least squares. An agent-assisted protected-computation workflow supports two-party and multi-party collaboration. To reduce fitting overhead, we develop a full-dimensional equivalent solve that applies block factors directly to the target matrix, avoiding construction of the full inverse. We further study two width-reduction strategies: local spectral projection with coefficient lifting, and full-dimensional local fitting with low-rank cross-party correction. The first compresses the prediction space, whereas the second retains local components outside the collaboration subspace. We establish their algebraic properties and analyze their computational scope. Experiments on five benchmarks show the accuracy--cost tradeoffs of these strategies and compatibility with diverse frozen backbones. Predictive performance and training time identify useful operating points and the limits of compression.

</details>


### [92] [LLM-Assisted Multi-Fidelity Bayesian Optimization: Fusing Expert Knowledge with Numerical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24377)
*Dongjun Han,Mingyu Lee,Ikjin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LLM辅助的多保真贝叶斯优化（LLM-Assisted MFBO），利用大语言模型将专家对低保真模型所忽略物理的定性描述转化为成对判断，经成对高斯过程构建"知识基"，由高保真数据校准其贡献与符号，在锂电池设计问题上将传统MFBO的遗憾AUC降低61%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MFBO依赖低保真（LF）与少量高保真（HF）数据学习LF-HF差异，但HF数据稀缺时差异难以从数值数据中学习，尤其当LF模型省略的物理效应随设计变化（design-dependent）时。而领域专家往往已知LF模型省略了哪些物理、其影响在何种条件下增大，现有MF模型却无法直接利用这类定性知识。

Method: 在常规MF模型上增加"知识基"：由专家描述被省略的物理，LLM判断两个设计中哪一个需要更大的有符号残差修正；用成对高斯过程（pairwise GP）将这些判断转化为知识基函数；再由HF数据校准其贡献幅度与符号，避免盲信知识。

Result: 在锂电池设计问题上，所提方法将传统MFBO的遗憾曲线下面积（regret AUC）降低61%。控制实验独立变化知识质量与LLM选择，表明加速与否取决于知识质量而非所用LLM；知识层面的消融进一步量化了每条知识陈述的贡献。

Conclusion: LLM可解读的专家知识能够作为数值数据之外的信息源，补充MFBO中的数据不足，且在数据校准下不被盲目信任。

Abstract: Multi-fidelity Bayesian optimization (MFBO) reduces engineering design cost by combining inexpensive low-fidelity (LF) models with a few accurate high-fidelity (HF) evaluations and sequentially selecting designs to evaluate. However, when HF data are scarce, the LF-HF discrepancy is difficult to learn from numerical data alone, especially when physics omitted in the LF model makes it design-dependent. Yet even without sufficient data, domain experts often know which physics the LF model omits and under which conditions its effect grows. Existing multi-fidelity (MF) models cannot use such qualitative knowledge directly, but large language models (LLMs) can interpret it and compare designs accordingly. The proposed LLM-Assisted MFBO adds a knowledge basis to the conventional MF model: from an expert description of the omitted physics, an LLM judges which of two designs requires the larger signed residual correction, and a pairwise Gaussian process converts these judgments into the basis. HF data calibrate its contribution and sign, so the knowledge is not trusted blindly. On a lithium-ion battery design problem, the proposed method reduced the regret area under the curve of conventional MFBO by 61%. Knowledge quality, rather than the choice of LLM, determined whether the search was accelerated, as shown by a controlled study that varied the two independently. A knowledge-level ablation further quantified the contribution of each knowledge statement. These results show that LLM-interpreted expert knowledge can complement numerical data as an information source in MFBO.

</details>


### [93] [How to Turn an Attention Neural Network into an Interpretable Hard-Logic Predicate Circuit: Truth-Table Predicate Extraction and State-Machine Merging under Ship-of-Theseus Replacement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24381)
*Yuchen Li,Zongqi Fan,Xintong Li,Nguyen H. Tran,Ken-Tye Yong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将训练好的注意力网络整体（含注意力）转换为硬逻辑谓词电路，逐块替换并让软部分再适应，在TweetEval-emotion上以约8.5个点的精度代价换取无乘加、位精确、FPGA上微秒级的推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低注意力网络的推理成本：消除乘法器与权重搬运，使模型可导出为可综合硬件描述并高效部署于低功耗FPGA。

Method: 以0/1谓词为单元、最小化真值表为映射，把网络转换为硬逻辑谓词电路；短路真值表精确最小化，宽特征转换用容噪最小化转为蕴含规则；自上而下逐块替换（Ship of Theseus），冻结电路后软部分再适应；注意力选择分整数分排序选择与学习规则选择两种，仅在教师分类正确的普通训练样本上拟合。

Result: TweetEval-emotion上DistilGPT2全电路测试金标精度66.5±1.3%（排序选择）/57.4±2.5%（规则选择，3种子），教师74.7%，同数据词片袋64.0%；BERT-base为70.0±0.5%/63.7±1.2%，教师77.8%。电路无需乘法器与权重传输，导出硬件描述位精确复现；综合估算单条推文约1–2 μs（DistilGPT2）、3–5 μs（BERT）于FPGA卡，5–9 μs于7.5–15 W掌上板，比GPU批量1（0.9–2.6 ms）快2–3个数量级，代价为seed-0电路金标精度下降8.5/8.6点。

Conclusion: 注意力网络可端到端蒸馏为可综合的硬逻辑谓词电路，在可观的精度损失下获得数量级的速度与能效优势，适合边缘低功耗部署。

Abstract: We convert a trained attention network as a whole, attention included, into a hard-logic predicate circuit that runs in its place. Every unit of the circuit is a 0/1 predicate on tokens, and every map between predicates is a minimised truth table. Short tables, such as the product of a query code and a key code, are minimised exactly, and wide feature-to-feature transitions become implication rules by noise-tolerant minimisation. Blocks are replaced one at a time from the output down, in the manner of the Ship of Theseus, and after each replacement the remaining soft part re-adapts to the frozen circuit. Attention selection takes two forms, a row-wise comparison of integer scores (order selection) and a learned rule (rule selection). Both are fitted on the ordinary training set alone, and only on the examples that the teacher classifies correctly, so that the circuit imitates the teacher where the teacher is right. On TweetEval-emotion, the complete circuit of DistilGPT2 reaches a test gold accuracy of 66.5 $\pm$ 1.3% with order selection and 57.4 $\pm$ 2.5% with rule selection over three seeds, against 74.7% for the teacher and 64.0% for a bag of word pieces fitted on the same tweets, and that of BERT-base reaches 70.0 $\pm$ 0.5% with order selection and 63.7 $\pm$ 1.2% with rule selection against 77.8%. The circuit needs no multiplier and no weight traffic, and its exported hardware description reproduces it bit for bit. By a synthesis-based estimate it classifies one tweet in about 1-2 $μ$s (DistilGPT2) and 3-5 $μ$s (BERT) on an FPGA card and in 5-9 $μ$s on a palm-sized board whose module draws 7.5-15 W, two to three orders of magnitude faster than the network on a GPU at batch 1 (measured, 0.9-2.6 ms for DistilGPT2 including an optimised CUDA-graph baseline), at a cost of 8.5 (DistilGPT2) and 8.6 (BERT) points of gold accuracy for the synthesised seed-0 circuits.

</details>


### [94] [Temporal Rank in Recurrent Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24394)
*Leo Dobson,Anton Ragni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文指出RNN性能受"时序秩容量"支配：隐藏状态动力学的秩约束造成信息瓶颈，限制了可长期保持的独立输入方向数；作者提出结构化高维状态动力学以维持时序可分性、防止输入-状态雅可比饱和，在受控序列任务上实现快速学习，且严格仍在标准RNN框架内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RNN常因顺序计算不可并行及长程依赖难捕获（通常归因于梯度消失/爆炸）而被弃用。作者认为存在一个被忽视的独立限制：秩受限的隐藏状态动力学带来的信息瓶颈，它通过输入-状态映射的秩限制可随时间保留的独立输入方向数量，且该限制独立于梯度稳定性。

Method: 以输入-状态雅可比的秩作为时序容量度量，理论分析秩约束对时序容量的限制（与梯度稳定性解耦）；进而设计结构化高维状态动力学，保持时序可分性并避免输入-状态雅可比随时间饱和，同时不脱离标准RNN表述。

Result: 在受控序列任务上，该模型虽严格属于标准RNN形式，却表现出快速学习；验证了梯度稳定性与秩保持是两条相对独立的性能通道。

Conclusion: RNN性能由时序秩的演化与保持所决定；仅缓解梯度病态问题而不显式维持时序容量并不充分，需从秩容量角度重新审视RNN设计与分析。

Abstract: Recurrent Neural Networks (RNNs) are a natural framework for temporal modelling, yet are often dismissed due to their sequential, non-parallelisable computation and difficulty in capturing long-range dependencies, typically attributed to vanishing and exploding gradients. We identify a distinct and under-explored limitation: an information bottleneck induced by rank-constrained hidden state dynamics, which limits the number of independent input directions that can be preserved over time, as reflected in the rank of the input-to-state mapping. We show that this constraint restricts temporal capacity independently of gradient stability. To address this, we introduce structured high-dimensional state dynamics that preserve temporal separability and prevent saturation of the input-to-state Jacobian over time. On controlled sequence tasks, the resulting model exhibits rapid learning despite remaining strictly within a standard RNN formulation. Our results demonstrate that RNN performance is governed by the evolution and preservation of temporal rank, and suggest that mitigating gradient pathologies alone is insufficient without explicitly maintaining temporal capacity.

</details>


### [95] [RSIBench-Infra: Can Agents Optimize Their Own Training and Inference Infrastructure at Production Scale?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24395)
*Hanlin Wu,Yifei Wang,Zhicheng Cai,Huan-ang Gao,Wei-Ying Ma,Ya-Qin Zhang,Jingjing Liu,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RSIBench-Infra基准，含20个覆盖多模型族与模态的训练/推理任务，部署规模至32加速器（NVIDIA GPU与Ascend NPU），允许智能体在engine、kernel、instruction层改动软件栈，以OCI镜像层提交并独立评估端到端性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向智能体驱动优化的基准多局限于单一软件层（如kernel），无法说明智能体能否在生产负载下取得端到端性能收益；训练与推理基础设施效率是递归自我改进的实用环节。

Method: 构建RSIBench-Infra：20个跨模型族与模态的训练/推理任务，基于官方recipe提供可运行基线，支持跨engine、kernel、指令级修改；采用OCI镜像层提交协议保证可复现部署，并在任务特定质量约束下进行独立端到端评估。

Result: 全部任务结果中66.2%达到超过1.10倍的经校验加速比。

Conclusion: 当前智能体已能提升生产规模训练与推理性能，但识别有效优化策略、并将局部改进稳定转化为端到端收益仍具挑战。

Abstract: Improving the efficiency of training and inference infrastructure is a practical component of recursive self-improvement. Existing benchmarks for agent-driven optimization largely focus on individual software layers such as kernel, leaving unclear whether agents can deliver end-to-end performance gains under production workloads. We introduce RSIBench-Infra, a benchmark of 20 training and inference tasks spanning diverse model families and modalities, with deployments of up to 32 accelerators on NVIDIA GPUs and Ascend NPUs. Agents start from runnable baselines derived from official recipes and can modify the software stack across engine, kernel, and instruction levels. An OCI image-layer submission protocol captures these changes for reproducible deployment, while independent evaluation measures end-to-end performance under task-specific quality constraints. Across all task results, 66.2% exceed $1.10\times$ validated speedup. These results demonstrate that current agents can improve production-scale training and inference performance, but identifying effective optimization strategies and consistently translating local improvements into end-to-end gains remain challenging.

</details>


### [96] [MINT: Meeting-time Indicators for Truncation in Multi-Step Off-Policy RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24413)
*Seungyub Han,Taehyun Cho,Dohyeong Kim,Kyungjae Lee,Jungwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MINT用二元停止指示器替代逐步迹系数连乘，实现自适应截断，兼顾方差控制与长程信号，无需调参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步离策略TD方法以逐步迹系数连乘处理策略不匹配，导致方差控制与长时程信号之间存在权衡，最大迹长度成为敏感超参数。

Method: 提出MINT：当耦合的行为轨迹与目标轨迹首次相遇时指示器取1、之后取0；真值函数处指示器的幂等性给出由相遇时间存活率控制的方差界。实践中用单次采样动作构造无模型代理停止规则，无需转移模型或混合时间估计；并分别推导状态耦合算子及其代理在固定策略评估下的收缩充分条件。

Result: 在MuJoCo与DeepMind Control上优于所报告的多步基线（MuJoCo主对比统一使用共同上限、无逐任务调参）；在Atari 100k、RWRL、OGBench上亦有效，能自适应备份步长并降低目标波动。

Conclusion: MINT以幂等二元指示器取代迹系数连乘，提供了理论方差界与收缩保证，并在多类基准上稳定提升性能。

Abstract: Many multi-step off-policy temporal-difference methods handle policy mismatch through products of per-step trace coefficients, leading to a trade-off between variance control and long-horizon signal. The maximum trace length consequently becomes a sensitive hyperparameter. We propose MINT (Meeting-time INdicators for Truncation), which replaces these products with a binary stopping indicator: one until coupled behavior and target trajectories first meet, zero thereafter. At the true value function, the indicator's idempotence yields a variance bound governed by meeting-time survival. For practical implementation, a single sampled action provides a model-free surrogate for the stopping rule, requiring no transition model or mixing-time estimate. We derive separate sufficient conditions for contraction of the state-coupled operator and its practical surrogate under fixed-policy evaluation. On MuJoCo and DeepMind Control tasks, MINT outperforms the reported multi-step baselines; the main MuJoCo comparison uses a common cap without per-task cap-length tuning. We also demonstrate the effectiveness of MINT on Atari 100k, RWRL, and OGBench experiments; MINT adapts the backup horizon to reduce target variability relative to long fixed-horizon backups across tasks and dynamics.

</details>


### [97] [Probing Physics in Motion Diffusion Models Against Their Own Estimate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24423)
*Saeed Ghorbani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在运动学文本到动作的扩散模型（MDM 两个 checkpoint 与 MotionDiffuse）中精确计算每个中间估计的物理量，并记录采样轨迹每一层，检验“模型隐藏了物理知识”这一说法。统一调参的读出下，平衡与飞行的层间差距被估计自身闭合（增益≤0.01，正值对照可检出）；唯一明确例外是跳跃的坠落率：早期去噪中 MDM 的层比其自身估计多约 0.08 的 R²（重拟合最高 0.11），随采样推进而消失，MotionDiffuse 上弱且不稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究以“激活比输入/输出更易解码物理属性”来论证视频扩散模型“知道的比展示的多”，但模型正是用同一批激活计算干净样本估计，因此该比较只衡量读出难易度，而非模型是否隐藏信息。作者希望在大统一读出条件下、在可精确计算物理量的运动学扩散模型中，严格检验是否存在真正的隐藏物理知识。

Method: 利用文本到动作扩散模型（MDM 两个 checkpoint、MotionDiffuse）的采样轨迹，逐层记录激活并精确计算每个中间估计的物理量；以上述中间估计和模型自身干净样本估计作为基线，对所有读出（线性探针）采用相同调参方式，并设置正值对照以确认可检出的效应量级；考察平衡、飞行、跳跃坠落率等指标在去噪早期至后期的演化；另在视频模型 Wan 2.1 的干净 latent 上检验加入一个固定非线性后线性探针能否读出此前缺失的合理性。

Result: 与常规基线相比，各层在平衡与飞行上看似领先于模型自身估计；但读出统一调参后差距消失，增益最多 0.01，且正值对照表明该效应量级可被检出。跳跃坠落率为唯一明确例外：MDM 去噪早期各层比估计多约 0.08 的 R²（重拟合最高 0.11），随采样推进而衰减；MotionDiffuse 上效应小且不稳健。模型无需训练即可标记漂浮样本，但中止它们仅节省不到十分之一的算力。在 Wan 2.1 的干净 latent 中，线性探针遗漏的合理性在加入一个固定非线性后变得可读。

Conclusion: 在统一读出条件下，“激活优于估计”的现象基本是读出精调带来的假象而非隐藏知识；运动学扩散模型中隐藏物理知识极为有限（仅跳跃坠落率在早期去噪出现微弱且模型相关的领先）。漂浮样本检测可零训练获得但收益有限，而视频模型中非线性读出能揭示线性探针漏掉的信息，提示“隐藏知识”结论对读出族与模型选择高度敏感。

Abstract: Recent studies report that video diffusion models "know more than they show": physical properties decode better from their activations than from their inputs or outputs. But a diffusion model computes its clean-sample estimate from those same activations, so such a comparison measures how easily a readout extracts the information. Whether the model hides anything is a separate question. We set out to find hidden physical knowledge in kinematic text-to-motion diffusion models, where the physics of every intermediate estimate can be computed exactly, and recorded every layer along the sampling trajectories of MDM (two checkpoints) and MotionDiffuse. Against the usual baselines the layers do look ahead of the model's own estimate on balance and flight. With every readout tuned the same way, the estimate closes the gap: for balance and flight, adding a layer gains at most 0.01, a size that a positive control shows we would detect. The fall rate of jumps is the one clear exception. Early in denoising, MDM's layers add about 0.08 in $R^2$ (up to 0.11 in a refit) that the estimate does not yet show, and the gain fades as sampling proceeds; on MotionDiffuse it is small and not robust. The estimate also flags floating samples without training, but aborting them saves under a tenth of the compute. In a video model, plausibility that linear probes miss in Wan 2.1's clean latent becomes readable after one fixed nonlinearity.

</details>


### [98] [Predicting Process-Level Behavior: A Benchmarking Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24425)
*Michael Grohs,Lukas Kirchdorfer,Jana-Rebecca Rehse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文通过大规模基准测试研究流程级预测任务，发现聚合轨迹级PPM预测会在流程级产生误差累积，流程级预测应作为独立研究任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PPM研究集中于轨迹级（单个流程实例）预测，而流程管理者的决策（如人员配置、KPI计算）发生在流程级，尚不清楚现有方法对流程级决策的支持程度。

Method: 针对两个流程级预测任务——并发轨迹预测与平均吞吐时间预测，在合成与真实事件日志上比较统计方法、机器学习方法、基础模型、业务流程仿真及聚合到流程级的轨迹级PPM方法。

Result: 不同建模范式性能差异显著；聚合轨迹级预测导致误差在流程级累积，降低准确性。

Conclusion: 流程级预测是区别于轨迹级预测的独立任务，需要专门研究；现有轨迹级PPM难以有效复用。

Abstract: Predictive process monitoring (PPM) typically forecasts the future of individual process instances, hence operating on trace level. Process managers, however, typically operate on process level, e.g., when assigning staff or calculating KPIs. The question is how well such process-level managerial decisions are supported by existing approaches, given that PPM research has so far not focused on process-level predictions. We answer this question through a comprehensive benchmarking study of two process-level prediction tasks: concurrent trace prediction and average throughput time prediction. Across synthetic and real-life event logs, we compare statistical and machine-learning-based methods, foundation models, business process simulation, and, importantly, established trace-level PPM approaches, aggregating their predictions to the process-level. We assess which modeling paradigms are most suitable for predicting process-level behavior and whether existing trace-level PPM can be effectively repurposed for this task. Our results show substantial variation in performance across approaches. Notably, aggregated trace-level predictions cause errors to compound on process-level, reducing accuracy and highlighting process-level prediction as a distinct predictive task that warrants dedicated research.

</details>


### [99] [DyNA-Mixer: Decoupling Time and Topology for Scalable Dynamic Node Affinity Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24426)
*Yuanxing Zheng,Liping Wang,Xuemin Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DyNA-Mixer解决动态节点亲和度预测(DNAP)问题：将多尺度时间预测与虚拟协作图结构建模解耦并通过门控残差融合，配合稀疏感知评估，在4个TGB数据集上取得NDCG@10最优，tgbn-token上相对提升17.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究集中于动态链接预测，仅预测未来交互是否发生，而推荐、交易等应用还需交互强度。DNAP将任务推广到连续目标值与全候选排序，更通用但更难：排序要求预测器同时刻画多尺度时间演化与图级协作模式，而对所有源-目标对逐一预测计算代价过高。

Method: DyNA-Mixer解耦时间预测与协作结构建模：1) 多尺度时间预测器建模源-目标对的亲和度演化；2) 构造虚拟协作图，在查询时推断关系并聚合相关交互的趋势；3) 门控残差机制融合两路信息；4) 稀疏感知评估策略，仅对近期非零亲和度的候选对执行昂贵预测。

Result: 在Temporal Graph Benchmark的4个DNAP数据集上，所有数据集NDCG@10均达新SOTA；最大数据集tgbn-token上相对最强基线测试提升17.8%。

Conclusion: 通过解耦时间与结构建模并引入稀疏感知评估，DyNA-Mixer在保证可计算性的同时显著提升连续亲和度预测与全候选排序性能，验证了DNAP设定下该架构的有效性。

Abstract: Continuous-time dynamic graphs preserve the ordering, timing, and evolving strength of interactions that static snapshots discard, making dynamic graph mining essential for evolving systems such as recommender systems, trade networks, and transaction platforms. Research in this area has centered on dynamic link prediction, which forecasts only whether a future interaction will occur, whereas many applications also need its intensity. The dynamic node affinity prediction (DNAP) problem generalizes this task to continuous targets and full-candidate ranking, making it more broadly applicable but substantially harder. This raises two coupled difficulties: ranking requires a predictor expressive enough to capture both multi-scale temporal evolution and graph-wide collaborative patterns, yet running such a predictor over every source--destination pair is computationally prohibitive. We propose DyNA-Mixer, which decouples temporal forecasting from collaborative structure modeling. It adopts a multi-scale temporal predictor for pair-specific affinity evolution, constructs a Virtual Collaborative Graph that infers query-time relationships and aggregates trends from related interactions, and fuses the two through a gated residual mechanism. A sparsity-aware evaluation strategy further restricts expensive forecasting to pairs with recent non-zero affinities. Experiments on four Temporal Graph Benchmark datasets for the DNAP problem show that DyNA-Mixer achieves new state-of-the-art NDCG@10 on every dataset, including a 17.8\% relative test improvement over the strongest baseline on tgbn-token, the largest dataset.

</details>


### [100] [ICICLE: In-Context Inference for Curve-Learning Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24469)
*Laurits Fredsgaard,Michael Riis Andersen,Mikkel N. Schmidt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ICICLE 是一个上下文内代理模型，用共享集合编码器接受可变数量超参数，仅 128 万参数（FT-PFN 的 8.7%），在 LCBench/PD1/TaskSet 上与 FT-PFN 平均后悔相当，并在 86 任务 Meta-Album 上以更低的归一化平均后悔超过 Quick-Tune。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结-解冻贝叶斯优化需预测部分观测学习曲线的后续走势；LC-PFN 将 PFN 用于学习曲线外推，FT-PFN 用十槽超参数编码器扩展到配置感知的冻结-解冻优化，但其槽位固定、配置受限，需要更通用、更轻量的方案。

Method: 提出 ICICLE：上下文内代理（in-context surrogate），通过共享集合编码器处理可变数量的超参数，无需固定槽位；在合成先验上训练，先验覆盖带类型超参数、重尾噪声、量化与发散情形；在线上即时采样合成训练任务。

Result: 参数量 1.28M（FT-PFN 的 8.7%）；在 LCBench、PD1、TaskSet 上平均后悔轨迹与 FT-PFN 相近；在含 33 个超参数的 86 任务 Meta-Album 上，整体及 extended 子集归一化平均后悔低于 Quick-Tune，mini/micro 无显著差异，且未在 Meta-Album 任务上预训练或元训练（Quick-Tune 则在相关任务上元训练）。

Conclusion: ICICLE 以极小参数量和可变超参数支持实现与专用代理相当或更优的冻结-解冻优化效果，并发布合成任务采样代码、Ray Tune 冻结-解冻调度器及无需 JAX/PyTorch 的 ONNX 推理模型。

Abstract: Freeze-thaw Bayesian optimization allocates training budget by predicting how partially observed learning curves will continue. LC-PFN first applied prior-data fitted networks (PFNs) to learning-curve extrapolation, and FT-PFN extended this approach to configuration-aware freeze-thaw optimization with a ten-slot hyperparameter encoder. We introduce In-Context Inference for Curve-Learning Extrapolation (ICICLE), an in-context surrogate that accepts a variable number of hyperparameters through a shared set encoder and is trained on a synthetic prior covering typed hyperparameters, heavy-tailed noise, quantization, and divergence. ICICLE has 1.28 million parameters (8.7% of FT-PFN's count) and produces similar mean-regret trajectories to FT-PFN on LCBench, PD1, and TaskSet. On the 86-task Meta-Album benchmark with 33 hyperparameters, ICICLE reaches lower mean normalized regret than Quick-Tune overall and on extended, with no clear difference on mini or micro. It does so without pre-training or meta-training on Meta-Album tasks, whereas Quick-Tune is meta-trained on related Meta-Album tasks. Our release samples synthetic training tasks on the fly and provides a freeze-thaw scheduler for Ray Tune and an ONNX model for inference without JAX or PyTorch.

</details>


### [101] [Latent Action Policies from Suboptimal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24482)
*Donghyeon Ki,Xiaoyu Chen,Jun Ho Seo,Hayeong Lee,Kyungyoon Kim,Byung-Jun Lee,Li Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究在潜动作空间中进行离线强化学习的问题，发现潜动作结构导致朴素离线RL不可靠，进而推导了策略改进可迁移与潜空间/物理最优一致的条件，并提出LAPIS方法实现目标条件潜策略改进，在多数任务上优于基线，且仅用1%动作标签仍有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Latent Action Models（LAMs）从无动作视频学习控制表征，但潜策略仅用行为克隆训练，缺乏显式策略改进目标。离线RL是自然的策略改进途径，然而标准离线RL方法无法直接迁移到学习得到的潜动作空间：直接对潜动作最大化价值或拟合单峰策略会产生物理执行无法实现的潜动作，且潜表征可能将获得不同奖励的物理控制合并。

Method: 论文首先分析潜空间优化何时有效，推导出策略改进能迁移到解码执行的条件，以及潜最优与物理最优对齐的条件。基于此提出LAPIS：将基于价值的策略改进锚定在观测到的状态-潜动作转移上（grounding），并采用条件流策略（conditional flow policy）建模其多峰结构，用于目标条件潜策略改进。

Result: LAPIS在多数任务上优于基线，在视觉操作任务中提升最明显；在仅有1%动作标签的情况下仍然有效，而竞争方法基本崩溃。

Conclusion: 潜动作空间中的离线RL需要显式处理可实现性与多峰性问题；通过在观测转移上约束价值改进并用流策略表达多峰分布，可使策略改进可靠地迁移到物理执行，并在标签稀缺时保持鲁棒性。

Abstract: Latent Action Models (LAMs) learn control representations from action-free video, but existing pipelines typically train latent policies with behavioral cloning, without an explicit policy improvement objective. Although offline RL offers a natural approach to policy improvement, standard methods do not transfer directly to learned latent action spaces. We find that the structure of learned latent actions makes naive offline RL unreliable: directly maximizing value over latent actions or fitting a unimodal policy can produce latent actions that cannot be realized by physical execution, while the latent representation can merge physical controls that receive different rewards. To understand when latent-space optimization remains valid, we derive conditions under which policy improvement transfers to decoded execution and under which latent and physical optima align. Building on these insights, we propose LAPIS for goal-conditioned latent policy improvement, grounding value-based policy improvement in observed state-latent transitions and using a conditional flow policy to capture their multimodal structure. LAPIS outperforms baselines on most tasks, with the clearest gains in visual manipulation, and remains effective even with only 1% action labels, where competing methods largely collapse.

</details>


### [102] [The geometry of neural network decision boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24557)
*Dylan M Paiton,David L Schultheiss,Matthias Kümmerer,Zac Cranko,Matthias Bethge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出一种直接在高维输入空间中可视化并度量ANN决策边界几何（距离与曲率）的方法，避免投影法失真，并系统分析了边界随子空间规模、对抗训练、架构与训练方法的变化规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ANN虽由清晰的数学运算构成，但其复杂结构使决策过程难以直观理解；传统基于投影的可视化技术会引入畸变，无法真实反映高维空间中的决策边界几何。

Method: 在高维输入空间中直接测量并可视化决策边界的曲率与距离，不依赖降维投影；在多种输入、架构和训练方法下对比分析边界曲率与距离，并考察对抗训练前后数据集样本附近边界距离与曲率的变化。

Result: 随子空间规模增大，边界距离平滑缓慢增长且曲率保持稳定，说明在大尺度连续子空间内决策边界靠近输入样本；对抗训练后，样本附近的边界距离与曲率显著减小；训练方法对边界距离与曲率的影响明显强于架构或测试集精度；结果支持或反驳了若干既有边界行为理论。

Conclusion: 该分析与实证兼顾的方法学为提升网络可解释性与理解奠定基础，可用于后续研究。

Abstract: Artificial neural networks (ANNs) are built using well-understood mathematical operations, yet their complex nature poses significant challenges to intuitively understanding their decision-making processes. This work introduces a method for visualizing and measuring ANN decision boundary geometry directly in the high-dimensional input space, avoiding distortions found with traditional projection-based techniques. We analyze boundary curvatures and distances in diverse inputs, architectures, and training methods. Boundary distances grow smoothly and slowly with consistent curvature as the size of the subspace increases, suggesting that decision boundaries are close to input samples within large, continuous subspaces. We also quantify how much boundary distances and curvatures reduce near dataset samples after adversarial training. Training methodology has a significantly stronger impact on boundary distance and curvature than architecture or test-set accuracy. These findings are contextualized within prior work, supporting or refuting several previously proposed theories of boundary behavior. Our analytically and empirically supported methodology lays the foundation for future studies to improve network interpretability and understanding.

</details>


### [103] [From Activity Recognition to Shift Detection: Variational Wi-Fi Sensing with Evidential Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24596)
*Luca Cotti,Marco Cominelli,Francesco Gringoli,Lance M. Kaplan,Mani B. Srivastava,Trevor Bihl,Erik Blasch,Kara Combs,Daniel O. Brigham,Federico Cerutti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Wi-HAR 采用四个变分自编码器从 Wi-Fi 6 CSI 幅度学习紧凑分布，并以延迟融合分类器结合均值与对数方差，在12类活动上达到96.6%中位准确率，并借助证据深度学习实现部署漂移检测（11个数据集宏平均 ROC-AUC 0.897）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CSI 受人体、接收机位置、采集会话与环境的强烈影响，分类器在部署条件偏离训练分布后仍会输出活动标签，造成静默失效；需要同时利用多天线互补性与可检测的部署漂移。

Method: 多天线架构：四个 VAE 分别从原始 Wi-Fi 6 CSI 幅度学习紧凑潜在分布，延迟融合分类器组合各天线的均值与对数方差；并复用该潜在骨干，基于证据深度学习构造类条件不确定性分数用于漂移检测，不依赖漂移样本做模型选择或校准。

Result: 公开基准12类活动上中位准确率96.6%，单天线独立优化模型为70.8–81.5%；在改变接收机位置、参与者、采集会话或房间的11个数据集上宏平均 ROC-AUC 0.897，在5.2%分布内拒绝率下检出82.5%漂移样本；检测性能随接收机位置差异显著。

Conclusion: 多天线提供同一活动的互补视图并显著提升识别性能；同一潜在表示可兼作不确定性驱动的部署漂移检测，但漂移引发的uncertainty响应并不均匀，需按位置/条件分析；紧凑表示亦支持跨天线变量的浅层决策树可解释分析。

Abstract: Human motion perturbs wireless channels, making commodity Wi-Fi devices a practical platform for device-free activity recognition. Yet Channel State Information (CSI) also depends strongly on the person, receiver position, recording session, and surrounding environment, so a classifier can continue producing activity labels after its deployment conditions have moved away from those seen during training. We propose Wi-HAR, a multi-antenna architecture in which four Variational Auto-Encoders learn compact distributions from raw Wi-Fi 6 CSI magnitudes and a delayed-fusion classifier combines their means and log-variances. On twelve activities from a public benchmark, Wi-HAR reaches 96.6% median recognition accuracy, compared with 70.8-81.5% for independently optimized single-antenna models, showing that the antennas provide complementary views of the same activity. We then use the same latent backbone to detect deployment shifts without using shifted samples for model selection or calibration. A class-conditional uncertainty score derived from Evidential Deep Learning achieves a macro-average ROC-AUC of 0.897 across eleven datasets that change receiver position, participant, recording session, or room, and detects 82.5% of shifted samples at a realized 5.2% in-distribution rejection rate. Detection performance nevertheless varies substantially across receiver positions, showing that the same physical change does not induce a uniform uncertainty response. The compact representation also supports shallow decision-tree analysis using variables from several antennas.

</details>


### [104] [Morpho-Spectral Projection Analysis (MSPA) for Plant Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24599)
*Lucas Prado Osco,Marcelo Santos da Silva,Filipe Vieira Silva,Liang Zhao,Ana Paula Marques Ramos,Ednaldo Jose Ferreira,Roberto Dantas de Medeiros,Amaury Burlamaqui Bendahan,Lucio Andre de Castro Jorge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Morpho-Spectral Projection Analysis（MSPA）监督降维算子，将1580波段连续光谱压缩96%，在8,963条光谱、5类植物系统、6种经典ML与4种Tabular Foundation Models上，宏平均F1由全光谱0.7756提升至0.8520，推理最高加速126.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可见光—近红外—短波红外植物反射光谱承载叶片生化与解剖性状信息，但波段间极端共线性与低样本-特征比使预测建模困难；传统光谱降维方法具有列置换不变性，将相邻波长视为无序表格列，丢弃了吸收特征的序列微分几何结构。

Method: MSPA为监督降维算子，含三个互补模块：(i) 加性连续统去除后的吸收深度、面积与红边拐点运动学特征；(ii) 在连续辐射子窗口上分区提取的局部多阶奇异本征模（0/1/2阶导数）；(iii) 经迹归一化各向同性协方差收缩正则化的全局Fisher加权典型子空间。

Result: 在5类植物系统共8,963条光谱上，结合6种经典机器学习分类器与4种表格基础模型（TabPFN v3、TabICL v2、TabDPT v1.2、TabFM）验证；压缩96%波段情况下，MSPA在92.0%的基准中优于全光谱、在90.0%的模型-作物评估中优于常规降维器，全局宏平均F1由0.7756升至0.8520，上下文推理最高加速126.5倍。

Conclusion: MSPA作为一种自包含的光谱投影算子，可直接嵌入光谱学工作流，开源代码已发布于GitHub（https://github.com/Lucas-Osco/mspa）。

Abstract: Plant reflectance spectroscopy across the visible, near-infrared, and shortwave infrared domain encodes foliar biochemical and anatomical traits into contiguous spectral signatures with locally differentiable structure; however, extreme inter-band collinearity and unfavorable sample-to-feature ratios challenge predictive modeling. Conventional dimensionality reduction methods routinely adopted in spectroscopy, are column-permutation invariant: they treat adjacent wavelengths as unordered tabular columns and discard the sequential differential geometry of absorption features. Here, we propose Morpho-Spectral Projection Analysis (MSPA), a supervised dimensionality reduction operator that projects contiguous spectra into a compact morpho-spectral subspace through three complementary blocks: (i) additive continuum-removed absorption depth, area, and red-edge inflection kinematics; (ii) local multi-order singular eigenmodes (0th, 1st, and 2nd derivatives) partitioned across contiguous radiative sub-windows; and (iii) global Fisher-weighted canonical subspaces regularized via trace-normalized isotropic covariance shrinkage. We validated MSPA across 8,963 spectra spanning five plant systems, coupled with six classical machine learning classifiers and four Tabular Foundation Models (TabPFN v3, TabICL v2, TabDPT v1.2, and TabFM). Despite compressing the 1,580-band spectrum by 96%, MSPA outperformed the full spectrum in 92.0% of all benchmarks and conventional reducers in 90.0% of all model-crop evaluations, raising the global mean macro-F1 from 0.7756 (full spectrum) to 0.8520, accelerating in-context inference by up to 126.5x. Designed as a self-contained spectral projection operator, the MSPA method can be directly integrated into spectroscopy workflows via the open-source code available at GitHub: https://github.com/Lucas-Osco/mspa.

</details>


### [105] [Evaluating the Effectiveness of Location Encoding Methods for Species Distribution Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24622)
*Christian Lange,Oisin Mac Aodha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出一个标准化评估框架，量化比较不同位置编码在物种分布范围估计中的效果，覆盖局部与全球尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物种空间分布理解是生态学核心问题；现有方法（统计、传统机器学习到深度学习）多聚焦模型选择，而"何种输入特征最具信息量"这一问题相对独立且缺乏系统评估，尤其近期可选特征来源（气候变量、学习式编码、地球嵌入模型、原始变换坐标）大幅扩展。

Method: 构建标准化评估框架，以稀疏物种观测为输入、稠密空间范围为输出，在同一基准下对比多种位置编码方式（气候变量、学习式编码、地球嵌入模型、原始变换地理坐标），分别在局部尺度和全球尺度进行评测。

Result: 以摘要所含信息为限：框架实现了对不同位置编码有效性的量化比较，并揭示了编码效果在局部与全球尺度上的差异表现。

Conclusion: 输入位置编码对物种分布范围估计具有显著影响；标准化评估有助于公平比较各类编码，为后续特征选择与模型设计提供依据。

Abstract: Understanding the spatial distribution of species is a key question in ecology. A variety of solutions, from statistical, traditional machine learning, through to deep learning-based methods have been proposed. These methods take sparse observations for a species of interest as input and aim to generate dense spatial outputs indicating the spatial range of the species. Orthogonal to the choice of statistical model used is the question of what input features are most informative. Thanks to recent developments, the number of options has greatly expanded, with choices spanning different climate variables, learning-based encodings, earth embedding models, or raw transformed geographic coordinates. In this work, we introduce a standardized evaluation framework and quantify the effectiveness of different location encodings in the context of species range estimation, both at the local and global scale.

</details>


### [106] [Transferring an analysis of human brain organization to language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24626)
*Donghyeok Lee,Sangwon Yu,Sangkwon Park,Hye Youn Park,Munyoung Chang,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将用于刻画大脑皮层组织的扩散映射嵌入应用于三组基础模型与推理训练模型，发现梯度轮廓总体保守、仅存在局部变化，并据此定位出一段可迁移的"推理相关区域"，将其权重移植到指令微调宿主中可提升数学推理且对原有能力干扰较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
