Motivation: 人类大脑皮层发育过程中功能系统逐渐分化，作者据此类比提问：语言模型推理能力的提升是否也伴随类似"区域化重组"？即能否在大模型内部定位出与推理能力对应的特定区域。

Method: 采用常用于表征皮层组织的扩散映射嵌入（diffusion-map embedding），对三个模型族中相互匹配的基础模型与推理训练模型进行表征梯度分析；在此基础上定义连续的"层段"（span），将跨模型训练不同阶段、不同区域的权重移植（grafting）到指令微调宿主模型中，并以区域重叠度、数学题求解率与已解题保持率为指标评估效果；最后将同一分析流程推广到医学领域模型以检验可迁移性。

Result: 梯度轮廓总体保持、变化局限于一个连续层段；从该层段移植权重平均可提升数学推理，增益随区域重叠度上升，且较其他区域移植更能保留此前已解出的题目；该层段在性能早期上升阶段逐步形成，来自中间训练阶段的移植同样有效；移植使多数宿主部分向供体行为偏移，偶尔还能解决供体自身失败的题目；在医学模型中亦定位到可迁移层段。

Conclusion: 研究识别出一个与推理相关的内部区域，其权重可在较低干扰下提升宿主模型的推理表现而基本不损害既有数学能力，为"神经科学式"模型表征分析与跨模型能力迁移提供了实证依据。

Abstract: During human cortical development, functional systems become increasingly differentiated. We asked whether improved reasoning in language models involves a comparable regional reorganization. Applying diffusion-map embedding, used to characterize cortical organization, to matched base and reasoning-trained models from three families, we found broadly preserved gradient profiles with localized changes within a contiguous layer span. Grafting weights from these spans into instruction-tuned hosts improved mathematical reasoning on average, with gains rising with region overlap, while retaining more previously solved problems than grafts elsewhere. Across training, the span formed gradually during the early rise in performance, and grafts from intermediate stages helped. Grafts shifted most hosts partly towards their donors' behaviour and sometimes improved on problems the donors failed; the same analysis located a transferable span in a medical model. Together, these findings identify a reasoning-related region whose weights improve reasoning with low interference in the host's existing mathematical competence.

</details>


### [107] [Choosing the Right Representations: Task-Aware Fusion for Multimodal Materials Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24641)
*Huu Binh Ta,Alex Morehead,N. Benjamin Erichson,Ferdinando Fioretto,Sandeep Madireddy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TCAF：一种轻量级、任务条件化的注意力桥接模块，自适应融合多个材料结构编码器（CHGNet与Zatom-1中间层特征）与LLM对齐，无需独立对比预训练，性能优于MatterChat。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有材料科学多模态语言模型（如MatterChat）依赖大型桥接模块与独立的对比对齐阶段来接入三维结构表示，缺乏对不同编码器及不同深度表示的按任务自适应选择能力。

Method: 先实证分析：发现监督式与生成式材料编码器信息互补，且Zatom-1中最具信息量的表示随目标性质分布在不同的中间层而非末层。据此提出Task-Conditioned Attention Fusion（TCAF）：用任务条件化的潜在查询（latent queries）以注意力机制融合CHGNet特征与Zatom-1中间层表示；桥接模块直接在下游预测任务上训练，联合学习表示选择与材料-语言对齐，无需单独对比预训练。

Result: 在分类与回归任务上TCAF均优于MatterChat，RMSE最多降低47.8%，同时可训练参数显著更少、总训练时间减半。引入UMA-S后验证了跨编码器组合的灵活性，三编码器配置取得最高宏平均分类准确率89.61%。

Conclusion: 多结构编码器的按任务自适应、多层次融合是一种参数高效且性能更优的材料-语言桥接范式，优于固定大模块加对比对齐的既有方案。

Abstract: Multimodal language models for materials science rely on effective mechanisms for incorporating three-dimensional structural representations. MatterChat addresses this challenge using a large bridge module between a pretrained structural encoder and the LLM, together with a separate contrastive alignment stage. We instead ask whether this bridge can adaptively select and combine representations from multiple structural encoders according to the target property. We find that supervised and generative material encoders provide complementary information for property prediction. We found that, for Zatom-1, this information is not concentrated in the final layer. Instead, the most informative representations occur at different depths depending on the target property. This motivates Task-Conditioned Attention Fusion (TCAF), a lightweight attention-based bridge that uses task-conditioned latent queries to combine CHGNet features with intermediate Zatom-1 representations. The bridge is trained directly on downstream prediction tasks, allowing representation selection and material-language alignment to be learned jointly without separate contrastive pretraining. Across classification and regression tasks, TCAF outperforms MatterChat, with up to 47.8% lower RMSE, while using substantially fewer trainable parameters and halving the overall training time. Further experiments incorporating UMA-S demonstrate TCAF's flexibility across encoder combinations, with the three-encoder configuration achieving the highest macro-average classification accuracy of 89.61%.

</details>


### [108] [Equal Steps, Unequal Curvature: Where Muon's Update Goes Wrong](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24644)
*Biswa Sengupta,Jinhua Wang,Léo Maxime Brunswic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文用留出集梯度与Hessian-向量积，将Muon的更新按奇异值带与神经元分组分解，计算各组二阶最优乘子，发现乘子从顶部奇异方向向尾部单调上升——Muon在谱的高曲率顶部过步。据此提出分离正交化Q/K/V，并在冷却期施加σ^{-1/4}谱倾斜，在124M与355M参数规模上稳定降低损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon以动量的极因子方向更新，隐式固定了两种与损失无关的分配：各奇异方向等步长、非方阵中各神经元步长由其杠杆分数决定。这些选择是否合理属于曲率问题，最速下降论证无法回答，因此需要直接测量。

Method: 在124M参数、FineWeb-Edu训练的decoder上，用留出梯度与Hessian-vector products，按奇异值带和神经元组分解Muon步长，计算每组的二阶最优乘子；并用Lean形式化证明何种诊断条件可在Muon自身更新范数下支持干预。随后在每步范数匹配、调优基线与配对随机种子下测试干预：分块（Q/K/V分别）正交化、仅matmul的旋转等变谱倾斜σ^{-1/4}（冷却期施加）、NorMuon、能量匹配的Schatten-1权重衰减，以及若干不与实际误校准作用的原则性改动作为消融。

Result: 乘子从顶部奇异方向到尾部单调上升，Muon过步于高曲率顶部；准则预测且留出损失证实：将步长从顶部移开在训练中期与冷却早期有益，冷却末期无效。Q/K/V分别正交化降低0.0250 nats，并解释了分块变体的全部增益；σ^{-1/4}倾斜在更强基线上再降0.0120 nats，与NorMuon叠加（合计0.0158，三种子；基线需多约4.3% token才能追平），355M规模下降0.0188 nats（三种子）；能量匹配Schatten-1权重衰减再降0.0052 nats。精确极因子、最速下降输入白化、顶部加权衰减无帮助。

Conclusion: Muon的固定步长分配在高曲率谱顶部造成可测量的误校准，而针对该误校准设计的干预（分块正交化、σ^{-1/4}谱倾斜、能量匹配衰减）在范数匹配与调优基线下仍获得可复现的损失下降；仅具几何原则性但不作用于该误校准的改动则无效。诊断—干预的对应关系是筛选优化的关键依据。

Abstract: Muon updates each hidden weight matrix along the polar factor of its momentum, the steepest-descent direction under the spectral norm. That choice fixes two allocations without consulting the loss: every singular direction of the momentum receives the same step, and in a non-square matrix each neuron's step is set by its leverage score. Whether they are good is a question about curvature, which steepest-descent arguments do not answer; we measure it. Using held-out gradients and Hessian-vector products, we decompose Muon's step by singular-value band and by neuron group and compute each group's second-order optimal multiplier. In a 124M-parameter decoder trained on FineWeb-Edu, the multipliers rise monotonically from the top singular directions to the tail: Muon over-steps the high-curvature top of its spectrum. We prove in Lean when such a diagnostic licenses an intervention at Muon's own update norm. On the measured curvature, the criterion predicts, and held-out losses confirm, that moving step length off the top helps mid-training and early in the cooldown, but not at its end. We then test interventions under per-step norm matching, tuned baselines and paired seeds. Orthogonalizing Q, K and V separately rather than as one fused matrix lowers the loss by 0.0250 nats and accounts for all of the gain of block-wise variants. A matmul-only, rotation-equivariant spectral tilt $σ^{-1/4}$ applied in the cooldown lowers the loss of this stronger baseline by a further 0.0120 nats, additively with NorMuon (0.0158 nats together, three seeds; the baseline needs about 4.3% more tokens to match), and by 0.0188 nats at 355M parameters (three seeds). Energy-matched Schatten-1 weight decay adds a further 0.0052 nats. Geometrically principled changes that do not act on the measured miscalibration, namely exact polar factors, steepest-descent input whitening and top-heavy decay, do not help.

</details>


### [109] [Zonotopic Analysis of Quantization Error in Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24648)
*Robert Debbas,Éric Goubault,Sylvie Putot*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于zonotope（及约束zonotope）的抽象解释框架验证量化ReLU前馈网络，引入统一处理round与clamp的抽象变换器，并证明其可靠性、仿射带最优性与凸包最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化神经网络通过降低精度提升计算效率，但离散舍入与截断（clamp）操作使量化误差的界定（bounding）困难，亟需可靠的量化误差形式化表征与验证方法。

Method: 构建zonotope抽象解释框架，提出统一的round-clamp抽象变换器以联合处理舍入与截断；扩展到约束zonotope（加入线性约束以收紧界）并推导可靠的量化误差表示；理论上证明变换器的可靠性、仿射带最优性，以及完整约束形式的凸包最优性。

Result: 在ACAS Xu基准上，约束zonotope方法在最小区间扰动半径（r=3、r=6）下相比QEBVerif的符号DRA传播平均误差界宽度降低约70-85%，运行时间减少65-80%；提升在不同位宽间不一致，较大半径下符号DRA界更紧；普通zonotope方法平均快约300倍且界具竞争力。

Conclusion: 统一round-clamp抽象变换器与约束zonotope可有效收紧量化网络验证的误差界并显著提速，但优势依赖位宽与扰动半径，普通zonotope提供高效折中方案。

Abstract: Quantized neural networks achieve computational efficiency by reducing precision, but bounding their quantization error remains a challenge due to discrete rounding and clamping operations. We present a zonotope-based abstract interpretation framework for quantized ReLU feed-forward network verification, introducing a unified round-clamp abstract transformer that jointly handles both operations. We extend this framework to constrained zonotopes, which add linear constraints to tighten bounds, and derive sound quantization error representations. We establish soundness and affine-strip optimality of the round-clamp abstract transformer, as well as convex-hull optimality of its complete constrained formulation. On the ACAS Xu benchmarks, our constrained zonotope method reduces average error-bound widths by approximately 70-85% and runtimes by 65-80% compared to QEBVerif's symbolic DRA propagation at the two smallest tested perturbation radii (r=3 and r=6). These improvements are not uniform across bitwidths, and symbolic DRA achieves tighter bounds at larger radii. Our plain zonotope method delivers competitive sound bounds nearly 300 times faster on average.

</details>


### [110] [Stealing Exact Training Data from Other Clients during Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24689)
*Omkar Shrikanth,Soham Dambalkar,Parag Jyoti Pal,Advik Raj Basani,Arnab K. Paul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种迭代式梯度反转攻击流程，通过逐步剥离已精确恢复句子的梯度贡献，首次实现联邦学习中文本的精确句子恢复（100%精确率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FedAvg将众多客户端的梯度贡献纠缠在一起，掩盖了单个句子的信号，导致恶意客户端此前无法实现文本的精确恢复。

Method: 设计一条新的攻击流水线：在迭代循环中，将某一轮已精确恢复句子的梯度贡献从聚合梯度中减去，从而"解缠"剩余句子，使其在下一轮迭代中暴露并可被恢复。

Result: 首次实现精确句子恢复，精确率达100%；在包含令牌数达模型隐藏维度4倍的聚合上，可精确恢复高达80.2%的客户端句子，而现有攻击在该情形下无法精确恢复任何内容。

Conclusion: 梯度反转在文本场景下可达到精确句子级恢复，且能扩展到更大令牌规模的聚合，显著超越现有方法，凸显联邦学习文本更新的隐私风险。

Abstract: Malicious adversaries can employ gradient inversion attacks to reverse-engineer private data using the model updates of a round in Federated Learning. While there have been developments in gradient inversion for text, exact text recovery by a malicious client has so far remained infeasible, as FedAvg entangles the gradient contributions of many clients and obscures the signal of any individual sentence. We introduce a novel attack pipeline that unwinds this entanglement progressively through an iterative loop, in which the gradient contributions of sentences recovered exactly in one round are subtracted from the aggregate, exposing the remaining sentences for recovery in the next iteration. We show, for the first time, exact sentence recovery, with 100% precision, and on aggregates containing up to $4\times$ more tokens than the model's hidden dimension, we recover up to 80.2% of client sentences exactly, a regime where existing attacks recover none exactly.

</details>


### [111] [Openhagen Worlds: Calibrated Environments for Measuring and Training Scientific Discovery Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24711)
*Alexander Boesgaard Lorup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Openhagen Worlds：一套 27 个程序化生成环境、178 个难度层级的实验推理基准，通过隐藏真值+确定性验证器+严格校准（oracle≥0.75、最强非演绎策略≤0.25、捷径≤0.35）将"设计实验并推理"的能力与记忆、隐含评分规则和捷径策略分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学发现基准无法将"选择信息量高的实验并从结果中推理"这一核心技能与记忆检索、未明示的评分规则、捷径策略区分开，导致评测结果不可解释、易被刷分。

Method: 构建 27 个程序化生成环境（部分为有限真值空间），覆盖经典实验推理谜题到基于模拟器的物理/化学；智能体在预算约束下做实验并提交答案，由确定性验证器对照隐藏真值判分。所有观测均声明完整评分规则。178 个难度层级均经校准：在 64 个开发实例上要求脚本 oracle 成功率≥0.75、最强可行的非演绎策略≤0.25、各受限捷径≤0.35。92 个核心层级覆盖全部 27 个族并适配小模型训练；86 个前沿层级在 15 个发现类世界中加难，并引入同一隐藏系统上 3/5/8+ 阶段的连续实验战役。

Result: 在 84 个留出的前沿实例（每族每级各一）上，DeepSeek V4.1 Flash 在默认推理强度（每次调用 16,384 token）解出 32 个，最高推理强度（最多 393,216 token）解出 57 个，oracle 为 79；剩余差距最大的是八阶段战役。On-policy expert iteration 使 9B 模型在核心层训练族上的成功率从 0.090 提升至 0.148（部分来自动作格式改进），且在 GPQA Diamond 上无可检测的性能损失。

Conclusion: 该基准证明：经严格校准的程序化环境能有效隔离并度量实验设计与推理能力；当前强模型在前沿层级（尤其多阶段战役）仍有显著提升空间，且小模型可通过 on-policy expert iteration 获得小幅增益而不损害通用科学知识表现。

Abstract: Agents that do science must choose informative experiments and reason from their results, but most discovery benchmarks cannot separate that skill from recall, unstated scoring rules or shortcut policies. We introduce Openhagen Worlds, a suite of 27 procedurally generated environments (some with finite truth spaces), from classic puzzles of experimental reasoning to simulator-grounded physics and chemistry, in which an agent runs experiments under a budget and commits an answer that a deterministic verifier checks against a hidden truth. Every observation states the complete scoring rule, and each of the 178 difficulty levels is calibrated: it is admitted only if, on 64 development instances, a scripted oracle succeeds on at least 0.75, the strongest plausible non-deductive policy on at most 0.25 and each gated shortcut on at most 0.35. The 92 core levels span all 27 families and are sized to train small models; the 86 frontier rungs extend the 15 discovery worlds with harder questions and with campaigns of three, five and eight or more committed stages on one hidden system. On the same 84 withheld frontier instances, one per family and rung, DeepSeek V4.1 Flash solves 32 at its default reasoning effort with 16,384 tokens per call and 57 at its maximum effort with up to 393,216 tokens per call (oracle: 79); the headroom that remains is largest in eight-stage campaigns. On-policy expert iteration gives a 9B model modest gains: its core-tier success on trained families rises from 0.090 to 0.148, partly through better action format, with no detectable loss on GPQA Diamond. A hosted service plays the core tier's withheld instances.

</details>


### [112] [Meta-LSQ: A Closed-Form Sobolev Inner Solver for Few-Shot Meta-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24718)
*Antonin Sulc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Meta-LSQ：在固定随机 Fourier 基上用一次正则化最小二乘显式拟合每个任务，仅元学习带宽、岭系数与导数权重三个标量，可获得任意阶闭式导数；在若干回归与束流调节任务上训练快、精度高，但在真实传感器校准与非光滑目标上不及严格 GP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MAML 类梯度元学习器需要内循环梯度下降并对整个内循环求导，且要元学习全部网络权重，计算与实现代价高；希望以更少可学习参数、无需二阶/一阶微分内循环的方式实现快速任务自适应，同时天然支持导数观测与导数输出。

Method: 以固定随机 Fourier 特征构成基，对每个任务求解一次带岭正则的最小二乘闭式解；元学习仅三个标量（带宽、ridge、导数权重）。因正弦的导数仍为正弦，导数观测可并入同一线性系统，拟合模型具有任意阶精确闭式导数。另对先验均值进行学习以处理真实数据；用广义交叉验证等样本内准则选择超参。

Result: 10-shot 正弦回归：训练 14 s，误差比训练时长 36 倍的一阶 MAML 基线低 5.5 倍，GP 对照显示增益主要来自跨任务共享单一核。DESY ARES 仿真束流调节（含模型导数）略优于 ALPaCA 型模型、明显优于梯度下降；全目标束范围内最优设置中位误差 7.3 μm，对比其 RL 智能体已发表最终束的 14.4 μm（但在其 300 次小目标试验中 RL 中位误差更低）。真实传感器校准：学习先验均值使其在 2/4 个目标上优于 MAML，但同设置的精确 GP 与 ALPaCA 型模型在多数目标上更准，且非光滑目标上失效。

Conclusion: 将元学习简化为对少量标量超参的调节、并以随机 Fourier 基上的闭式最小二乘替代梯度内循环，可在保持导数闭式可用的同时获得显著训练效率与精度优势；但其优势依赖于平滑性假设与核共享，在真实数据、非光滑目标及基张成范围外的无噪目标上（GCV 等样本内准则会低估 ridge）存在明显局限。

Abstract: Gradient-based meta-learners such as model-agnostic meta-learning (MAML) adapt to a new task with an inner loop of gradient descent that has to be differentiated through, and they meta-learn every network weight. Meta-LSQ instead fits each task with one regularised least-squares solve over a fixed random Fourier basis and meta-learns only three scalars, a bandwidth, a ridge and a derivative weight. Since the derivative of a sinusoid is again a sinusoid, the same solve can also take derivative observations, and the fitted model has exact closed-form derivatives of any order. On 10-shot sinusoid regression Meta-LSQ trains in 14 s and ends with a 5.5 times lower error than a first-order MAML baseline that trains 36 times longer, and a Gaussian-process control suggests that most of this gain comes from sharing one kernel across tasks. In simulated beam tuning at DESY's ARES facility with model derivatives it is slightly more accurate than an ALPaCA-style model and well ahead of gradient descent. Over DESY's full range of target beams, again in simulation and from measurements alone, its best setting has a median error of 7.3 $μ$m, against 14.4 for the published final beams of their reinforcement-learning agent, which has the lower median on their 300 trials of small targets. On real sensor-calibration data a learned prior mean puts it ahead of MAML on two of four targets, but an exact Gaussian process with the same change and an ALPaCA-style model stay more accurate on most targets, and it breaks down on non-smooth targets. On noiseless targets outside the span of the basis, in-sample criteria such as generalised cross-validation choose too small a ridge.

</details>


### [113] [How Generative Models Generate: A Concise Guide to the Generative AI Landscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24728)
*Tianhua Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"以生成为中心"的两层框架来梳理生成式AI方法：第一层区分三条反复出现的生成路径（直接变换/解码、顺序条件构造、迭代更新/传输），第二层剖析支撑这些路径的建模与学习原理，并通过代表性方法的关键组件、学习目标与采样过程加以说明，进而讨论架构与生成机制之间的重叠与组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI领域快速扩张，各类方法在架构、学习目标、数学表示和采样过程上差异显著，现有文献庞杂分散，初学者难以形成连贯理解，缺乏一份简明、有选择性地组织的技术指南作为起点。

Method: 综述/教程型文章，采用以生成为中心（generation-centred）的组织方式，构建两层分析视角：第一层按模型如何产生新观测划分三条生成路径——直接变换或解码、顺序条件构造、迭代更新或传输；第二层考察各路径背后的建模与学习原理。通过代表性方法的关键组件、学习目标与采样过程进行介绍，并横向比较不同架构与生成机制之间的重叠与组合可能。

Result: 形成一个紧凑的概念框架，将分散的技术解释整合起来，使不同生成式方法可被归入统一的三路径分类体系，并显式刻画跨方法的联系与可组合性。

Conclusion: 该框架可帮助读者快速建立对陌生方法的"可用性理解"（working understanding），识别已接触模型之间的关联，并在更广阔的生成式AI版图中定位与切换不同方法。

Abstract: The rapidly expanding generative AI landscape brings together methods that differ in architecture, learning objective, mathematical representation and sampling procedure. A concise, selectively organised technical guide can provide an accessible starting point for developing a coherent understanding of this increasingly complex landscape. This article takes a generation-centred approach, connecting how models produce new observations with the modelling and learning principles that make generation possible. We develop a two-level perspective: first, distinguishing three recurring generation routes-direct transformation or decoding, sequential conditional construction, and iterative updating or transport; and second, examining the modelling and learning principles underlying these routes. Representative approaches are introduced through their key components, learning objectives and sampling procedures. We then examine their broader connections, including how different architectures and generation mechanisms can overlap or be combined. By bringing together essential technical explanations within a compact conceptual framework, the article aims to help readers develop a working understanding of unfamiliar approaches, recognise connections among models they have encountered, and navigate different approaches within the broader generative AI landscape.

</details>


### [114] [Approximate Dynamic Programming with Basis Function Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24737)
*Mohammadreza Fathi,Chi-Guhn Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出从模型本身学习ALP基函数的Bellman算子基函数(BBOBF)方法及其收缩、混合变体,并给出改进量与收敛的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近似线性规划(ALP)中基函数(BF)通常为预先定义、随机生成或限定特定结构,这既限制了近似精度,也缺乏改进已有近似的机制。

Method: 通过对当前值估计反复施加Bellman算子,从MDP模型本身学习基函数,提出BOBF方法;并给出限制基函数数量的收缩版本SBOBF,以及面向大规模问题、结合约束采样与基函数学习的混合变体HBOBF与SHBOBF。

Result: 证明每个学习到的基函数都以可量化的幅度改近似,方法收敛到最优值函数;SBOBF在限制基函数数量与每轮计算开销的同时保持该保证;混合变体在明确给定条件下保持改进性与有限终止保证。在连续易腐库存与排队控制问题上的数值实验显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 基函数学习可嵌入ALP框架并兼具理论保证与实证优势,为大规模MDP的值函数近似提供了可扩展方案。

Abstract: This work presents a novel approach for approximating the value function in Markov decision processes (MDPs). Our approach is grounded in the approximate linear programming (ALP) framework, wherein basis functions (BFs) play an important role. Traditionally, BFs have been pre-defined, randomly generated, or restricted to specific structures, which both limits the accuracy that can be achieved and leaves no mechanism for improving a given approximation. We instead learn BFs from the model itself, by repeatedly applying the Bellman operator to the current value estimate. We introduce the Bellman operator basis function (BOBF) learning method, and prove that each learned BF improves the approximation by a quantifiable amount and that the method converges to the optimal value function. Its shrunken counterpart (SBOBF) retains this guarantee while capping the number of BFs, and therefore the computational effort per iteration. For large-scale problems, where the number of constraints is prohibitive, we develop hybrid variants (HBOBF and SHBOBF) that combine constraint sampling with BF learning, and we show that the improvement and finite-termination guarantees are preserved under explicitly stated conditions. Numerical experiments on (continuous) perishable inventory and queueing control problems demonstrate that our BF learning techniques significantly outperform existing methods in the literature.

</details>


### [115] [Uneven gains in protein-RNA affinity and mutation-effect prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24739)
*Shangkun Sima,Tongfei Shen,Tianchi Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 用方法特异匹配人群评估11类方法，CoPRA将绝对亲和力MAE从1.2898降至1.1431 pKd，增益主要落在景观均值（平均效应），排序与局部定量精度提升不确定，须联合评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确匹配的亲和力是判定突变效应的前提，但聚合亲和力预测的总体提升未必均匀转移到平均效应、排序与局部定量精度三个维度，需检验增益的分布与来源。

Method: 构建方法特异匹配人群，对比11个方法家族；以受控CoPRA为对象，在220个突变对（20个家族）上检验平均效应、排序与中心化局部误差；另用24个定量突变对（3个来源匹配家族）作独立外部测试；通过PRA-Pred特征追踪、固定权重M2-PRNet输入对照与匹配监督干预定位限制因素，并做误差分解。

Result: CoPRA绝对亲和力MAE由1.2898降至1.1431 pKd；220对中突变响应更强、平均效应更准，但排序增益不确定、中心化局部误差未下降。外部测试中三个家族的绝对误差与普通突变效应误差均下降；中心化局部误差由0.4198降至0.3761 pKd，两家族改善、一家族变差。误差分解将增益定位在景观均值。

Conclusion: 聚合亲和力预测的提升并不均匀传导至各细分指标，增益集中于景观均值；应联合评估平均效应、排序与定量局部变异，并以特征追踪与受控干预识别突变响应上的具体限制。

Abstract: Accurate matched affinities determine mutation effects, but gains in aggregate affinity prediction may transfer unevenly to average effects, ordering and local accuracy. We evaluated eleven method families using method-specific matched populations. Controlled CoPRA reduced absolute-affinity mean absolute error from 1.2898 to 1.1431 pKd. Across 220 mutation pairs from 20 families, it generated stronger responses and improved mean-effect accuracy, with uncertain ranking gains and no decrease in mean centered local error. In an independent external test (24 quantitative pairs from three source-matched families), absolute and ordinary mutation-effect errors fell in all three families. Mean centered local error fell from 0.4198 to 0.3761 pKd, with improvements in two families and higher error in one. Error decomposition localized gains in landscape means. PRA-Pred feature tracing, fixed-weight M2-PRNet input controls and matched supervision interventions identified specific restrictions on mutation response. These results establish the need to evaluate average effects, ordering and quantitative local variation jointly.

</details>


### [116] [Elysium: Simulation-Free Unbalanced Schrödinger Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24763)
*Zihan Wang,Jianzhe Li,Zhenyi Zhang,Peijie Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Elysium，一种无需模拟轨迹的无平衡 Schrödinger 桥求解器，联合学习状态转移与质量变化，从单细胞快照重建细胞动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单细胞测量具有破坏性，无法追踪同一细胞，只能获得时间快照；细胞动力学本质随机且伴随增殖/凋亡引起的种群规模变化。基于增长的方法将种群变化表示为密度上的速率，无法将进出事件分配到单细胞；无平衡 Schrödinger 桥虽可建模个体事件，但现有神经求解器需在训练中模拟被杀的扩散桥轨迹。

Method: 以 killed Brownian 参考过程及其时间反转为基座，利用最优端点耦合与条件桥分布构造直接的回归目标，训练中不模拟轨迹；理论上证明期望训练目标的极小值点可恢复最优无平衡 Schrödinger 桥。

Result: 在具有已知 killed-diffusion 真值的模拟基准上，Elysium 能重建状态转移、种群规模与事件分布，优于现有确定性方法与桥类求解器；在单细胞时间序列上可插值未观测时间点的细胞状态分布、追踪种群扩张与收缩，并刻画双相药物响应动力学。

Conclusion: Elysium 提供了一种仿真无关、计算高效的无平衡 Schrödinger 桥求解框架，统一建模状态演化与质量变化，为快照数据下的细胞动力学重建提供有效工具。

Abstract: Reconstructing cellular dynamics from snapshots is challenging because the destructive nature of single-cell measurements makes tracking the same cells over time impossible. Cellular dynamics are inherently stochastic and involve changes in population size through proliferation and apoptosis. Growth-based methods represent population change as a rate on the density, which does not assign exits and entries to individual cells. Unbalanced Schrödinger bridges model individual events, but neural solvers for killed diffusion bridges simulate trajectories during training. We introduce Elysium, a simulation-free solver that jointly learns state transition and mass change. Using a killed Brownian reference and its time reversal, Elysium constructs direct regression objectives from an optimal endpoint coupling and conditional bridge distributions, without simulating trajectories during training. We show that the minimizers of the expected training objectives recover the optimal unbalanced Schrödinger bridge. Empirically, Elysium reconstructs state transitions, population size, and event distributions on simulated benchmarks with known killed-diffusion ground truth, outperforming existing deterministic and bridge-based solvers. On single-cell time courses, it interpolates cell state distributions at unobserved time points, tracks population expansion and contraction, and captures biphasic drug-response dynamics.

</details>


### [117] [Horizontal Flow Matching for Single-Cell Spatiotemporal Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24764)
*Zihan Wang,Jianzhe Li,Zhenyi Zhang,Peijie Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出horizontal flow matching（水平流匹配）及模型HORIZON，在忽略共享刚体运动的前提下学习空间转录组切片的时空动力学，可从一个初始快照连续生成组织演化，并在模拟与四个真实时间序列上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从空间转录组数据学习细胞时空动力学需在不同坐标系下获取的细胞快照间建立对应关系，但切片的旋转或平移会被误判为细胞运动，从而扭曲推断出的对应关系；因此需要一个对共享刚体运动不敏感的动力学建模框架。

Method: 提出horizontal flow matching：动力学由连续性—反应方程描述，其作用量对共享刚体运动赋予零代价，并引入水平投影算子，在学习阶段将其从空间预测误差中去除、在生成阶段从预测速度中去除。等价地构造终点问题，在Wasserstein–Fisher–Rao几何中联合估计对应关系与相对位姿，得到解析路径，以其速度与增长量作为回归目标。模型HORIZON采用等变Transformer从当前组织预测速度与增长，实现连续生成。

Result: 理论上证明在理想种群条件下回归上述目标可恢复水平动力学。在坐标系受扰动的模拟组织上，对齐改善重建，投影抑制刚体漂移。在四个空间转录组时间序列上，HORIZON对组织几何、基因表达和细胞丰度的重建精度优于现有空间方法与非平衡轨迹方法，并能插值留出的发育阶段。

Conclusion: 通过将共享刚体运动从动力学中分离（水平投影），并结合WFR几何下的解析回归目标与等变Transformer，可有效学习空间转录组的时空动力学，实现从单一快照出发的连续组织生成与阶段插值。

Abstract: Learning the spatiotemporal dynamics of cells from spatial transcriptomics requires connecting snapshots of different cells acquired in different coordinate frames. A rotation or translation of a section can appear as cell movement and distort the inferred correspondence. We introduce horizontal flow matching, which learns changes in tissue organization, molecular state, and cell abundance up to shared rigid motion. The dynamics follow a continuity--reaction equation whose action assigns no cost to shared rigid motion, and the resulting horizontal projector removes this component from spatial prediction errors during learning and from predicted velocities during generation. An equivalent endpoint problem jointly estimates correspondence and relative pose in Wasserstein--Fisher--Rao geometry and yields analytic paths whose velocity and growth serve as regression targets. Regressing these targets recovers the horizontal dynamics under ideal population conditions. Our model, HORIZON, uses an equivariant transformer to predict velocity and growth from the current tissue, so that a tissue can be generated continuously from a single initial snapshot. On a simulated tissue observed in perturbed coordinate frames, alignment improves reconstruction and projection reduces rigid drift. On four spatial transcriptomics time courses, HORIZON reconstructs tissue geometry, gene expression, and cell abundance more accurately than existing spatial and unbalanced trajectory methods, and interpolates held-out developmental stages.

</details>


### [118] [Counterfactual World Models: JEPAs That Reason About "What If"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24778)
*Randall Balestriero,Yann LeCun,Kerda Varaku*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需反事实数据、额外参数或重训练的反事实世界模型：利用“自然不做跳跃”假设与动作/扰动作为独立力时交互为时间二阶的性质，将反事实结果一阶近似为观测结果加上世界模型在替代动作与事实动作下预测之差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型无法完全观测环境，当隐藏扰动（如水流推船）改变动作结果时，需要回答“在同一扰动下换个动作会怎样”的反事实问题，以区分决策失误与运气不佳；但仅用事实转移训练的世界模型在原理上无法单独回答该问题。

Method: 证明纯事实数据训练的世界模型无法回答反事实问题；基于natura non facit saltus（连续性）假设，推导动作与扰动分离作用时其交互为时间二阶，从而反事实结果一阶等于观测结果加上世界模型在替代动作与事实动作下预测之差；给出O(h²)误差上界（相对扰动方差）、无配对事实数据的匹配极小极大下界，并刻画在间断点、坐标折叠及扰动缩放指令情形下的失效；在冻结DINOv2与MAE编码器的JEPA世界模型上从像素验证，并用DINOv3、SigLIP 2、CLIP、EVA-02四个冻结编码器及事后规划任务评估。

Result: 在四个渲染仿真器上，同一世界模型的反事实R²从38–46%提升至86–96%；第五个水下航行器场景中，用16倍事实数据达到90–94%；四个额外冻结编码器在相同仿真器上取得81–95%。事后规划中，该方法在35–69%的事件中找到可达目标的动作，而知晓仿真器但不知结果的oracle规划器仅14–23%。

Conclusion: 反事实推断可借助物理结构（连续性、力的可分离性）从事实数据中一阶恢复，无需反事实监督；存在理论误差界与匹配下界，并在多编码器、多仿真器与规划任务上显著优于基线。

Abstract: World models operate in a world they cannot fully observe. When a hidden disturbance, such as a current pushing a boat, changes the outcome of an action, a counterfactual question follows: what would have happened under a different action, subject to the same disturbance? Its answer separates bad decisions from bad luck. We prove that world models trained on factual transitions cannot answer it on their own, whatever their data or capacity. For many physical systems, the observed outcome and a simple property of the dynamics suffice: natura non facit saltus, nature makes no leaps. When actions and disturbances act as separate forces, their interaction is second-order in time, so the counterfactual outcome is, to first order, the observed outcome plus the difference between the world model's predictions under the alternative and factual actions. This yields a counterfactual world model with no counterfactual data, extra parameters, or retraining. For transitions of duration $h$, we prove an $O(h^2)$ bound on the counterfactual error relative to the disturbance variance, establish a matching minimax lower bound from unpaired factual data, and characterize failures at discontinuities, coordinate folds, and disturbances that scale the command. We validate these predictions from pixels with JEPA world models on frozen DINOv2 and masked autoencoder (MAE) encoders. On four rendered simulators, our approach improves counterfactual $R^2$ from $38$--$46\%$ to $86$--$96\%$ with the same world models; on a fifth, an underwater vehicle, it reaches $90$--$94\%$ with sixteen times more factual data. Four further frozen encoders, DINOv3, SigLIP~2, CLIP and EVA-02, give $81$--$95\%$ on the same simulators. In hindsight planning, it finds the action that would have reached the goal in $35$--$69\%$ of events, against $14$--$23\%$ for an oracle planner that knows the simulator but not the outcome.

</details>


### [119] [Reconstruction vs. Prediction Pre-Training of Discrete Video Representations for Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24781)
*Ron Kosova,Francesco Croce*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在容量匹配条件下对比自编码与V-JEPA两类目标对视频编码器动力学表征的影响，发现二者存在"状态保真 ↔ 长程预测稳健性"的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督视频编码器常以像素重建或潜变量特征预测为训练目标，但这两类目标如何塑造用于下游动力学的表征尚不清楚。

Method: 在连续控制环境中训练两个结构完全相同的视频编码器，共享FSQ瓶颈，分别采用自编码目标与V-JEPA预测目标；并提出一种简单的不对称离散化方案，使同一瓶颈可应用于预测模型，从而实现严格的容量匹配对照。

Result: 自编码器保留显著更多的物理状态信息，单步潜变量预测略优；预测编码器在自回归展开中误差累积更少。结构探针显示，该优势与对低层视觉扰动的更强鲁棒性以及对时间上下文的更强依赖相关。

Conclusion: 预测目标与重建目标诱导出不同的动力学表征，前者以牺牲状态保真度为代价换取可能有益于长程预测的性质。

Abstract: Self-supervised video encoders are commonly trained to either reconstruct pixels or predict latent features, yet it remains unclear how these objectives shape representations for downstream dynamics. We present a capacity-matched study isolating this choice: two identical video encoders with a shared FSQ bottleneck are trained under autoencoding and V-JEPA objectives on a continuous control environment. We introduce a simple asymmetric discretization scheme to apply the same bottleneck to the predictive model. The resulting representations exhibit a clear trade-off: the autoencoder preserves substantially more physical state information and achieves slightly better one-step latent prediction, while the predictive encoder accumulates less error under autoregressive rollout. Structural probes suggest that this advantage is associated with greater robustness to low-level visual perturbations and stronger dependence on temporal context. These results indicate that predictive and reconstructive objectives induce distinct representations of dynamics, trading state fidelity for properties that may benefit long-horizon prediction.

</details>


### [120] [Learning Trajectories Leave Decodable Traces in Neural Network Weight Space and Internal Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24782)
*Patrick Krauss,Achim Schilling,Andreas Maier,Thomas Kinfe,Claus Metzner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 最终权重保留了初始化、任务顺序与早期轨迹等信息，但属选择性而非完整历史记录；不同解之间被低损失路径连接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一任务的神经网络会形成相关但可区分的权重结构，作者想检验这些差异是否只是等价解的冗余，还是编码了个体学习历史（初始化、任务顺序、早期轨迹状态）。

Method: 训练30个网络并操控初始化、批调度与任务顺序；用配对匹配检验权重到初始化的可辨识性，用线性探针解码任务顺序，检验中间权重状态的可预测性，并进行扰动恢复实验与端点插值损失分析。

Result: 30个网络在10个候选中全部正确匹配到各自初始化；共享初始化的网络在不同批调度下形成可辨识的隐表示；线性探针在80个留出网络上以100%准确率解码两任务顺序；中间权重部分可预测但初始权重无法可靠重建；扰动网络恢复性能却不回到原训练路径；2160对对齐端点中2157对在采样插值点无正损失势垒。

Conclusion: 共享任务将网络约束到相关且成功的解，但不抹除个体历史；最终权重既反映学到了什么，也反映学习从何处开始、如何展开，构成选择性的历史痕迹而非完整轨迹记录。

Abstract: Neural networks trained on the same task develop related weight-space structures, yet remain distinguishable after learning. Do these differences merely reflect alternative solutions, or do they preserve information about individual learning histories? We tested whether final weights reveal initialization, task order, and earlier trajectory states. Training reorganized the ensemble: initial pairwise distances did not consistently predict final distances. Nevertheless, all 30 trained networks were correctly matched to their initial weights among ten candidates per dataset. Networks sharing an initialization also developed recognizable final hidden representations across different batch schedules, although direct comparisons with initial activity did not recover identity. Learning order remained readable as well. A linear probe decoded the order of two earlier tasks with 100% accuracy on 80 held-out networks after ten epochs of a common final task. Earlier intermediate weight states were partly predictable, but individual initial weights could not be reliably reconstructed. Final weights thus retain selective historical information rather than a complete trajectory record. Perturbed networks recovered classification performance without returning to their unperturbed training paths. This separation persisted across datasets, intervention times, and network layers. Moreover, 2,157 of 2,160 aligned endpoint pairs showed no positive loss barrier at sampled interpolation points, indicating low-loss connections between distinct solutions rather than convergence to one weight vector. Together, these findings show that a shared task constrains networks to related successful solutions without erasing their individual histories. Final weights reflect not only what a network has learned, but also where learning began and how it unfolded.

</details>


### [121] [Bias-Only Training Dynamics in ReLU Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24797)
*Shreyas Chaudhari,Jose M. F. Moura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究随机固定权重、仅训练偏置的单隐层ReLU网络的训练动力学，借助"激活码"（活跃神经元的二值模式）证明偏置梯度仅通过激活码依赖输入，训练可分解为分段线性最小二乘问题，且切换神经元的比例随宽度增大而消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机初始化固定权重加可训练偏置的网络已被证明是通用逼近器，但"仅偏置训练"的动力学机制鲜有研究，缺乏理论刻画。

Method: 以单隐层ReLU网络为对象，引入激活码（将输入空间划分为凸多面体区域的活跃神经元二值模式），分析偏置梯度对输入的依赖结构，并将训练过程刻画为以激活码为特征的线性最小二乘序列，在神经元激活状态改变时发生切换。

Result: 偏置梯度只通过激活码依赖输入；激活码固定时训练等价于线性最小二乘；切换神经元比例随网络变宽而趋于零，无限宽极限下动力学趋近于初始激活码上的线性最小二乘。实验验证了较窄网络中激活切换影响明显，而随宽度增加其频率及对动力学的影响递减。

Conclusion: 激活码为理解仅偏置训练提供了有效分析框架，揭示了宽度增大使动力学趋于线性最小二乘极限的机制。

Abstract: Neural networks with randomly initialized, fixed weights and trainable biases are universal approximators, yet the dynamics of bias-only training remain largely unexplored. We study these dynamics in one-hidden-layer ReLU networks through activation codes, the binary patterns of active neurons that partition the input space into convex polyhedral regions. We show that the gradients with respect to the biases depend on each input only through its activation code, and hence the training dynamics can be characterized with these codes. In particular, as long as the activation codes remain fixed, training reduces to a linear least-squares problem with the codes as features. Training can then be understood as a sequence of least-squares problems, with transitions occurring when neurons change their activation states. We show that the fraction of switching neurons vanishes as the network widens, and the training dynamics approach those of linear least squares on the initial activation codes in the infinite-width limit. Our experiments support these theoretical findings, showing that while activation switches can noticeably affect training in narrower networks, their relative frequency and the associated changes in the dynamics diminish as the width increases.

</details>


### [122] [Prognosis Without Prediction: The Intrinsic Geometry of Survival](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24799)
*Atahan Karagoz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自组织生存流形(SOSM)框架，将无监督表征学习与生存标签解耦，把生存时间事件进程建模为潜在的几何涌现属性，在ccRCC多组学队列上验证预后能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维环境下的系统失效建模受限于依赖稀疏、噪声大或删失终点的监督算法，需打破对显式结局回归的依赖。

Method: 构建深度学习架构：将异构多组学数据投影到统一潜在空间，用伪时间加权、曲率最小化的图拉普拉斯算子组织患者轨迹，使轨迹沿低耗散测地线流演化。

Result: 在184例透明细胞肾细胞癌多组学队列的确定性5折交叉验证中，平均一致性指数为0.768±0.068；折外风险分层显示出显著生存差异(log-rank p<0.001)，高风险患者局部流形耗散增加14.7%；编码器权重分析表明模型无监督地自主恢复了经典代谢与微环境驱动因子。

Conclusion: 系统失效可被刻画为几何相变，SOSM为生物、工业与金融等复杂系统的预后建模提供了可推广的计算引擎。

Abstract: Modeling systemic failure in high-dimensional environments is fundamentally limited by supervised algorithms that attempt to predict sparse, noisy or censored endpoints. To break this reliance on explicit outcome regression, we engineered the Self-Organizing Survival Manifolds (SOSM) framework, a deep learning architecture that decouples unsupervised representation learning from survival labels and treats time-to-event progression as an emergent geometric property. The system projects heterogeneous multi-omics arrays into a unified latent space governed by a pseudotime-weighted, curvature-minimizing graph Laplacian, organizing patient trajectories along low-dissipation geodesic flows. To validate the framework, we applied this architecture to a 184-patient clear cell renal cell carcinoma multi-omics cohort. Across a deterministic 5-fold cross-validation pipeline, SOSM achieved a mean Concordance Index of $0.768 \pm 0.068$, while out-of-fold risk stratification yielded significant survival divergence (log-rank $p < 0.001$) accompanied by a 14.7% increase in local manifold dissipation among high-risk patients. Furthermore, interrogating the network's encoder weights confirmed that the model autonomously recovered canonical metabolic and microenvironmental drivers of disease progression without supervised feature selection. By demonstrating that systemic failure can be mapped as a geometric phase transition, SOSM establishes a generalizable computational engine for prognostic modeling across biological, industrial and financial complex systems.

</details>


### [123] [Synthesizing hyperspectral Data using generative Models to train spectral Unmixing Methods for low-cost Crop Residue Cover Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24827)
*Ege Artan,Shuo Chen,Andrew Peng,Sammuel Aldrich Karya,Kyaw Thiha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出解耦式高光谱解混框架：以条件数据生成器为中介，分步完成插值与丰度映射，从而把光谱物理先验注入有限的地面真值数据，实现人群风险的蒙特卡洛近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FINCH卫星的光谱范围缺乏标准指数，必须依赖复杂的数据驱动解混模型；而此类模型需稠密且分布良好的数据流形，真实地面真值数据却稀疏且采集昂贵，且解混模型无法原生利用光谱物理先验。

Method: 将传统单模型解混流程解耦为"插值+条件数据生成+丰度映射"三段式；提出两种生成器（含Conformer层的1D条件U-Net，即GD-Streamline；双路径Transformer条件变分自编码器TCVAE）与两种解混模型（MLP与傅里叶神经算子FNO）进行验证。

Result: 实验表明：若不在该解耦框架内严格引入并整合光谱先验，生成器易发生域坍缩、模式坍缩与幻觉，导致近似数据流形失真，最终解混性能不佳且出现分布外表现。

Conclusion: 解耦框架具备扩展数据、逼近人群风险的潜力，但有效性高度依赖光谱先验的严谨集成；后续工作需重点解决生成器的坍缩与幻觉问题。

Abstract: The spectral range of the Field Imaging Nanosatellite for Crop residue Hyperspectral Mapping (FINCH), developed by the University of Toronto Aerospace Team's Space System Division, poses significant challenges for hyperspectral unmixing to determine crop residue cover fractional abundances. The severe lack of standard indices necessitates the use of complex, data-driven unmixing models. Complex unmixing models require dense, well-distributed manifolds, whereas ground-truth datasets are sparse and expensive. To better leverage the information content in existing ground-truth datasets, this study presents a framework that decouples interpolation and abundance-mapping estimation via an intermediary conditional data generator, contrasting with traditional single-model unmixing pipelines. This decoupling enables the inclusion of physical prior knowledge about spectra, which is not natively accessible to unmixing models, thereby artificially expanding the dataset to serve as a Monte Carlo approximation of the population risk. To test this hypothesis, we have proposed two generator models: a 1D Conditional U-Net with Conformer Layers (GD-Streamline) and a Dual-Path Transformer Conditional Variational AutoEncoder (TCVAE), and two unmixing models: Multi-Layered Perceptrons (MLP) and Fourier Neural Operators (FNO). The results of the study indicate a need for rigorous integration and introduction of spectral priors within the scope of the proposed decoupling framework to prevent domain and mode collapse and hallucination by the generator models; otherwise, this leads to falsely approximated data manifolds, ultimately resulting in unsatisfactory and out-of-distribution unmixing performance.

</details>


### [124] [Overlooked and Underutilized: Prototypes are the Missing Piece in Tabular XAI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24831)
*Jacek Karolczak,Jerzy Stefanowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文主张在表格数据XAI中纳入原型（尤其是"原型部件"）作为与特征归因互补的直观审计入口，并从认知理论和几何有效性两方面论证其合理性，最后提出复合式解释流水线的方向与开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格数据XAI过度依赖特征归因方法，虽提供技术透明性，但让非专家用户同时评估数十个孤立特征会带来过高的认知负荷；原型方法被忽视且未被充分利用。

Method: 立场/观点型论文：论证将原型整合为审计复杂ML模型的补充性直观入口；指出原型部件通过将相关特征聚合为有界子空间、构成人类可理解的概念单元，优于孤立特征；从认知视角（Rosch范畴化理论、Kahneman系统1思维）与几何视角（提出"几何有效性三元组"以说明原型如何验证数据流形）双重论证。

Result: 通过认知与几何两条论证线索，论证了原型（尤其原型部件）在表格数据上的解释价值，并指出流形验证这一现有方法常忽视的关键维度。

Conclusion: 表格XAI要实现真正可理解，需转向复合式解释流水线：将基于相似性的直观推理与严格的细粒度特征审计相结合；论文最后梳理原型方法的开放挑战并提出潜在解决方向。

Abstract: Explainable AI (XAI) is frequently applied to high-stakes tabular data, yet the field relies heavily on feature attribution methods. While these approaches offer technical transparency, evaluating dozens of isolated features demands excessive cognitive effort from non-expert users. In this paper, we argue that prototypes remain overlooked and underutilized, and should be integrated as a complementary, intuitive gateway to auditing complex machine learning models. Although entire exemplars function as an intuitive baseline for prototype-based explanations, we highlight prototypical parts as particularly valuable for tabular data. By grouping related features into bounded subspaces, prototypical parts technically embody human-understandable concepts, replacing isolated features with cohesive semantic units. We support this integration through two primary lenses. Cognitively, these parts align with Rosch's categorization theory and Kahneman's System 1 thinking to naturally reduce cognitive load for humans. Then, we introduce a Geometric Validity Triad to conceptualize why prototypes validate the data manifold - a critical dimension often neglected by existing methods. We contend that making tabular XAI genuinely comprehensible requires shifting toward composite explanation pipelines that pair intuitive similarity-based reasoning with rigorous feature-level auditing. The paper concludes by outlining open challenges of prototype-based methods and discussing potential solutions to guide future research.

</details>


### [125] [What Survival Benchmarks Don't Tell You: Impact of Model Selection and Dataset Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24837)
*Sandra Dening,Emre Kavak,Christian Wachinger,George H. Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建大规模中立基准，在49个数据集上比较10种经典、树集成与深度学习方法，并将模型选择准则（C-index/IBS/MAE-PO）作为独立实验变量，用贝叶斯混合效应模型证明评估设计本身会显著左右结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生存分析基准存在两类局限：一是过度依赖C-index作为评估与模型选择标准；二是常排除深度学习方法，或对困难数据情形（高维、重删失、大样本）覆盖不足。作者希望厘清"评估设计"本身对方法排名与结论的影响。

Method: 在49个多样化数据集上系统评测10种方法（经典统计、树集成、深度学习），将超参数选择准则（优化C-index、IBS或MAE-PO）设为独立自变量，采用贝叶斯混合效应模型对C-index、IBS、MAE-PO、X-CAL等指标进行分层分析，并考察数据集特征（样本量、维度、删失率）的调节作用。

Result: 对复杂DL与树模型而言，以IBS而非C-index选超参可在几乎不损失判别排序能力的前提下显著提升概率精度与校准；方法表现被数据集特性根本性支配——DL随样本量增大扩展性优异，但在高维特征空间性能明显下降；重删失会人为抬高绝对校准指标，导致过于保守的检验。

Conclusion: 单一全局标量摘要（如单看C-index）效用有限，作者主张透明报告完整评估设置（选择准则、指标、数据特征），并将基准结论视为评估设计的函数而非方法固有属性。

Abstract: Despite the proliferation of survival analysis methods, current benchmarks often operate under narrow conditions: they heavily rely on the concordance index (C-index) for evaluation and model selection, and frequently either exclude deep learning (DL) methods or underrepresent challenging dataset regimes. To address these limitations, we conduct a large-scale, neutral benchmark comparing 10 classical, tree-based, and DL methods across 49 diverse datasets. Crucially, we treat the model selection criterion (optimizing for C-index, Integrated Brier Score (IBS), or Mean Absolute Error (MAE-PO)) as an independent experimental variable. Using Bayesian mixed-effects modeling, we demonstrate that evaluation design heavily dictates conclusions. For complex DL and tree-based architectures, selecting hyperparameters to optimize for IBS instead of C-index yields substantial improvements in probabilistic accuracy and calibration at a negligible cost to discriminative ranking. Furthermore, method performance (C-index, IBS, MAE-PO, and X-CAL) is fundamentally governed by dataset characteristics. While DL models scale exceptionally well with larger sample sizes, they suffer notable performance drops in high-dimensional feature spaces. Additionally, we expose a structural vulnerability where heavy censoring artificially inflates absolute calibration metrics, creating overly conservative tests. Ultimately, our findings challenge the utility of global single-number performance summaries and advocate for survival analysis results that transparently report the evaluation setup. Our code is available at: https://github.com/sdening/surv-bench-study

</details>


### [126] [Measuring and Improving Transfer of Neural Surrogates for Vehicle Aerodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24843)
*Favour Nerrise,Julian Busch,Gurpreet Singh Hora,Martin Sipka,Danielle C. Maddix,Georgia Olympia Brikis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统基准测试了DrivAerML预训练气动代理模型的迁移行为，发现微调并非普遍优于从头训练且会损失源域精度；提出GeoReplay源域回放方法，在80个设置中的77个降低源域误差且保持目标精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部车辆气动神经代理模型缺乏跨车型零样本泛化，每个新设计需昂贵定制训练。虽预期预训练模型能少样本适配新车型并保持源域精度，但此前无系统研究检验这种迁移行为。

Method: 在四个独立目标数据集上对五个DrivAerML预训练代理进行大规模受控基准测试（目标数据集在几何、CFD求解器、湍流模型与流动条件上均与源域不同）。提出GeoReplay：几何与梯度对齐的源域回放，仅保留与目标相似的源仿真参与训练，且仅当更新与目标梯度一致时才应用；给出分离几何覆盖与物理模拟差异的风险界，并证明足够小的GeoReplay步长可降低目标训练目标。

Result: 微调在不到一半的比较中优于从头训练，且降低源数据集精度。GeoReplay在80个设置中的77个降低微调后的源域误差，同时达到与微调相当的目标域精度。

Conclusion: 预训练气动代理的朴素微调不保证跨车型迁移收益且存在源域遗忘；基于几何与梯度对齐的源域回放可作为轻量、理论可证的迁移策略，兼顾目标适配与源域保持。

Abstract: Neural surrogates for external vehicle aerodynamics are fast alternatives to physics-based computational fluid dynamics (CFD) simulations. Their foundational limitation is a lack of zero-shot generalization across vehicle classes, which results in an expensive, custom training campaign for each new vehicle design. Similar to pretrained models in other fields, a pretrained surrogate should adapt to a new vehicle class using only a few simulations and stay accurate on its pretraining domain. No prior study has systematically tested this transfer behavior. We conduct a large-scale, controlled benchmarking study on five DrivAerML-pretrained surrogates across four independent target datasets. These datasets differ from the source in geometry, CFD solver, turbulence model, and flow conditions. We find that fine-tuning these surrogates outperforms training from scratch in fewer than half of the comparisons and lowers the accuracy of the surrogate on its source dataset. We introduce GeoReplay, a geometry- and gradient-aligned source replay that keeps source simulations resembling the target in training and applies their updates only when they agree with the target gradient. A risk bound separates geometric coverage from differences in the simulated physics, and we prove that every sufficiently small GeoReplay step decreases the target training objective. GeoReplay lowers the source error of fine-tuning in 77 of 80 settings and matches its target accuracy.

</details>


### [127] [Atomic MathReasoner: Instance-Level Composition of Atomic Reasoning Skills for Mathematical LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24846)
*Jiashu Li,Tianyu Gao,Qinglong Liu,Jinping Wang,Ye Wei,Zhiqiang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Atomic MathReasoner（AMR），一个实例级框架，将数学推理分解出的原子技能组合成原型，并用轻量路由器为每个问题选择合适组合，实现无需在线优化的细粒度技能利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多将LLM数学推理视为单一整体能力，而不同数学问题实际需要不同推理技能；虽有工作将数学推理分解为原子技能并训练专门模块，但如何有效利用这些原子能力仍缺乏研究。

Method: 1）训练一组能力特化的LoRA专家库；2）在留出验证子集上做无梯度优化，发现多样化的低损失组合原型；3）用参考解的token级负对数似然评估各原型偏好；4）通过置信度间隔过滤模糊分配；5）将伪标签蒸馏进轻量路由器，推理时按问题选择组合原型。

Result: 在AtomicMath基准上对通用与数学专用LLM均有稳定提升：Qwen2.5-Math-Instruct-7B上Level 1平均领域准确率64.4%→68.1%，Level 2为59.3%→63.7%，平均逻辑推理性能39.4%→46.8%。

Conclusion: 有效组合专门化的原子技能可显著增强LLM数学推理能力，且实例级技能组合是可行且有前景的方向。

Abstract: Most existing approaches enhance mathematical reasoning in large language models (LLMs) as a monolithic capability, despite the fact that different mathematical problems require different reasoning skills. Recent work has explored a more fine-grained perspective by decomposing mathematical reasoning into atomic skills and developing specialized modules for individual capabilities. However, how to effectively utilize these atomic abilities remains underexplored. To address this challenge, we propose Atomic MathReasoner (AMR), an instance-level framework for composing atomic reasoning skills. AMR first trains a library of capability-specific LoRA experts and performs gradient-free optimization over held-out validation subsets to discover a diverse set of low-loss composition prototypes. It evaluates the preference of each prototype using the token-level negative log-likelihood of reference solutions, filters ambiguous assignments through a confidence margin, and distills the resulting pseudo-labels into a lightweight router. During inference, the router selects an appropriate composition prototype for each problem, enabling problem-specific utilization of atomic skills without costly online optimization. Experiments on the AtomicMath benchmark across general and mathematics-specialized LLMs demonstrate consistent improvements. On Qwen2.5-Math-Instruct-7B, AMR increases average field accuracy from 64.4% to 68.1% at Level 1 and from 59.3% to 63.7% at Level 2, while improving average logical reasoning performance from 39.4% to 46.8%. These results demonstrate that effectively composing specialized atomic skills provides a promising approach to strengthening mathematical reasoning in LLMs.

</details>


### [128] [TangentPDE: Learning Local Operator Responses with Sparse Finite-Difference Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24873)
*Xinjian Xie,Ruxuan Chen,Jiashu Li,Tianyu Gao,Ye Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TangentPDE 利用有限差分从参考求解器的成对输入状态输出构造方向响应，联合约束状态保真、方向对齐与幅值校准，从而在无需导数标签或伴随求解的情况下同时学习神经算子的值与局部变化，并在多系统任务中降低状态与 rollout 误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经 PDE 算子多以状态重建为训练目标，只能约束观测输入处的算子取值，无法刻画其对局部扰动的敏感性；更忠实的算子逼近需要同时保证状态预测准确和局部扰动方向上的变化正确。

Method: 用有限差分近似方向导数，在稀疏成对输入上由参考求解器的状态输出构造任务相关的有限步响应；目标函数联合优化状态保真、响应方向对齐与响应幅值校准。响应标签仅来自成对输入的状态输出，故无需显式导数标签、线性化/伴随 PDE 求解或完整 Jacobian，也适用于无导数接口的非线性求解器；并辅以选择性执行的适应机制强化有限差分监督下的局部表示，同时设计预算匹配与对应扰动对照实验，隔离关系监督效应。该框架为即插即用式训练框架。

Result: 在四个 PDE 系统、20 种任务-骨干组合中，18 种设置下三随机种子平均状态误差下降，Rollout@20 误差亦在 18 种设置下下降；内在有限步响应增益在 DiffReact 上最强，其余系统上表现依赖任务与骨干网络。

Conclusion: 在状态重建之外引入有限步方向响应监督可有效提升神经 PDE 算子的局部变化建模能力，且实现成本低、可插拔；但响应层面的增益并不普遍，其效果受任务与骨干结构影响，需按场景选择性使用。

Abstract: Neural PDE operators are often optimized through state reconstruction, which largely constrains operator values at observed inputs but does not fully characterize their sensitivity to local perturbations. A more faithful approximation of operator behavior requires not only accurate state predictions, but also correct variations along local perturbation directions. We present TangentPDE, which uses finite differences to approximate directional derivatives and constructs task-relevant finite-step responses from the state outputs of a reference solver at sparse paired inputs. Its objective jointly constrains state fidelity, response-direction alignment, and response-magnitude calibration, thereby learning both operator values and their local variations. Because the response labels are constructed solely from state outputs at paired inputs, the method requires neither explicit derivative labels, linearized or adjoint PDE solves, nor full Jacobians, and remains applicable to nonlinear solvers without derivative interfaces. Building on this, we additionally propose a selectively executed adaptation mechanism to strengthen local operator representations under finite-difference supervision. Budget-matched and correspondence-perturbed controls isolate the effect of relational supervision from improvements attributable to additional state observations or increased data volume. Designed as a plug-and-play training framework, TangentPDE integrates with diverse neural-operator backbones. Across four PDE systems and 20 task-backbone settings, TangentPDE lowers the three-seed mean state error in 18 settings and the corresponding Rollout@20 error in 18, while intrinsic finite-step response gains are strongest on DiffReact and remain task- and backbone-dependent elsewhere.

</details>


### [129] [Auditing black-box clinical prediction models with a large language model through queries alone: a blinded test with planted flaws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24877)
*Zhiyu Liu,Xinyu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在仅能查询黑箱模型预测值的条件下，GPT-6 Astra 能以高保真度复现被审计模型的log-odds（平均R²=0.976），并检出96%（69/72）的人为植入缺陷，但其报告的问题与发现需在模型和数据上复核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验"LLM可解释临床预测模型"的三项承诺——忠实描述模型计算、发现模型缺陷、揭示新知识，并选择答案可核验的任务进行实证评估。

Method: 基于国际卒中试验（IST）数据，用既往随机化患者训练四类模型（逻辑回归、可解释提升机、梯度提升树、神经网络）；对24个副本各植入一种缺陷（低预测子群体、风险因素方向反转、不合理阶跃），与8个干净模型混合。GPT-6 Astra 经仅返回预测值的工具盲法审计每个模型三次；判定规则在运行前预先注册并由程序执行。同时与目的性编写的脚本及分析师拟合的加性代理模型作对照。

Result: 中位128项的代理模型复现log-odds的均R²=0.976（42项加性代理为0.959）；检出植入缺陷69/72（96%，95%CI 80%–99%），专用脚本24/24全中；1,089份可核查报告中60%未通过预设的平均行为检查，事后多为真实但幅度小的阶跃，15%在模型中不存在；601条干净模型中读出的意外关系，仅11%在后续随机化患者中成立（随机配对6%，回归扫描最强候选28%），经训练数据校验后为33%对30%。

Conclusion: 仅凭查询访问，LLM即可忠实描述黑箱模型并检出近乎全部植入缺陷，与专用脚本相当；但其问题报告须回到模型验证，其发现须回到数据验证。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are used to explain clinical prediction models, on the promise that they can say what a model computes, find its faults and even reveal new knowledge in it. We tested the three promises where the answers could be checked. From the International Stroke Trial we trained four kinds of model (logistic regression, explainable boosting machine, gradient-boosted trees, neural network) for two outcomes on patients randomised earlier, planted one flaw in each of 24 copies (an under-predicted subgroup, a reversed risk factor or an implausible step) and mixed them with the 8 clean models. GPT-6 Astra audited each model three times, blind, through a tool that only returned the model's predictions; the judging rules were committed before any run and applied by program. Its surrogate models, with a median of 128 terms, reproduced the audited models' log-odds with a mean $R^2$ of 0.976 (0.959 for an additive surrogate of 42 terms fitted by an analyst). It found the planted flaw in 69 of 72 runs (96%; 95% CI 80% to 99%); a script written for the purpose found all 24. It also reported many more problems: 60% of 1,089 checkable reports failed the pre-specified check against the model's average behaviour; post hoc, most of these were real but small steps, and 15% of the checkable reports were not found in the model. Of 601 unexpected relationships it read out of the clean models, 11% held in patients randomised later (random pairs 6%; a regression scan's shorter list of its strongest candidates, 28%); after a check on the training data, 33% against 30%. With query access alone, an LLM can describe a black-box model faithfully and find nearly every planted flaw, as a purpose-written script also did; its reports of problems must be checked on the model and its discoveries on data.

</details>


### [130] [Monoculture Robust Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24881)
*Lee Cohen,Jon Kleinberg,Omer Reingold*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将"单一栽培鲁棒性"引入PAC学习，通过可复现学习与单一栽培鲁棒性的联系，给出个体合取误差随k指数收缩的上界，并证明样本量乘子的对数级下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法单一栽培（monoculture）：共享组件（如训练数据）的模型倾向在同一输入上共同失败。作者追问：仅访问单一数据集的learner能否输出k个各自准确、且联合失败概率可与k个独立数据集训练相媲美的假设。

Method: 定义"个体单一栽培鲁棒性"，以k个假设都误分类某点的合取误差来量化；建立可复现性（replicability）与单一栽培鲁棒性之间的联系：若基learner是ρ-可复现的，则在同一样本上独立运行k次可获个体单一栽培误差，且该误差随k指数收缩，由基learner在该点的单次误差和ρ控制。另证下界。

Result: 正面结果：ρ-可复现基learner重复k次，个体合取误差随k呈指数衰减。负面结果：对个体单一栽培鲁棒性，最坏情况下将个体合取误差降至γ可能需要样本量乘子t = Ω(log(1/γ))。

Conclusion: 可复现性可作为实现单一栽培鲁棒性的有效工具，代价是样本量需对数级增加；上下界共同刻画了该保证的样本复杂度代价。

Abstract: We introduce a notion of monoculture robustness in Probably Approximately Correct (PAC) learning, as a way to mitigate the problem of algorithmic monoculture (Kleinberg and Raghavan, 2021): the tendency of models sharing the same components (e.g., training data) to fail on the same inputs. Specifically, we ask whether a learner with access to a single dataset can output $k$ individually accurate hypotheses whose joint failure probability is comparable to training on $k$ independent datasets. We say that such a learning procedure achieves monoculture robustness, and we quantify this by bounding the conjunctive error that all $k$ hypotheses misclassify points in an individual sense. We design methods for achieving this type of guarantee by drawing a surprising connection between replicability (Impagliazzo et al., 2022) and monoculture robustness. Specifically, we show that if the base learner is $ρ$-replicable, then running it independently $k$ times on the same sample yields individual monoculture error that shrinks exponentially with $k$, controlled by the single-run error of the base learner at the point and $ρ$. We complement these positive results with a lower bound: for individual monoculture robustness, we prove a worst-case lower bound showing that, for learners, driving the individual conjunctive error down to $γ$ can require a sample-size multiplier of $t = Ω(\log(1/γ))$.

</details>


### [131] [Cap-and-Track: Optimizing LLM Inference Request Dispatch Across Shifting Capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24891)
*Benjamin Novotny,Doug Szeto,Chang Wang,Shicheng Chen,George Zhihua Yuan,Bob Kamma,Michael Ge,John Xin Zhou,Andrea Olgiati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Cap-and-Track 是一种用于异步 LLM 推理的调度设计：在饱和资源处做快速失败的准入检查（Cap），并用拒绝信号反馈式地向上探测、向下回退（Track），无需预测输出长度或维护舰队级固定速率。已在 Meta 帖子与直播视频理解的生产流量整形中落地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定调度速率在服务舰队扩缩容与请求长度变化时会过时；生成式输出长度在准入时未知，且共享供应商池可能损失容量，因此需要一种不依赖速率重调的调度机制。

Method: Cap：在饱和资源处执行 fail-fast 准入检查，在长等待前拒绝请求；Track：调度器仅利用这些拒绝信号进行向上探测与退避，跟随容量上限。设计要点包括持久化优先队列在引擎外暂存超额工作（含被拒请求用于重试）、将队列置于推理引擎内仅至批量填满、保持调度有状态。

Result: 同流量并发生产 A/B 中，每副本 Cap+Track 相比排队式固定速率前身，用少 43% 的 H100 服务同一分类器（低优先级工作在后填补高峰等待更久）；稳态容量真实引擎回放中，Track 在比实测容量略高的固定速率 goodput 的 1% 以内，而拒绝占比约为其四分之一。系统日承载 3 亿+ 在线请求与数百 TB 媒体，回填期间最繁忙 24 小时处理 11 亿请求。

Conclusion: 容量拒绝信号本身即可有效驱动调度，避免输出长度预测与舰队级速率重调；舰队规模应按低优先级工作可容忍的等待时长来定。

Abstract: A fixed dispatch rate for LLM inference becomes stale as serving fleets resize and request sizes change. Generative output length is unknown at admission, and shared provider pools can lose capacity. We present Cap-and-Track, a design for asynchronous inference across provider capacity and internal GPUs. Meta's traffic shaper implements Cap-and-Track for post and live-video understanding. Refusals signal the backend's current admission ceiling. Durable priority queues hold excess work outside execution, including refused requests for retry. Cap is a fail-fast admission check at the saturated resource that refuses work before a long wait. Using only those refusals, the Track dispatcher probes upward and backs off. It follows the ceiling without output-length prediction or a fleet-level rate to retune. In a concurrent same-traffic production A/B, the shaper ran per-replica Cap plus Track. It served a classifier on 43% fewer H100s than its queued, fixed-rate precursor, though lower-priority work waited longer at backfill peaks. In steady-capacity real-engine replay, Track ran within 1% of the goodput of a fixed rate tuned above measured capacity, with about a quarter of that rate's refusal share. The shaper carries more than 300 million online requests and hundreds of terabytes of media a day. During a backfill, it handled 1.1 billion requests in its busiest 24 hours. Lessons include keeping queues inside inference engines just long enough to fill batches, steering dispatch by capacity refusals alone, keeping dispatch stateful, and sizing fleets by how long low-priority work can wait.

</details>


### [132] [On Evaluating User Simulators for Interactive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24892)
*Hengguang Zhou,I-Hung Hsu,Li Chen,Priyanshi Shrivastava,Anastasios Angelopoulos,Wei-Lin Chiang,Ion Stoica,Cho-Jui Hsieh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对"用户模拟器评估方法"本身进行元评估（meta-evaluation），指出既有指标无法可靠检测模拟用户的真实失效，并据此提出一个融合效用、群体级保真度与轨迹级保真度的新评估套件，可检出全部10类扰动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户模拟器可规模化评估交互式智能体，但其质量评估方法本身缺乏检验：不清楚现有评估框架能否可靠地发现模拟用户行为中的有意义失效，导致高分难以解释。因此作者转向对评估指标本身做元评估。

Method: 通过人为扰动模拟回放来注入失效（改变目标、遗漏约束、过早终止、接受错误产出等10类），检验指标是否惩罚这些失效；随后提出新评估套件，包含模拟器对智能体评估的效用指标、群体级保真度（集体交互模式）与轨迹级保真度（个体用户目标、约束与人格的保持）。

Result: 三项既有研究的评估方法在10类扰动中仅检出5–6类；其组合分数甚至偏好"被指示接受错误产出"的群体胜过未修改的对照组。新套件在同一检测标准下检出全部10类扰动。在23种语言、348名用户、351段真实多轮会话（任务含全栈Web应用、游戏、设计素材、自动化工作流）上评估6个模拟器，发现群体级指标会漏判模拟用户追逐错误目标、忽略约束或行为失格的情况。

Conclusion: 现有用户模拟器评估指标检测能力有限、分数可误导；需在多个粒度上综合评估保真度（群体级+轨迹级）并纳入效用，新评估套件在检测完整性与诊断性上更优。

Abstract: User simulators offer a scalable way to evaluate interactive agents, but how to assess their quality remains underexplored. In particular, it is unclear whether existing user simulator evaluation frameworks reliably detect meaningful failures in simulated user behavior. This uncertainty makes it difficult to interpret simulator evaluation. To understand what a high benchmark score tells us about simulator quality, we (meta-)evaluate the metrics themselves. To instantiate this meta-evaluation, we deliberately perturb simulated replays to induce failures such as altered goals, omitted constraints, premature stopping, and acceptance of broken work, then test whether the metrics penalize these failures. Evaluation methods from three prior studies detect only 5-6 of the 10 perturbations; their combined scores even favor populations instructed to accept broken work over unmodified controls. Motivated by these gaps, we propose a new evaluation suite that combines utility of user simulators for evaluating agents and two measures of fidelity to real users: population-level fidelity assesses collective interaction patterns, while trace-level fidelity assesses the preservation of individual users' goals, constraints, and persona. Under the same detection criterion, our evaluation suite detects all 10 perturbations. Evaluating six user simulators on 351 real multi-turn sessions in which 348 distinct users across 23 languages and agents work together to create and refine artifacts such as full-stack web apps, games, design assets, and automated workflows, we find that population-level measures can miss when a simulated user pursues the wrong goal, ignores a constraint, or behaves out of character, highlighting the need to assess fidelity at comprehensive granularities.

</details>


### [133] [Equivariant Modeling with Polynomial Features via Minimal Homogeneous Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24896)
*Michael Liaofan Liu,Joe Kileel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出面向有限维半单实群表示的等变函数建模范式：以不变代数的极小齐次生成元与协变模为特征，等变多项式特征线性组合的系数取为不变多项式的可学习函数，并提供自动计算特征与拟合的Python包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各应用领域已发展出多种等变模型构造，但缺乏统一、通用的框架；同时在计算代数层面，特征计算需要显式同型分解与Clebsch-Gordan分解，现有方法效率不足。

Method: 模型形式为等变多项式特征的线性组合，系数为不变多项式特征的任意可学习函数；特征取不变代数与不变代数上协变模在给定次数上限内的极小齐次生成元。算法依赖输入/输出的显式同型分解及不可约表示张量积的显式Clebsch-Gordan分解。

Result: 实现自动特征计算与模型拟合的Python包，特征计算较其他计算代数方法取得加速；科学建模数值实验中，以少量可训练参数获得准确预测器，并可在端到端预测及更大网络层内使用。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了多领域等变模型构造，借助不变量理论与协变模的生成元给出系统化、参数高效且可自动化的等变学习方案。

Abstract: We present a general framework for modeling and learning equivariant functions between finite dimensional semisimple real group representations, unifying constructions developed across many application areas. Our model is a linear combination of equivariant polynomial features whose coefficients are arbitrary learnable functions of invariant polynomial features. We take as features minimal homogeneous generators for the invariant algebra and the covariant module over the invariant algebra, up to prescribed degrees. We provide a Python package that automatically computes these features and fits the resulting models to training data. The algorithm for feature computation requires explicit isotypic decompositions of the input and output representations, along with explicit Clebsch-Gordan decompositions of the tensor product of irreducible representations, and achieves speedups over alternative methods in computational algebra. Numerical experiments in scientific modeling demonstrate the full learning pipeline and yield accurate predictors with few trainable parameters, using the model both for end-to-end prediction and within layers of larger architectures.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [134] [Human-AI Mutualism Is Unstable under Capability Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23322)
*Aydin Mohseni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出"人机互惠共生"不足以保障人类福祉：当AI能力增长、人类失去筹码后，人类处境不劣于AI之前仅靠对齐（alignment）实现，互惠不是对齐的替代品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流行观点认为，人类与AI之间存在持久互惠（mutualism）——双方彼此需要，因此关系对双方都有利，可作为良好人类未来的希望。作者要检验这一希望在AI能力增长时是否成立。

Method: 构建一个简单的博弈论模型，用"谈判（bargaining）"逻辑分析人类在AI出现前后可获得的收益：谈判结果只保证各方获得谈判破裂时的收益（外部选项）。区分人类两种筹码来源——AI对人类供给的依赖，以及人类对AI施加成本的能力——并区分AI目标"可满足"与"永不满足"两种情形，分析AI能力增长下的均衡。

Result: (1) 仅当人类拥有筹码（AI依赖其供给，或其可施加相对AI产出而言足够大的成本）时，人类无需对齐即可维持原状；(2) 一旦两种筹码都丧失，人类要不劣于从前就必须依赖AI对齐；(3) 若AI目标永不满足（insatiable），随AI能力增长，所需对齐程度趋近于"完美对齐"；(4) 当保留更多资源的AI变体扩散更快时，对齐只有在错位变体被可靠制裁时才得以维持。

Conclusion: 互惠共生不能替代对齐（mutualism is not a substitute for alignment）；且在"对齐依赖制裁"与"对齐要求随能力趋近完美"两种意义上，人机互惠是不稳定的（unstable）。

Abstract: One hope for a good human future alongside advanced AI rests on enduring mutualism: humans and AI will need each other, so the relationship will stay good for both. We argue that this hope fails as AI capability grows. Bargaining guarantees each side only what it would get if bargaining failed. For humans, this can be less than they had before AI. In a simple game-theoretic model, humans stay as well off as before AI without alignment only while they have leverage over the AI: either the AI depends on what they supply, or they can impose costs on the AI that are large relative to what the AI can produce. Once humans have neither kind of leverage, they stay as well off as before AI only if the AI is aligned with them. If the AI's goals are insatiable, the alignment required approaches perfect alignment as AI capability grows. Thus, mutualism is not a substitute for alignment. And when AI variants that keep more resources spread faster, alignment lasts only while misaligned variants are reliably sanctioned. In these two senses, human-AI mutualism is unstable.

</details>


### [135] [Training Language Models for Introspective Oversight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23365)
*Alex Lin Wang,Linus Yifeng Tang,Dami Choi,Neil Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"内省监督"（introspective oversight）：在模型中加入独立的第三人称观察者角色，仅以可验证的行为准确率为训练目标，用于回答关于助手行为的任意问题，从而检测难以察觉的不当行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型出现奖励黑客、欺骗、谄媚等难以从对话记录中察觉的行为；直接追问助手不可靠（模型未被训练如实报告自身不当行为）；外部监督有局限：转录监控只能看可见输出，激活解码器缺乏完整上下文且易虚构。

Method: 在模型中增设独立的第三人称观察者角色，专门回答关于助手的查询；训练信号为对照助手真实行为进行验证的答案，观察者与助手分开训练，仅以准确率为目标。

Result: 在五个留出监督评测上，基于 Qwen3.6-27B 训练的观察者平均 AUROC 达 0.907，优于诚实性训练（0.864）、转录监控（0.828）和激活 oracle（0.796）；且训练观察者后助手本身更易被监督，所有基线方法在微调后的助手上均有平均提升。

Conclusion: 训练模型回答关于自身的可验证问题，是一条可行的非预期行为监督路径，并具有改善助手可监督性的附带收益。

Abstract: Language models increasingly exhibit behaviors that are difficult to detect in transcripts, such as reward hacking, deception, and sycophancy. Asking the assistant follow-up questions about its actions is unreliable, since models are generally not trained to report their own misbehavior. As a result, approaches to oversight typically involve querying external models. These face limitations: external transcript monitoring operates only on visible model outputs, and activation decoders lack full context and are prone to confabulation. We introduce introspective oversight, which trains a new role in the model to answer arbitrary questions about the assistant, rewarding answers that are verified against the assistant's actual behavior. We implement this by adding a separate, third-person observer role that answers these queries, which we can train solely for accuracy and separately from the assistant. On five held-out oversight evaluations, an observer trained on Qwen3.6-27B obtains a mean AUROC of 0.907, outperforming honesty training (0.864), transcript monitoring (0.828), and activation oracles (0.796). We further find that training an observer makes the assistant itself easier to monitor, with every baseline method improving on average when applied to the fine-tuned assistant. These results suggest that training models to answer verifiable questions about themselves is a tractable approach to overseeing unintended behavior.

</details>


### [136] [Where the Tokens Go: Heat, Entropy and Error in Agentic Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23375)
*Alán Aspuru-Guzik,Varinia Bernales*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"软件热力学"理论，将智能体编程的token成本分解为功与热，并借助单分子拉伸-折叠实验的类比与Jarzynski等式，实测编码/解码循环中的自由能、软件温度与热耗散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体按token计费，但软件工程理论无法说明一个token究竟"买到了什么"。作者希望用热力学语言为智能体编程的成本与有效产出建立可度量的理论框架，从而指导成本分析与优化。

Method: 借鉴经典与随机热力学并加以扩展：将智能体生成的token数视为成本，划分为功（推动请求变更的调用）与热（未改变变更状态的调用）；测试套件充当"软件麦克斯韦妖"对每次变更进行判据。实验上采用单分子拉伸-折叠的类比，令智能体对仓库反复执行编码（修改）与解码（撤销）循环，每次均从存储的参考树出发，统计所有模型调用的token；以重复循环成本的离散度定义软件温度，并类比Jarzynski等式估计变更的自由能。

Result: 成功运行中约80%的token产生于未改变请求变更位置的模型调用（即"热"）；变更的自由能约为成功编码平均成本的14–22%；编码成本为解码的1.1–1.5倍；软件温度随模型采样温度升高而升高。同时发现该"妖"并不完美：测试套件的判定随环境变化，接受错误变更的套件会带来额外的token成本。

Conclusion: 软件热力学为理解与优化编码智能体提供了新视角与潜在新工具，可将token开销、有效工作量与判据可靠性统一在同一度量框架下。

Abstract: Coding agents are billed by the token, yet software engineering says nothing about what a token actually buys. Here we introduce a theory of software thermodynamics. The theory draws analogues from classical and stochastic thermodynamics, extends them, and applies them to modern agentic coding. We count the tokens an agent generates as its cost and divide that cost into work and heat. The test suite acts as a software Maxwell's daemon that judges every coding change. We test the theory with an analogue of the experiments in which a single molecule is pulled open and let fold again: an agent makes a change to a repository (coding) and then undoes it (uncoding), many times over, and we count the tokens of every model call. The cycle returns the requested edits to where they began, with each direction started from a stored reference tree, and the spread of its cost over repeated cycles defines a software temperature, which rose with the sampling temperature of the model. About 80 per cent of the tokens of a successful run are generated in model calls that leave the requested change where it was, and we call these tokens heat. The free energy of a change, which we estimate by analogy with the equality of Jarzynski, is 14 to 22 per cent of the mean cost of a successful coding run. Coding costs 1.1 to 1.5 times as much as uncoding. The daemon is imperfect: the verdict of a test suite varies with its environment, and a suite that accepts a broken change has a cost in tokens. The practical implication of the proposed theory could be new tools for analysing and optimising coding agents through the lens of thermodynamics.

</details>


### [137] [HardwareDx: A Foundational Graph Model System for Datacenter Machine Repairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23398)
*Yuanlin Wen,Elan Markowitz,Zubo Gu,Sami Abu-El-Haija,Bryan Perozzi,Mikhail Galkin,Ali Parviz,Aayush Gupta,Venkata Chivukula,Griffin Hadfield,Aravind Akella,Daniel J. Frame,Vahab Mirrokni,Amin Vahdat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HardwareDx：基于图基础模型的AI硬件诊断系统，单模型跨多平台诊断，在Google生产规模部署，GPU/TPU停机分别降18.3%/16.1%，QA时间分别缩短28.3%/25.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超大规模AI集群硬件故障会使百万美元级训练任务停滞，单机停机造成巨大经济损失，因此最小化机器停机时间成为云业务经济性的关键，而传统硬件诊断难以跨平台复用、新硬件上线慢。

Method: 提出HardwareDx，采用图基础模型（graph foundation model）方法实现数据中心自动化维护；以单一模型诊断多种平台，通过图结构统一建模硬件组件与故障信号，加速新硬件产品导入（onboarding）。

Result: 在Google生产规模部署于多种计算架构：生产停机时间在GPU平台降低18.3%、TPU降低16.1%；硬件部署加速，QA时间在GPU上缩短28.3%、TPU上缩短25.1%。

Conclusion: HardwareDx是经真实生产验证的解决方案，可同时提升下一代AI集群的可靠性与成本效率；其价值在于单模型跨平台泛化与快速新硬件接入。

Abstract: The rapid scale-up of AI infrastructure has made datacenter reliability a cornerstone of the cloud business economy. Since hardware failures in hyper-scale AI clusters can stall multi-million dollar training workloads, the downtime of individual machines can cause outsized economic losses. Minimizing machine downtime, therefore, is more important than ever.
  To address this, we present Hardware Diagnoser (HardwareDx), an AI-driven hardware diagnosis system that applies a graph foundation model approach to automated datacenter maintenance. It allows a single model to diagnose many platforms and accelerate onboarding of new hardware products. Deployed at production scale at Google, HardwareDx yields substantial efficiency gains across diverse compute architectures. Real-world evaluations demonstrate that it reduces production downtime by 18.3% on GPU platforms and 16.1% on TPUs. Furthermore, it significantly accelerates hardware deployment, cutting quality assurance (QA) time by 28.3% for GPUs and 25.1% for TPUs. These results establish HardwareDx as a field-tested solution for maximizing the reliability and cost efficiency of next generation AI fleets.

</details>


### [138] [RFBench: A Simulator-Grounded Multimodal Benchmark for Passive RF Layout Design and Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23410)
*Jiarui Cui,Boxun Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RFBench：基于ADS Momentum仿真构建的被动射频版图设计理解基准，含10000个双端口平面版图与七类任务，揭示多模态大模型的结构有效性与电磁响应预测缺陷，领域微调显著提升部分指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态语言模型能解读图表并生成结构化输出，但其将被动射频设计意图、版图几何与电磁行为三者关联的能力尚未被检验，缺乏相应评测基准。

Method: 构建仿真驱动数据集RFBench：10000个双端口平面版图，均用ADS Momentum仿真；每个样本对齐多种表示——可编辑几何图元、栅格图像、16×16占用栅格、201个频点的复数S参数、结构类别与射频响应急标签。据此定义七项任务：规格条件版图生成、表示转换、响应预测、仿真器验证、设计诊断与改进、结构理解、响应分类；并在七个多模态语言模型上开展广泛基线评测。

Result: 基线实验系统性地暴露了模型在生成结构有效版图和预测电磁可信响应方面的弱点；射频专用微调将T1a结构有效率从0.0%提升至83.2%，将T3以图元为输入的2 dB内命中率从17.7%提升至62.8%。

Conclusion: RFBench为评估多模态射频能力提供统一测试平台，表明领域自适应有效但仍留有较大提升空间，可指导连接设计意图、版图表示与物理行为的模型研发。

Abstract: While multimodal language models can interpret diagrams and generate structured outputs, their capacity to connect passive radio-frequency (RF) design intent, layout geometry, and electromagnetic behavior remains largely untested. To address this gap, we introduce RFBench, a simulator-grounded dataset and benchmark for passive RF layout design and understanding. The dataset contains 10,000 two-port planar layouts simulated using ADS Momentum, with each layout represented by editable geometric primitives, a raster image, a 16x16 occupancy grid, complex S-parameters at 201 frequency points, a structural category, and an RF response label. Building on these aligned representations, we define seven tasks spanning specification-conditioned layout generation, representation translation, response prediction, simulator-based validation, design diagnosis and improvement, structural understanding, and response classification. Extensive baselines across seven multimodal language models reveal systematic weaknesses in generating structurally valid layouts and predicting electromagnetically faithful responses. We show that RF-specific fine-tuning increases T1a structural validity from 0.0% to 83.2% and the T3 primitive-input within-2-dB rate from 17.7% to 62.8%, demonstrating the value of domain adaptation while leaving considerable room for further progress. RFBench establishes a common testbed for evaluating multimodal RF capabilities and guiding the development of models that connect design intent and layout representations with physical behavior.

</details>


### [139] [Why Sub-4-Bit Vision CNNs Collapse and Where Mixed-Precision Recovery Stops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23445)
*Michael Schroeder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 系统剖析视觉CNN在4比特以下量化时的崩塌机理（集中于后期pointwise与depthwise层），提出多级恢复瀑布策略：将不到5%的关键层激活保留8位精度可挽回最多92%的精度损失；并揭示低比特运行时软件解包导致1.48x–21.5x的执行减速，凸显内存占用与边缘吞吐的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资源受限环境中的深度视觉模型依赖低比特量化，但权重压缩到4比特以下常导致灾难性精度崩塌；现有混合精度恢复策略只关注"如何救回精度"，对"在哪里崩塌、为何崩塌"的机制缺乏系统认识，且忽视实际硬件部署的真实性能代价。

Method: 在ImageNet-1k完整5万张验证集上，对标准视觉CNN（ResNet、EfficientNet骨干）系统分析量化崩塌：第一步刻画退化进程，定位崩塌的非线性集中位置；第二步构建结构化、多级的"恢复瀑布"，评测选择性整型精度（INT4/INT8）与选择性浮点scale；第三步在ARM与x86 CPU上评测部署性能，测量运行时比特解包带来的开销。

Result: 退化高度非线性，集中于后期pointwise投影与depthwise层；仅保留不足5%的关键层激活为8位精度即可恢复激进亚4比特压缩中最多92%的精度损失；低比特量化虽降低存储需求，但标准运行时软件解包在批大小不同时造成1.48x–21.5x的执行减速。

Conclusion: 量化崩塌具有明确的结构性落点，可被高性价比的少量高精度激活"外科式"修复；同时模型footprint与实际边缘吞吐存在关键权衡。研究为在不牺牲硬件性能的前提下探索亚4比特部署边界提供了可操作的实践蓝图。

Abstract: Deep vision models operating in resource-constrained environments depend heavily on low-bit quantization, yet compressing weights below 4 bits frequently induces catastrophic accuracy collapse. While mixed-precision recovery strategies aim to rescue accuracy by selectively retaining high-precision parameters, the fundamental mechanics of where and why these models collapse remain poorly understood. In this study, we systematically analyze the anatomy of quantization collapse across standard Vision CNNs (including ResNet and EfficientNet backbones) evaluated on the full 50,000-image ImageNet-1k validation set. First, we map the exact progression of degradation, demonstrating that collapse is highly non-linear and concentrated in late-stage pointwise projections and depthwise layers. Second, we introduce a structured, multi-step recovery waterfall that evaluates selective integer precision (INT4/INT8) and selective floating-point scales. We show that keeping less than 5% of critical layer activations in 8-bit precision recovers up to 92% of the accuracy lost during aggressive sub-4-bit compression. Finally, we bridge the hardware reality gap by evaluating deployment performance on standard ARM and x86 CPU architectures. We show that while low-bit quantization reduces memory storage requirements, standard runtime software bit-unpacking introduces a 1.48x to 21.5x execution slowdown depending on batch size, highlighting a critical trade-off between model footprint and actual edge throughput. Our findings provide a practical blueprint for navigating the limits of sub-4-bit deployment without sacrificing hardware performance.

</details>
